### [1] [Lattice Aggregation in Distributed Verification under Crash and Byzantine Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13954)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Borzoo Bonakdarpour,Armando Castañeda,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了c-Lattice Aggregation问题，这是一种在崩溃和拜占庭故障下的容错重构问题，并建立了紧密的可行性阈值；同时证明了任何监控系统的可靠性当且仅当解决了c-Lattice Aggregation问题，首次完整刻画了拜占庭故障下的容错验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分布式系统在崩溃和拜占庭故障下的验证问题，目标是从部分、可能重叠的观察中协作重构完整执行，同时加强经典的Lattice Agreement和Byzantine Lattice Agreement问题。

Method: 引入冗余参数x（I的每个元素至少出现在x个初始样本中），基于SCD-broadcast设计匹配算法，并利用不可区分性方法证明下界。

Result: 崩溃故障下（最多t个故障进程），Lattice Aggregation可解当且仅当x≥t+1；拜占庭故障下（t<n/3），c-Lattice Aggregation可解当且仅当x≥2t+c。所有界限紧密。

Conclusion: 定义全局依赖语言（包括共识、线性化、k-set协议和领导者选举），证明任何监控系统的可靠性当且仅当解决了c-Lattice Aggregation问题，首次完整刻画了拜占庭故障下的容错验证。

Abstract: We introduce c-Lattice Aggregation, a fault-tolerant reconstruction problem for distributed verification under crash and Byzantine failures. In our setting, n asynchronous processes supervise a concurrent execution I: each process holds a local sample, and must collaboratively reconstruct I from partial, potentially overlapping observations. A protocol solves c-Lattice Aggregation if at least c correct processes output the complete execution I, while all correct outputs are comparable and bounded by I. This strengthens Lattice Agreement [Attiya, Herlihy and Rachman, 1995] and Byzantine Lattice Agreement [Di Luna et al., 2020; Zheng and Garg, 2020].
  We parameterize inputs by a redundancy parameter x -- every element of I appears in at least x initial samples -- and establish tight feasibility thresholds. Under crash failures with at most t faulty processes, Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= t + 1. Under Byzantine failures with t < n/3, c-Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= 2t + c. All bounds are tight: we present matching algorithms based on SCD-broadcast [Imbs et al., 2018; Khanchandani and Wattenhofer, 2024] and indistinguishability-based lower bounds.
  Finally, we define globally dependent languages -- those for which no partial view can certify correctness, including consensus, linearizability, k-set agreement, and leader election -- and prove that soundness of any monitoring system is achievable if and only if c-Lattice Aggregation is solved, yielding the first complete characterization of fault-tolerant verification under Byzantine failures.

</details>


### [2] [STREAM: Multi-Tier LLM Inference Middleware with Dual-Channel HPC Token Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13968)
*Anas Nassar,Steve Mohr,Leonard Apanasevich,Himanshu Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: STREAM提出三层路由架构，统一本地、HPC中心和云API三种LLM部署方式，通过双通道HPC流式架构将TTFT降低至0.54秒（比批处理模式快21.1倍），实现85.1%的免费层保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM部署环境呈现碎片化特征：本地模型免费且私密但受硬件限制；机构HPC中心提供免费GPU资源但位于防火墙后且为批处理模式；商业云API提供前沿模型但成本高昂且不适合敏感数据。现有系统无法统一这三种资源。

Method: （1）三层路由架构结合本地、HPC和云推理，采用本地LLM复杂度判断器；（2）双通道HPC流式架构，将Globus Compute控制平面与WebSocket中继数据平面分离，采用AES-256-GCM端到端加密；（3）层级感知的上下文摘要机制；（4）HPC-as-API代理模式，提供OpenAI兼容端点。

Result: Llama 3.2 3B在1200查询基准测试（跨10个领域）上实现85.1%免费层保留率。TTFT测量结果：本地0.26秒，HPC（中继）0.54秒，云1.68秒。HPC中继TTFT比批处理模式（11.40秒）快21.1倍。

Conclusion: STREAM成功弥合了本地、HPC和云三种LLM部署方式之间的鸿沟，在保持数据私密性和控制成本的同时，实现了亚秒级响应时间和高免费层利用率，为研究者和从业者提供了统一的LLM访问解决方案。

Abstract: Researchers and practitioners working with large language models face a fragmented landscape: local models are free and private but hardware limits the model size and context windows a researcher can use; institutional HPC centers offer powerful GPU resources at no marginal cost and keep data within institutional boundaries, but operate behind firewalls and are designed for batch jobs rather than interactive use; commercial cloud APIs provide frontier-model quality on demand but impose significant cost and data retention policies unsuitable for sensitive research data. No existing system unifies all three. STREAM (Smart Tiered Routing Engine for AI Models) addresses this gap with four contributions: (1) a three-tier routing architecture combining local, HPC, and cloud inference with a local LLM-based complexity judge; (2) a dual-channel HPC streaming architecture that separates the Globus Compute control plane (authentication and job dispatch) from a WebSocket relay data plane (token delivery), enabling sub-second TTFT (0.54 s median, 21.1x over batch mode's 11.40 s) through institutional firewalls without VPN or firewall rule changes, with end-to-end AES-256-GCM encryption ensuring the relay operator cannot read token payloads; (3) tier-aware context summarization that prevents long conversations from forcing simple queries onto expensive tiers; and (4) an HPC-as-API proxy mode that exposes HPC inference as an OpenAI-compatible endpoint callable from any standard client with no HPC expertise, a deployment pattern made practical only by the sub-second TTFT of contribution (2). Llama 3.2 3B achieves 85.1% free-tier retention on a 1,200-query benchmark spanning ten domains. Measured TTFT: 0.26 s local, 0.54 s HPC (relay), 1.68 s cloud.

</details>


### [3] [VaultxGPU: GPU-Accelerated Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14007)
*Samuel Taiwo Fatunmbi,Om Amit Gandhi,Luke Logan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出VaultxGPU，一种GPU加速的Proof-of-Space绘图器，通过将Blake3哈希流水线卸载到GPU并优化内核设计，相比单线程CPU基线实现59.2倍加速，仅需45.4秒完成K=31绘图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链PoW共识机制能耗巨大，而Proof-of-Space虽然通过存储替代计算降低了成本，但其绘图过程仍受限于CPU哈希吞吐量，亟需更高性能的解决方案。

Method: 开发了VaultxGPU，将Blake3哈希流水线卸载至GPU，分别实现CUDA（NVIDIA）和SYCL（AMD/Intel）版本；采用将Table 1完全保留在GPU显存中，并将排序与匹配阶段融合为单一内核以最小化数据移动的优化策略。

Result: SYCL GPU实现相比单线程CPU基线获得59.2倍加速（K=31绘图从2688秒降至45.4秒），性能优于最优384线程CPU配置；在K值27至31范围内验证了GPU加速的有效性。

Conclusion: GPU加速是实现Proof-of-Space绘图规模化扩展的正确方向，能够突破CPU并行性的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Blockchain consensus mechanisms based on Proof-of-Work consume significant energy, with Bitcoin alone estimated at approximately 150 TWh per year. Proof-of-Space reduces this cost by replacing repeated computation with storage, but plot generation remains bottlenecked by CPU hashing throughput. Prior work on VaultX demonstrated a high-performance CPU-based Proof-of-Space plotter using multi-threaded Blake3 hashing, achieving plotting speeds 4 to 50x faster than Chia depending on hardware configuration. In this paper, we present VaultxGPU, a GPU-accelerated extension of the VaultX plotter that offloads the Blake3 hashing pipeline to the GPU using custom kernels. We implement the plotter in both CUDA for NVIDIA hardware and SYCL for AMD and Intel GPUs, keeping Table 1 entirely in GPU VRAM and fusing the sort and match stages into a single kernel to minimize data movement. We evaluate VaultxGPU across K-values 27 through 31 against CPU baselines. Our SYCL GPU implementation achieves a 59.2x speedup over a single-threaded CPU baseline, completing a K=31 plot in 45.4 seconds compared to 2688 seconds, and outperforms even the best 384-thread CPU configuration. These results confirm that GPU acceleration is the correct direction for scaling Proof-of-Space plotting beyond what CPU parallelism can achieve.

</details>


### [4] [Extreme-Scale Atomistic Simulation of Real-Temperature Magnetic Skyrmion Dynamics by Coupled Spin-Lattice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14073)
*Pin Chen,Cheng-bing Chen,Hai Liu,Yuewen Huang,Kangyou Zhong,Hai-Jun Zhao,Liu-Liu Han,Guixin Guo,Jiang Li,Dan Huang,Ben Xu,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个机器学习框架，实现了7个数量级的加速，在LineShine百亿亿次超级计算机上扩展到1245万CPU核心，模拟了1.34万亿原子和等量自旋，首次在介观尺度直接解析了室温下斯格明子的成核与重组过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际温度下功能材料中的拓扑磁动力学由晶格与自旋的耦合演化控制，但现有的预测性模拟方法无法在器件相关尺度上进行研究。以FeGe中热驱动的螺旋-斯格明子转变为例，该过程需要原子级分辨率、显式晶格运动和微米级域来解析器件尺度的拓扑织构形成，这在传统方法中是无法实现的。

Method: 将自旋约束密度泛函理论训练的神经进化势与保结构自旋-晶格积分器相结合，构建统一的机器学习框架。采用架构特定优化、内核融合、SVE2向量化和NUMA感知数据布局，在LineShink百亿亿次超级计算机上实现大规模并行计算。

Result: 相比之前的自旋感知方法实现了7个数量级的加速；在LineShine超级计算机上扩展到1245万CPU核心，弱扩展效率达89.7%；成功模拟了1.34万亿原子及等量自旋，双精度浮点性能达48.5 PFLOPS；直接解析了先前无法企及尺度的实际温度斯格明子成核与重组过程。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了耦合自旋-晶格拓扑磁动力学预测性模拟的新范式，为器件尺度拓扑磁结构的形成机制研究开辟了新途径，为功能材料中的拓扑磁动力学研究提供了前所未有的模拟能力。

Abstract: Real-temperature topological magnetic dynamics in functional materials is governed by coupled lattice and spin evolution, yet remains inaccessible to predictive simulation at device-relevant scales. As a flagship example, thermally driven helix-to-skyrmion transformation in FeGe requires atomistic resolution, explicit lattice motion, and micrometer-scale domains to resolve device-scale topological texture formation. We combine a spin-constrained density-functional-theory-trained neuro-evolution potential with a structure-preserving spin-lattice integrator within one machine-learned framework. Architecture-specific optimizations, kernel fusion, SVE2 vectorization, and NUMA-aware data layout deliver a seven orders-of-magnitude speedup over prior spin-aware methods. Deployed on LineShine exascale supercomputer, the full application scales to 12.45 million CPU cores with 89.7% weak-scaling efficiency, enabling simulations of 1.34 trillion atoms and an equal number of spins while reaching 48.5 PFLOPS in double precision. The simulations directly resolve real-temperature skyrmion nucleation and reorganization at previously inaccessible scales, establishing a new regime for predictive simulation of coupled spin-lattice topological magnetic dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [On the Limits of Causal Observation in Shared-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14093)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Armando Castañeda,Miguel Piña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在并发操作的边界事件上无法同时实现完备性和可靠性，提出了因果可观测性问题（COP），并通过三种因果监视器实现验证了监测点位置相对于操作边界的放置方式独立于底层时间戳机制，唯一决定了监视器能保证的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定一个并发操作是否在另一个操作开始之前完成是推理并发系统正确性的基本前提。现有方法在观测边界事件（调用和响应）上分配时间戳以反映实时操作顺序时，面临完备性（不遗漏真实优先关系）与可靠性（不报告虚假优先关系）之间的根本冲突，需要对此进行形式化分析并寻找解决方案。

Method: 提出因果可观测性问题（COP）的形式化框架；证明在可观测边界无法实现同时满足完备性和可靠性的强一致性解决方案；分析监测点位置相对于操作边界的放置方式的影响（内部放置保证完备性，外部放置保证可靠性）；设计三种无阻塞因果监视器实现（FAInc集中式原子计数器、Striped分散式计数器、Collect迭代寄存器快照）；在64核NUMA架构上进行实证验证。

Result: 证明强一致性解决方案在可观测边界不可实现；发现监测点位置的放置方式独立于底层时间戳机制，唯一决定监视器能保证的性质（内部放置实现完备性，外部放置实现可靠性，两者均无法同时实现）；三种实现（FAInc、Striped、Collect）尽管内部一致性不同（FAInc和Striped可线性化，Collect仅静止一致），但提供相同的COP保证；Striped在高线程数下解决了FAInc的缓存争用瓶颈，吞吐量与Collect相当。

Conclusion: 可观测边界处强一致性监测不可实现，但通过选择监测点相对于操作边界的放置位置，可以确定性地控制监视器能保证的性质（完备性或可靠性），且该选择独立于底层时间戳机制；Striped实现成功平衡了线性化保证和高线程数下的性能表现。

Abstract: Determining whether one concurrent operation completed before another began is a fundamental prerequisite for reasoning about the correctness of concurrent systems. We formalize this challenge as the Causal Observability Problem (COP): assign timestamps to the observable boundary events of a concurrent execution, invocations and responses, that faithfully reflect real-time operation order. A solution is complete if it never misses a genuine precedence, and sound if it never reports a spurious one. We prove that a strongly consistent solution, one that is simultaneously complete and sound, is unachievable at the observable boundary. We then show that the placement of instrumentation events relative to operation boundaries deterministically governs what a monitor can guarantee: internal placement yields completeness, external placement yields soundness, and neither achieves both. This dichotomy holds independently of the underlying timestamp mechanism. We instantiate this framework with three non-blocking implementations of a Causal Monitor object: FAInc (centralized atomic counter), Striped (decentralized counter), and Collect (iterative register snapshot). FAInc and Striped are linearizable; Collect is only quiescently consistent. Despite this internal consistency gap, we prove that all three provide identical COP guarantees: placement alone determines observable behavior. We validate these claims empirically on a 64-core NUMA architecture, showing that Striped matches Collect in throughput while preserving linearizability, resolving the cache-contention bottleneck of FAInc at high thread counts.

</details>


### [6] [Design Methodology and Performance Trade-offs Management for Distributed and Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14350)
*Milos Gravara,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出从模型中心转向系统中心的复合AI系统设计方法论，通过编排多个模型和工具，在准确率损失2.5-4个百分点的情况下，延迟降低高达60%，成本降低高达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模型中心方法在设计时选择单一模型，对所有输入应用相同计算，无法根据输入难度动态调整，导致性能下降和成本增加。模型作为主要设计变量决定了系统行为，将运营目标与单一设计时选择耦合，存在明显局限性。

Method: 提出复合AI系统设计方法论，沿工作流拓扑和配置选择两个维度组织设计空间，识别出八种设计模式，每种模式整合多种技术来解决单一模型部署的特定局限性。通过三个案例研究验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 复合AI配置在准确率方面与单一模型相差仅2.5-4个百分点，但延迟降低高达60%，成本降低高达71%。模型选择和参数配置共同决定系统性能，但设计空间随工作流复杂度呈组合式增长。

Conclusion: 识别了五个开放性挑战，为从手动配置原型向自动发现和维护SLO合规性的复合分布式AI系统演进提供了路线图。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems must typically satisfy service-level objectives including accuracy, latency, and cost. The prevailing model-centric approaches select a monolithic model at design time and apply identical computation regardless of input difficulty, cannot decompose tasks across specialized components, and have knowledge that is fixed at training time. During runtime, this can lead to performance degradation and increasing costs. Because the model is the main design variable, it determines the majority of system behavior, coupling operational objectives to a single design-time choice. Addressing these limitations requires shifting from model-centric to system-centric design. Compound AI systems realize this shift by orchestrating multiple models, algorithms, and tools as distributed AI systems through explicit control logic. The performance of such systems depends on their workflow topology, the models assigned to each task, and the parameters governing runtime behavior. We present a design methodology that organizes this space along two dimensions, workflow topology and configuration selection, and identifies eight design patterns, each consolidating techniques to address a specific limitation of monolithic deployment. We validate our methodology through three case studies. Across our case studies, Compound AI configurations approach accuracy of monolithic models within 2.5 to 4 percentage points while reducing latency by up to 60% and cost by up to 71%. We show that model selection and parameter configuration jointly determine system performance, but the resulting design space grows combinatorially, as workflows compose more patterns and components. Thus, we identify five open challenges that define a roadmap from manually configured prototypes towards systems that automatically discover and maintain SLO-compliance in Compound and Distributed AI systems.

</details>


### [7] [PLAIground: SLO-Driven Runtime Model Selection for Compound AI Systems in the Edge-Cloud-Space Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14356)
*Milos Gravara,Cynthia Marcelino,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了PLAIground框架，通过运行时模型选择支持复合AI系统，在保持SLO合规的同时实现高达91.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D计算连续体中的复合AI系统需要满足严格的SLO要求，而现有的分布式和复合AI框架不支持运行时模型选择，无法动态切换模型以维持SLO合规性。

Method: 提出了PLAIground框架，引入CAIM抽象通过任务和数据契约解耦任务语义与AI模型实现，并提出Pixie算法进行SLO驱动的运行时模型选择。

Result: 在两个实际复合AI工作流上的评估表明，Pixie实现了高达91.3%的准确率，同时保持SLO合规；相比之下，固定模型策略要么违反成本和延迟预算高达21倍，要么错失4%的准确率目标。

Conclusion: PLAIground框架通过CAIM抽象和Pixie算法有效支持复合AI系统的运行时模型选择，能够在满足SLO约束的同时优化系统性能。

Abstract: Applications in the 3D Computing Continuum, which unifies edge, cloud, and space, require combining multiple AI tasks such as object detection, time-series analytics, and natural language processing into Compound AI systems. These systems must satisfy stringent Service Level Objectives (SLOs) on accuracy, latency, and cost. A key mechanism for maintaining SLO compliance of Compound AI systems is runtime model selection, where AI models are dynamically switched for each workflow task. However, existing distributed and compound AI frameworks do not natively support runtime model selection. We present PLAIground, a framework that enables runtime model selection for Compound AI systems. PLAIground introduces Compoundable AI Model (CAIM) abstraction, which decouples task semantics from AI model implementations via Task and Data Contracts, enabling model switching without workflow changes. Additionally, PLAIground introduces Pixie, an SLO-driven runtime model selection algorithm, which dynamically selects the most suitable model for each task during execution. Our evaluation on two realistic Compound AI workflows demonstrates that Pixie achieves up to 91.3% accuracy while maintaining SLO compliance where fixed-model strategies either violate cost and latency budgets up to 21x or miss accuracy targets by 4%.

</details>


### [8] [Simulation-Based Performance Evaluation of Sharded Blockchain Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14394)
*Om Amit Gandhi,Ioan Raicu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于SimPy的分片区块链离散事件模拟器，用于评估可扩展性。实验显示在256个分片下可实现1.6M TPS（本地环境）和0.6M TPS（广域网），但随着分片数增加存在收益递减效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公有区块链面临可扩展性瓶颈。单纯增大区块大小会增加验证和传输成本，缩短区块间隔会导致传播延迟、分叉和陈旧区块概率上升。这些限制促使研究者转向分片技术，即将交易处理分散到多个并行分片组中执行。

Method: 构建了一个基于SimPy的可配置离散事件模拟器，用于在受控工作负载和网络假设下评估分片区块链架构。模拟器建模了挖矿、验证、分片间协调、区块传播、吞吐量测量、平均区块时间和通信开销等关键组件。

Result: 在256个分片配置下，本地数据中心环境达到1.6M TPS，全球广域网环境达到0.6M TPS，展现了并行执行的显著吞吐量提升。但收益并非无限：超过一定分片数量后，协调流量、同步和网络开销开始主导系统性能，导致收益递减。

Conclusion: 分片技术能够显著提升区块链吞吐量，但扩展性收益存在边界。当分片数量超过阈值时，系统开销（协调、同步、网络通信）会限制性能增长，这为分片区块链系统的设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Public blockchains continue to struggle with scalability because improving throughput is not as simple as increasing block size or reducing block interval. Larger blocks increase validation and transmission cost, while shorter intervals raise the likelihood of propagation delays, forks, and stale blocks. These limits motivate sharding, where transaction processing is divided across multiple parallel shard groups. In this work, we present a configurable SimPy-based discrete-event simulator for evaluating sharded blockchain architectures under controlled workload and network assumptions. The simulator models mining, verification, inter-shard coordination, block dissemination, measured throughput, average block time, and communication overhead. Our simulator achieves 1.6M TPS at 256 shards under a local datacenter-like setup and 0.6M TPS in a global WAN setup, showing strong throughput gains from parallel execution. However, the gains are not unbounded: beyond a certain number of shards, coordination traffic, synchronization, and network overhead begin to dominate, leading to diminishing returns.

</details>


### [9] [Simple-IT: Practical Low-Latency Signature-Free BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14404)
*Qianyu Yu,Juan Villacis,Giuliano Losa,Zhuolun Xiang,Xuechao Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Simple-IT，一种简单、无签名的拜占庭容错（BFT）共识协议，能够匹敌当前最先进但易受量子攻击的BFT协议的性能，理论延迟为4个消息延迟（乐观路径仅需3个），并在实验中验证了其竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数拜占庭容错（BFT）共识协议依赖易受量子计算攻击的公钥签名方案（如Ed25519和BLS12-381），而转向后量子安全的签名方案代价高昂。无签名协议是一个替代方案，但现有研究尚未解决其延迟问题，且现有协议过于复杂难以实现。

Method: 提出Simple-IT，一种基于领导者的无签名BFT共识协议。该协议设计简洁，理论延迟为4个消息延迟（比最优多一个），乐观路径仅需3个。其简洁性使得实际实现和投机性流水线等优化成为可能。

Result: 在地理分布式测试床上的实验表明，Simple-IT在吞吐量和延迟方面均能达到与当前最先进（但易受量子攻击）协议相竞争的性能水平。

Conclusion: 无签名的BFT共识协议可以在不牺牲性能（吞吐量和延迟）的情况下实现后量子安全，Simple-IT证明了简洁的设计是实现和优化的关键，为后量子时代的BFT协议提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing pose a looming threat to most current Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, which rely on quantum-vulnerable public-key signature schemes such as Ed25519 and BLS12-381. Instead of switching to much more expensive post-quantum secure signature schemes, an alternative is to use signature-free protocols, which rely only on cheap, post-quantum secure authenticated channels.
  In this paper, we ask whether signature-free BFT consensus protocols can match the performance of current state-of-the-art, quantum-vulnerable BFT consensus protocols. While previous work on the Sailfish++ protocol showed that state-of-the-art throughput is attainable signature-free, the question of latency is still open. Several recent signature-free protocols have low latency in theory, but they are all very intricate, and no practical implementation has so far been presented. In this work, we propose Simple-IT, a new leader-based, signature-free BFT consensus protocol that achieves a theoretical latency of 4 message delays (one more than the optimum), and only 3 on its optimistic path. Crucially, Simple-IT is simple enough to be amenable to implementation and to practical optimizations such as speculative pipelining, and, as we show experimentally in a geo-distributed testbed, it achieves both throughput and latency competitive with state-of-the-art quantum-vulnerable protocols.

</details>


### [10] [parRSB: Exascale Spectral Element Mesh Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14659)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Paul Fischer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了parRSB，一种针对谱元网格的高质量、高可扩展并行图划分器，基于递归谱二分法（RSB）实现，通过计算Fiedler向量来最小化分区间的通信量，并在Summit和Frontier超级计算机上验证了其优异的划分质量和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱元方法的网格划分质量直接影响并行计算中分区间的通信开销，传统方法难以兼顾划分质量与大规模并行可扩展性。为在超大规模计算环境下生成高质量网格划分并最小化通信体积，亟需一种高效、可扩展的并行划分算法。

Method: 在输入网格的对偶图上实现递归谱二分法（RSB）算法，利用拉普拉斯矩阵最小非零特征值对应的Fiedler向量指导划分决策。采用Lanczos方法和基于共轭梯度（CG）的逆迭代法两种数值方法计算Fiedler向量，并引入优化策略加速划分过程。

Result: 在Summit和Frontier超级计算机上测试，parRSB生成的划分质量高、通信体积小，并展现出优异的大规模并行可扩展性，同时优化措施显著加速了划分过程。

Conclusion: parRSB验证了基于RSB与Fiedler向量的并行图划分方法在谱元网格上的有效性与可扩展性，为大规模科学计算提供了高效的网格划分工具。

Abstract: We introduce parRSB - a parallel, highly scalable graph partitioner for spectral element meshes that produce high quality partitions. parRSB is based on Recursive Spectral Bisection (RSB) algorithm implemented on the dual graph of the input mesh. RSB uses the Fiedler vector, which is the eigenvector associated with the smallest non-zero eigenvalue of the Laplacian matrix of the dual graph for making partitioning decisions and tries to minimize the communication volume between the partitions. We implemented two numerical methods: Lanczos, and Inverse iteration using Conjugate Gradient method to compute the Fiedler vector. We present partitioning results using parRSB on Summit and Frontier supercomputers at Oak Ridge National Laboratory to illustrate the quality of the partitions produced by parRSB and the scalability of our implementation. We also present results for some of the optimizations we did to speed up the partitioning process.

</details>

### [11] [Large Language Models as Supervised Extraction Assistants: Lowering the Barrier to Documentation Standard Adoption in Agent-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13749)
*Peer-Olaf Siebers,Christopher Frantz*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用大语言模型(LLMs)来自动化基于主体建模(ABM)文档生成过程，发现LLMs在描述性任务上表现可靠，但在解释性和评估性任务上仍需人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ABM领域存在文档标准（如ODD、TRACE、RAT-RS等），但由于编写文档需要大量精力且常被视为补充性工作，这些标准的采用率仍然有限。研究旨在探索利用LLMs来促进和部分自动化文档生成过程，以提高文档编写的效率和质量。

Method: 以使用率较低的RAT-RS（严谨性与透明性报告标准）为研究对象，使用四种不同的LLMs从已发表的ABM论文中提取报告，评估LLMs在不同问题类型上的一致性和性能表现。

Result: LLMs能够生成连贯的输出内容，在描述性任务上表现较为可靠，但在解释性和评估性任务上表现不够稳定。研究识别了LLM辅助文档生成可靠性的实际启发式规则，明确了何时需要人工监督。

Conclusion: LLMs虽然可以提高ABM文档报告的质量和一致性，但也存在明显局限性。研究呼吁ABM社区进行系统性的探索，以增强文档报告的严谨性并促进标准采用。

Abstract: Agent-Based Modelling (ABM) relies on clear documentation to ensure credibility and transparency. Although standards exist for documenting models (e.g. ODD), processes (e.g. TRACE, EABSS), and data use (e.g. RAT-RS), their adoption remains limited due to the effort required to produce documentation that is often treated as supplementary. This paper explores the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) to facilitate and partially automate such processes. We conduct a feasibility study focusing on the underused Rigour and Transparency Reporting Standard (RAT-RS), using four LLMs to extract reports from a published ABM paper. We assess consistency and performance across question types, finding that LLMs generate coherent outputs and perform more reliably on descriptive than on explanatory or evaluative tasks. While LLMs can improve reporting quality and consistency, they also exhibit notable limitations. We identify practical heuristics for when LLM-assisted documentation is reliable and when human oversight is needed and call for systematic community-level exploration to enhance rigour and adoption in ABM reporting.

</details>


### [12] [Safety-Contract Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Network Security Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13832)
*Jose Luis Lima de Jesus Silva*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了安全契约图MARL框架（ACD³-GAT），解决了仅奖励驱动的多智能体强化学习在自主网络安全响应系统中无法满足实际运营约束的问题，显著降低了违规率和停机成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仅奖励驱动的多智能体强化学习（MARL）方法虽然能提升安全奖励，但在实际部署中缺乏运营纪律，无法满足安全运营中心（SOC）的预算约束（如平均恢复时间MTTR、误报响应、防火墙变更管理等），导致100%的评估周期都违反停机预算。

Method: 提出安全契约图MARL框架，实例化为ACD³-GAT架构，包含以下关键组件：1）将仿真器观测与可重用运营预算分离；2）约束优化（拉格朗日运营成本控制）；3）图状态编码（图注意力网络）；4）反事实动作筛选（包括预算感知筛选、CVaR尾部风险估计、对手信念状态和图反事实风险传播G-CRP）。通过对比IPPO、MAPPO-GAT、C-MAPPO-GAT和ACD³-GAT四种方法进行评估。

Result: 在CAGE Challenge 4基准测试中：1）所有无约束方法在100%的评估周期中违反SOC停机预算（预算上限50，实际成本311-430）；2）C-MAPPO-GAT将违规率从100%降至0.3%，平均停机成本从355.4降至15.5；3）ACD³-GAT将平均停机成本降至48.2，违规率为13.8%，位于安全契约前沿而非最保守合规点；4）拓扑种子和耦合自适应红队压力测试显示安全约束策略比仅奖励MAPPO-GAT具有更低的最坏自适应退化。

Conclusion: 安全约束对于使MARL方法达到可部署性至关重要，仅奖励学习缺乏运营纪律。所提出的安全契约框架通过分离运营预算和约束优化，成功将MARL从纯性能优化转向实际可部署的安全响应系统，同时保持了对抗压力下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous network-security response systems promise to reduce Security Operations Centre (SOC) reaction latency, but reward-only multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) can improve security reward while remaining non-deployable. We present a safety-contract graph MARL framework and instantiate it as ACD$^3$-GAT (Adaptive Constrained Counterfactual Decisioning with a Graph Attention Network encoder), an architecture that separates simulator observations from reusable operational budgets, constrained optimization, graph state encoding, and counterfactual action screening. We evaluate the method in CAGE Challenge 4, where agents operate under budgets for Mean Time to Recover (MTTR), false-positive response, and firewall change-management disruption. Across the benchmark, every unconstrained method violates the SOC downtime budget in 100% of evaluated episodes, with mean downtime proxy costs of 311-430 against a budget of 50. This complements prior CAGE Challenge 4 findings by showing that reward-only learning lacks operational discipline. Constrained MAPPO-GAT (C-MAPPO-GAT) isolates Lagrangian operational-cost control and budget-aware screening, while ACD$^3$-GAT adds budget context, CVaR tail-risk estimation, opponent-belief state, and Graph Counterfactual Risk Propagation (G-CRP). The replicated comparison includes three 200-episode seeds for IPPO, MAPPO-GAT, C-MAPPO-GAT, and ACD$^3$-GAT. C-MAPPO-GAT reduces downtime violation from 100% to 0.3% and mean downtime cost from 355.4 to 15.5 relative to MAPPO-GAT. ACD$^3$-GAT reduces mean downtime cost to 48.2 with a 13.8% violation rate, placing it on the safety-contract frontier rather than at the most conservative compliance point. Topology-seed and coupled adaptive Red-process stress tests preserve this contrast and show lower worst adaptive degradation for safety-constrained policies than reward-only MAPPO-GAT.

</details>


### [13] [Naive Visual Memory is Not Enough: A Failure-Mode Study of GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14106)
*Seoyoung Choi,Minseok Ko,Hyunseok Lee,Kunwoong Kim,Woomin Song,Chanseok Jeon,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AGMem框架，通过存储与动作相关的局部GUI截图替代全屏截图，在OSWorld上相比全图记忆将任务成功率提升33.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体的视觉记忆方法效果不明确，不清楚视觉记忆缓解或加剧了哪些失败类型。需要系统分析视觉记忆对GUI智能体失败模式的影响，以设计更有效的记忆表示方法。

Method: 1) 提出GUI智能体四种失败类型的分类体系（认知失败、视觉状态误解、隐藏操作盲区、接地错误），映射到感知-推理-行动流程的不同阶段；2) 分析全图记忆对失败分布的影响发现其减少状态级失败但恶化动作级失败；3) 提出动作接地视觉记忆（AGMem）框架，存储与成功动作或恢复密切相关的局部GUI图像裁剪区域，而非全屏截图。

Result: 全图记忆对失败分布产生分化影响：减少状态级失败但恶化动作级失败，增加隐藏操作盲区和接地错误。AGMem在OSWorld基准测试上相比全图记忆提升任务成功率33.3%，证明其作为视觉记忆表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉记忆的表示形式对GUI智能体性能有关键影响，基于动作接地的局部图像记忆比全屏截图更有效。AGMem为GUI智能体的视觉记忆设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to automate complex computer tasks across applications, websites, and operating systems. To improve their reliability, recent work has introduced experiential memory, where agents retrieve prior trajectories to guide decision-making in similar states. More recent approaches further extend this idea to visual memory by storing and retrieving screenshots from past interactions, providing agents with richer contextual information than text-only memories. However, the effect of visual memory in GUI agents remains insufficiently understood: it is unclear which failures visual memory mitigates, or which failures it exacerbates. To systematically analyze the effect of visual memory, we introduce a taxonomy of four GUI agent failures (i.e., cognitive failure, visual state misunderstanding, hidden operation blindness, and grounding error) that map to distinct stages of the perception-reasoning-action pipeline. We find that prepending full-image memory has a divergent effect on the failure distribution: it reduces state-level failures but worsens action-level ones, and increases hidden operation blindness and grounding error. Motivated by this finding, we propose Action-Grounded Visual Memory (AGMem), an action-grounded memory framework for GUI agents. The core idea of AGMem is to store image crops that capture the local GUI region closely related to a successful action or a recovery, rather than storing full screenshots. Experiments on OSWorld show that AGMem improves task success rates by 33.3 % over full-image memory. These results demonstrate that AGMem is an effective representation for visual memory in GUI agents.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Coordinated Preference for Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14693)
*Pengxin Wang,Lihao Guo,Yi Xie,Bo Liu,Siyang Cao,Jingdi Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了PCMA方法，通过学习协调的智能体特定偏好来解决协作MOMARL中跨目标和跨智能体的冲突问题，在理论和实验上均证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作多目标多智能体强化学习(MOMARL)中存在两类冲突：多个潜在冲突目标之间的冲突，以及具有不同观察、角色和贡献的智能体之间的冲突。现有方法难以有效处理这些交叉冲突，需要一种能够实现智能体间互补权衡的协调机制。

Method: 提出偏好协调多智能体策略优化(PCMA)方法，该方法学习协调的智能体特定偏好，使智能体能够进行互补的权衡。理论上将协作MOMARL形式化为团队最优博弈，证明了在适当条件下，偏好多样性可以通过一阶改进分解诱导团队改进。

Result: 在多个协作MOMA环境和实际交通控制场景上的实验表明，PCMA在性能和权衡协调方面均有改善，能够有效处理多目标和多智能体之间的冲突问题。

Conclusion: PCMA成功解决了协作MOMARL中的跨目标和跨智能体冲突问题，通过偏好协调实现了互补权衡，为团队决策提供了有效的解决方案，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-objective multi-agent reinforcement learning (MOMARL) models team decision making under multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. In this setting, conflicts arise not only across objectives but also across agents with different observations, roles, and contributions. We propose Preference Coordinated Multi-agent Policy Optimization (PCMA), which learns coordinated agent-specific preferences to enable complementary trade-offs among agents. Theoretically, we formulate cooperative MOMARL as a team-optimal game and show that, under suitable conditions, preference diversity can induce team improvement through a first-order improvement decomposition. Experiments on multiple cooperative MOMA environments and a practical traffic-control scenario show that PCMA improves both performance and trade-off coordination.

</details>

### [15] [HierSVA: A Data Synthesis Pipeline, Dataset, and Benchmark for LLM-Driven Hierarchical Hardware Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13706)
*Maohua Nie,Jiang Zhu,Jingqun Zhang,Zhichen Zeng,Jiayi Wang,Sibo Zhang,Jialin Wang,C. -J. Richard Shi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HierSVA，一个集成流程、数据集和基准测试的综合套件，用于LLM驱动的层次化硬件形式验证，通过实验揭示了当前LLM在生成SystemVerilog断言方面的能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件设计复杂度提升，层次化RTL的形式验证面临挑战。现有方法缺乏针对层次化硬件的LLM断言生成评估体系，需要构建统一的工具链、数据集和评估基准来系统性研究和推进LLM在硬件形式验证中的应用。

Method: 提出HierSVA套件，包含三个核心组件：(1) HierSVA-SP流程，结合RTL预处理工具链与LLM-in-the-loop形式验证流程，生成层次化RTL的SystemVerilog断言；(2) HierSVA-DS数据集，基于BaseJump STL构建342个模块，包含层次元数据（深度0-9）和28个模块-缺陷对的深层子集；(3) HierSVA-B基准，从语法正确性、证明成功率、空洞性、规范忠实度、变异覆盖率和形式化核心覆盖率六个维度评估断言质量。

Result: 对12个最新LLM的评估发现：(1) 模块级编译率67.1%，可评估断言中82.1%非空洞证明，但断言集仅检测70.2%的注入故障，覆盖36.2%的形式化核心；(2) 在深层子集上，断言集检测缺陷RTL的召回率为0.87，但在正确RTL上40%的预测为误报，精度仅0.60；(3) 代理模式改善可证明性和强度指标，但增益趋于平台期并出现波动。

Conclusion: HierSVA为LLM驱动的层次化硬件形式验证提供了完整的工具链和评估框架。实验表明当前LLM在断言生成方面虽有较高非空洞证明率，但在故障检测覆盖率和精度方面仍存在明显不足，代理模式的效果提升有限，指出了未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: We present HierSVA, an integrated suite that combines a pipeline, dataset, and benchmark for LLM-driven hierarchical hardware formal verification. HierSVA-SP pairs an RTL preprocessing toolchain with an LLM-in-the-loop formal verification flow to produce reference SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA) on hierarchical RTL. Applying it to BaseJump STL yields HierSVA-DS, a dataset of 342 modules, with hierarchy metadata and depths 0--9, accompanied by a deep subset of 28 module-bug pairs with natural-language specifications and bug variants. HierSVA-B decomposes assertion quality into six metric axes: syntax correctness, assertion proof success rate, vacuity, specification faithfulness, mutation coverage, and formal core coverage. Applying HierSVA-B to twelve recent LLMs reveals three findings. First, the module-level compile rate is 67.1\%; among generated assertions in evaluable runs, 82.1\% prove non-vacuously, but the corresponding assertion sets detect only 70.2\% of eligible injected faults and cover 36.2\% of the formal core. Second, on 211 evaluable model--module entries in the deep subset, assertion sets flag buggy RTL with 0.87 recall, but 40\% of predicted-buggy outcomes are false positives on correct RTL, limiting precision to 0.60. Third, agentic mode improves S1-style provability and strength metrics, but gains plateau and oscillate. Codes and artifacts are available at \href{https://github.com/HierSVAAnon/HierSVACodeAndArtifacts}{https://github.com/HierSVAAnon/HierSVACodeAndArtifacts}. Dataset is available at \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/AnonymousHierSVA/HierSVA}{https://huggingface.co/datasets/AnonymousHierSVA/HierSVA}.

</details>


### [16] [Tiara: A Programmable Line-Rate ISA for Remote Memory Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13708)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Tiara，一种在内存侧NIC上执行的紧凑、可静态验证的指令集，通过预注册程序本地解决间接访问问题，将多RTT依赖链压缩为单次往返，显著提升了图遍历、页表遍历、分布式锁和分离式LLM推理的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMA单侧动词是内存分解的自然原语，但在目标地址依赖于必须先从远程内存读取的数据时，其1-RTT性能会崩溃，这种现象称为"间接墙"。间接访问在图遍历、地址转换、分布式协调和分离式LLM推理中普遍存在，每级间接访问都需要一次顺序依赖的网络往返，而现有的RDMA NIC卸载方案要么消耗远程CPU周期，要么吞吐量有限。

Method: 提出Tiara，一种紧凑、可静态验证的指令集，在内存侧NIC上执行。Tiara操作符是预注册的程序（类似于内核中的eBPF程序），能够本地解决间接访问问题，将多RTT依赖链压缩为单次往返。基于FPGA原型实现该系统。

Result: 在基于FPGA的原型上，Tiara相比单侧RDMA将10跳图遍历延迟降低2.85倍，吞吐量提高3.4倍；页表遍历延迟减少62%；无竞争分布式锁延迟降低2.9倍；在8KB块大小的分离式PagedAttention中实现2.8倍吞吐量提升；在32个专家的MoE专家收集场景中实现1.88倍延迟改善。

Conclusion: Tiara通过在内存侧NIC上执行可静态验证的程序，有效解决了间接访问导致的性能瓶颈问题，在多种实际应用场景中均取得了显著的性能提升，为内存分解系统提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: RDMA one-sided verbs are the natural primitive for memory disaggregation, but they require the client to supply the exact remote address. The 1-RTT performance breaks down when the target address depends on data that must first be read from remote memory, a pattern we call the Indirection Wall. Indirection is pervasive: graph traversals follow pointers hop by hop, address translation walks multi-level page tables, distributed coordination requires conditional multi-host logic, and disaggregated LLM inference must resolve paged KV caches through block-table lookups. Each level of indirection costs one sequentially dependent network round-trip, yet offloading to existing RDMA NICs either consumes remote CPU cycles or has limited throughput. We present Tiara, a compact, statically verifiable instruction set that executes on the memory-side NIC. Tiara operators are pre-registered programs, analogous to eBPF programs in the kernel, that resolve indirection locally, collapsing multi-RTT dependent chains into a single round-trip. On an FPGA-based prototype, Tiara reduces 10-hop graph-traversal latency by 2.85x over one-sided RDMA while sustaining 3.4x higher throughput, cuts page-table walk latency by 62%, reduces uncontended distributed-lock latency by 2.9x, achieves 2.8x throughput for disaggregated PagedAttention at 8 KB blocks, and 1.88x MoE expert-gather latency at 32 experts.

</details>


### [17] [A Modern Large-Scale Memory Characterization Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13725)
*Ataberk Olgun,Haocong Luo,Ismail Emir Yuksel,F. Nisa Bostanci,A. Giray Yaglikci,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DRAM Bender基础设施的更新版本，这是一个用于大规模DRAM特性分析的现代化实验平台，新增了实验类型支持、扩展了DRAM接口标准兼容性，并提升了大规模使用的便利性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过对真实内存芯片的特性分析，可以深入了解现代内存的基本操作特性，从而开发出能够提升内存性能、鲁棒性、安全性和能效的新机制，以解决困扰现代计算系统的巨大内存瓶颈问题。

Method: 构建了一个大规模DRAM特性分析实验室，其核心组件是DRAM Bender——一个多功能且易于使用的现代DRAM特性分析基础设施。对该基础设施进行了三方面更新：1)引入对新类型特性分析实验的支持；2)扩展DRAM接口标准支持范围；3)提升大规模部署使用的便捷性。

Result: 成功开发并升级了DRAM Bender基础设施，首次公开介绍了这些更新功能，为研究社区提供了一个能够发现新问题并解决关键内存扩展问题的实验平台。

Conclusion: 该基础设施有望帮助研究社区发现内存系统中的新问题并解决关键的内存扩展问题，从而克服现代计算系统中严重的内存瓶颈，推动内存性能、鲁棒性、安全性和能效的提升。

Abstract: Real memory chip characterization yields insights into fundamental operational characteristics of modern memory, enabling new mechanisms that improve memory performance, robustness, security, and energy efficiency. We describe our large-scale DRAM characterization laboratory for understanding DRAM. A key building block of this laboratory is DRAM Bender, a versatile and easy-to-use modern DRAM characterization infrastructure. We have updated DRAM Bender to i) introduce support for new types of characterization experiments, ii) expand on its DRAM interface standard support, and iii) make it easier to use at large scale. This paper introduces these updates for the first time. We hope our infrastructure enables the community to discover new problems and solve critical memory scaling issues, enabling the overcoming of the huge memory bottleneck that plagues modern computing systems.

</details>


### [18] [VHDLSuite: Unified Pipeline for LLM VHDL Generation with Data Synthesis and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13735)
*Yijun Shen,Minghao Shao,Yichen Zhao,Zhuoyan Yu,Boyuan Chen,Yik-Cheung Tam,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VHDLSuite，一个用于可扩展VHDL生成评估的基准测试框架，包含自动化基准合成、可执行验证和多模型诊断分析，并发布了包含200多个VHDL问题的VHDLBench基准，揭示了LLM辅助VHDL生成的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在RTL代码生成方面的评估主要集中在Verilog，而对VHDL的评估仍然有限。由于VHDL具有更严格的语义规则等独特语言特性，其评估考虑因素与Verilog不同。这种覆盖不足限制了对当前模型在不同结构和语义的硬件设计语言中泛化能力的全面理解。

Method: 提出了VHDLSuite框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）数据流水线，自动将Verilog设计及其测试平台转换为可执行的VHDL基准实例，并使用VUnit/GHDL进行验证以确保每个任务在VHDL环境中可编译、可运行和可一致性检查；（2）VHDLBench基准，包含200多个VHDL问题及完整验证的测试平台，覆盖不同复杂度级别；（3）对前沿LLM模型进行广泛评估并进行多模型诊断分析。

Result: 成功构建了包含200多个VHDL问题的基准测试集，每个问题都经过完整验证；通过广泛评估最先进的LLM模型，发现了LLM辅助VHDL生成特有的关键挑战，为多语言硬件设计自动化的未来研究提供了重要见解。所有数据流水线、基准和评估框架将开源发布。

Conclusion: VHDLSuite填补了VHDL生成评估领域的空白，为理解LLM在不同硬件描述语言中的泛化能力提供了重要工具。研究发现为未来多语言硬件设计自动化研究提供了有价值的见解和支持，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLM) have shown impressive capabilities in Register Transfer Level (RTL) code generation, particularly for Verilog. However, evaluating their performance with other Hardware Description Languages (HDL), especially VHDL, remains limited although its distinct language characteristics, such as stricter semantic rules, introduce evaluation considerations that differ from Verilog. This lack of coverage restricts fully understanding of how well current models generalize across hardware design languages with differing structures and semantics. To address this gap, we introduce VHDLSuite, a benchmark-centered infrastructure for scalable VHDL generation evaluation, integrating automated benchmark synthesis, executable validation, and multi-model diagnostic analysis. First, we propose a data pipeline that automatically converts Verilog designs and their accompanying testbenches into executable VHDL benchmark instances, followed by VUnit/GHDL-based validation to ensure each released task is compilable, runnable, and consistently checkable in the VHDL environment. Second, we introduce VHDLBench, a benchmark with over 200 VHDL problems with complete and validated testbenches across a wide range of complexity levels. Third, we extensively evaluate cutting-edge LLMs and uncover key challenges specific on LLM-aided VHDL generation. Our findings provide important insights and support future work in multi-language hardware design automation.Our data pipeline, benchmark, and evaluation framework will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [19] [BigPower: Hierarchical Source-Level Module Power Estimation for CPUs with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13747)
*Honghua Zhu,Chunjie Luo,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了BigPower，一种分层源代码级代理模型，利用大语言模型表示和架构信息直接从源代码设计信息估计CPU模块级功耗，无需仿真即可实现高精度细粒度功耗估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的功耗估算对理解和优化CPU功耗行为至关重要，但现有实用工作流严重依赖仿真派生信息或后硅分析，效率低下且成本高昂。

Method: 构建分层源代码级代理模型BigPower，结合大语言模型表示、架构层次、模块连接性、配置参数和工作负载上下文，直接从源代码级设计信息估计模块级功耗，推理过程无需额外仿真。

Result: 在开源XiangShan处理器家族上的实验表明，该方法能在不同配置和工作负载下实现实用的细粒度功耗估算，准确度高。

Conclusion: BigPower为传统基于仿真的功耗估算工作流提供了一种高效的替代方案，能够显著提升CPU设计阶段功耗分析的效率。

Abstract: Accurate power estimation is important for understanding and optimizing CPU power behavior, yet practical workflows often rely on simulation-derived information or post-silicon analysis. In this work, we present BigPower, a hierarchical source-level surrogate model for fine-grained module-level power estimation during CPU design. BigPower leverages large language model-based representations together with architectural hierarchy, module connectivity, configuration parameters, and workload context to estimate module-level power consumption directly from source-level design information, without requiring additional simulation during inference. Experimental results in the open-source XiangShan processor family demonstrate practical fine-grained power estimation across diverse configurations and workloads, offering an efficient alternative to conventional simulation-based workflows.

</details>


### [20] [Ramulator 2.1: A Composable Memory System Simulator for Modern DRAM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13844)
*Haocong Luo,F. Nisa Bostancı,Ataberk Olgun,Maria Makeenkova,Ziad Malik,Ipek Akdeniz,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Ramulator 2.1是Ramulator 2.0的重大升级，通过支持现代DRAM标准、引入Python接口提升可用性、完善测试验证流程三方面改进，为DRAM和内存控制器建模提供高性能、易扩展的开源模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着现代DRAM技术（如HBM3/4、LPDDR5/6、GDDR7）和新内存控制器特性的快速发展，现有模拟器在功能支持、易用性和可信赖性方面存在不足。需要构建一个能够支持新兴标准、便于用户扩展定制、且模拟结果可靠的DRAM模拟器。

Method: Ramulator 2.1从三个方向进行改进：1）扩展支持HBM3/4、LPDDR5/6、GDDR7等现代DRAM标准和内存控制器新特性；2）引入基于Python的建模与配置接口，通过双向代码生成框架将底层C++代码封装为高层Python DRAM规范，自动创建Python代理以支持快速变体开发和自动化设计空间探索；3）构建全面的测试验证基础设施，包括细粒度DRAM时序约束和调度行为验证，以及基于延迟-吞吐量曲线的系统级性能评估；此外还集成高性能DRAM命令轨迹可视化工具。

Result: Ramulator 2.1成功实现了三大改进：支持了最新的DRAM标准和内存控制器特性；通过Python接口显著提升了易用性和扩展性，使用户能快速创建DRAM变体并自动化设计空间探索；通过完善的测试验证流程增强了模拟结果的可信度，并提供了便捷的调试分析工具。

Conclusion: Ramulator 2.1作为一个开源且活跃开发的模拟器，通过系统性改进在功能覆盖、易用性和可信赖性三方面取得显著进展，为学术界和工业界的DRAM及内存控制器研究提供了强有力的工具支持。

Abstract: Ramulator 2.1 is a major overhaul of Ramulator 2.0 that substantially improves the simulator in three directions: 1) support of modern and emerging DRAM and memory-controller features, 2) better usability and extensibility of the simulator, and 3) more comprehensive tests and validation workflows. Ramulator 2.1 adds support for advanced features in recent and emerging DRAM standards and memory controllers, including HBM3/4, LPDDR5/6, and GDDR7. To improve usability and extensibility, Ramulator 2.1 introduces a Python-based modeling and configuration interface backed by a two-way code-generation framework that 1) hides low-level C++ code behind high-level DRAM specifications written in Python, and 2) automatically creates Python proxies for all components of the simulator. Doing so enables users to rapidly create variants of DRAM standards and automate design-space-exploration workflows. To improve trustworthiness in simulation results, Ramulator 2.1 provides a comprehensive testing and validation infrastructure that covers both 1) fine-grained validation of specific DRAM timing constraints and memory-controller scheduling behavior, and 2) system-level performance evaluation using latency-throughput curves. To aid performance analysis and debugging, Ramulator 2.1 also includes an easy-to-use and high-performance DRAM command trace visualizer. Ramulator 2.1 is open-source on GitHub and under active development.

</details>


### [21] [Extended Abstract: Re-Evaluating the Real-System Modeling Accuracy of Ramulator 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14566)
*F. Nisa Bostanci,Haocong Luo,Ataberk Olgun,Maria Makeenkova,Geraldo F. Oliveira,A. Giray Yaglikci,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文反驳了MICRO 2024一篇论文对Ramulator 2.0模拟器的负面评价，证明其结论错误源于配置和使用失误，并提出四项最佳实践以避免类似问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MICRO 2024最佳论文亚军"A Mess of Memory System Benchmarking, Simulation and Application Profiling"对Ramulator 2.0提出了强烈的负面评价，并展示了出人意料的结果。由于该论文获得了包括"可复现"在内的三个artifact徽章，这些错误结论可能会误导研究领域，损害Ramulator 2.0的声誉，因此有必要澄清事实真相。

Method: 作者对Mess论文中的实验配置和结果进行了系统性审查和复现，识别出Mess论文中存在的配置错误和模拟器使用不当问题；基于调查结果提出了四项最佳实践建议；强调在发布意外结果前应与模拟器作者和开发者进行沟通；同时开源了所有代码和脚本以支持结果复现。

Result: 证明了Mess论文中关于Ramulator 2.0的结果和声明是不正确的，这些错误源于该论文中的配置错误和模拟器使用方式不当；提出了四项最佳实践指导方针以帮助模拟器用户和开发者避免类似问题；开源了所有相关代码和脚本。

Conclusion: 研究强调在观察到意外结果时（特别是在发布这些结果之前），与模拟器作者和开发者进行沟通的重要性，以确保模拟器以正确的配置和预期方式使用；该研究还旨在激发关于artifact评估实践以及发布后纠正结果和artifact机制的讨论。

Abstract: Cycle-level DRAM simulators provide accurate and flexible models for DRAM and memory controller operations and enable research on current and future memory systems. Therefore, they are critical for improving the performance, efficiency, and robustness of DRAM-based memory systems. Ramulator 2.0 (successor of Ramulator) is a highly modular and extensible cycle-accurate DRAM simulator that enables rapid exploration of new ideas in DRAM-based memory systems.
  A MICRO 2024 best paper runner-up publication, A Mess of Memory System Benchmarking, Simulation and Application Profiling, which we refer to as "the Mess paper," with all three artifact badges awarded (including "Reproducible"), proposes a new benchmark to evaluate real and simulated memory system performance. While doing so, it makes strong negative claims about Ramulator 2.0 and shows unexpected results.
  In this talk and the associated extended abstract, we demonstrate that these results and claims in the Mess paper are incorrect and are due to configuration and simulator usage errors made in the Mess paper. We describe four best practices to aid users and developers of simulation tools to avoid such issues in the future. We emphasize the importance of contacting simulator authors and developers when unexpected results are observed (especially and importantly before publishing such results), to ensure these simulators are used with correct configurations and as intended. Our investigation also aims to stimulate discussion on artifact evaluation practices and on mechanisms for correcting results and artifacts after publication. To aid future works and reproduction of all our results, we open source all our code and scripts at https://github.com/CMU-SAFARI/Cleaning-up-the-Mess. We refer the reader to our full ISPASS 2026 paper and its artifact for the complete analysis, detailed methodology, and extended results.

</details>

### [22] [A Tutorial on IEEE 802.11bn Multi-AP Coordination for Wi-Fi 8: From Standardization to Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13759)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta,Giovanni Geraci,Lorenzo Galati-Giordano,Francesca Meneghello,Aleksandra Kijanka,Iñaki Val,David López-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文全面分析了IEEE 802.11bn标准中的多接入点协调(MAPC)框架，该框架旨在实现超高可靠性(UHR)目标，相比802.11be标准在吞吐量、延迟和丢包率方面提升至少25%。文章还介绍了开源Wi-Fi 8仿真工具Kom8ndor用于评估MAPC特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEEE 802.11bn修正案定义了无线局域网(WLAN)中超高可靠性(UHR)目标，需要在吞吐量、延迟第95百分位数和MPDU丢失率方面相比802.11be的极高吞吐量(EHT)操作提升至少25%。为实现这些雄心勃勃的目标，引入多接入点协调(MAPC)作为关键创新，使AP能够相互协调以提高频谱利用率并推进可靠性。

Method: 首先回顾802.11bn标准之前的现有AP协调解决方案，为理解向当前框架的过渡奠定基础。然后详细描述802.11bn任务组定义的技术MAPC框架，对每个候选MAPC特性进行全面概述并结合最新技术进行情境化分析。引入Kom8ndor开源Wi-Fi 8仿真工具来评估这些候选MAPC特性。

Result: 展示了候选MAPC特性实现UHR目标的潜力。通过Kom8ndor仿真工具验证了MAPC框架在提高频谱利用率和网络可靠性方面的有效性。证明了协调方案能够满足802.11bn标准设定的性能提升目标。

Conclusion: MAPC是实现IEEE 802.11bn超高可靠性目标的基础创新。文章展望了802.11bn之后MAPC的未来发展，探索了超越802.11bn的协调方案(如联合传输JT)和新思路，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The IEEE 802.11bn amendment defines significant modifications to the standard by establishing Ultra High Reliability (UHR) targets in Wireless Local Area Networks (WLANs). This is expected to deliver substantial enhancements over previous standards, including modes of operation that increase throughput, reduce the 95th percentile of the latency distribution, and decrease MAC Protocol Data Unit (MPDU) loss (all by at least 25%) compared to Extremely High Throughput (EHT) operations defined in the 802.11be amendment. A fundamental innovation for achieving these ambitious goals is the introduction of Multi-Access Point Coordination (MAPC), an unprecedented feature whereby APs will be able to coordinate among themselves to enhance spectrum utilization and advance towards reliability. This paper provides a comprehensive overview and analysis of this key framework. We begin by reviewing existing AP coordination solutions that precede the 802.11bn standard, which serve as a foundation for understanding the transition to the current framework. We then describe the technical 802.11bn MAPC framework as defined by the task group. A detailed overview of each candidate MAPC feature is provided, contextualized with the relevant state-of-the-art. Furthermore, we introduce Kom8ndor, an open-source Wi-Fi 8 simulation tool, to evaluate these candidate MAPC features and showcase their potential to achieve UHR goals. Finally, we outline the future of MAPC beyond 802.11bn, exploring promising directions such as coordination schemes beyond 802.11bn (e.g., Joint Transmission (JT)) and new ideas.

</details>


### [23] [Temporally Consistent Graph Q-Networks for Intelligent Network Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13848)
*Zacharias Veiksaar,Maxime Bouton*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的多智能体强化学习算法TC-GQN，用于移动网络的高级控制和编排，通过学习任务无关的网络自预测表示来实现全局协调的局部动作分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动网络复杂性的不断增长，下一代网络需要支持不断增加的流量负载和更多样化的服务，在动态或变化的目标下优化天线参数变得愈发具有挑战性。

Method: 提出时间一致性图Q网络（TC-GQN）算法，利用图神经网络学习整个网络的任务无关自预测表示，通过全局奖励函数训练实现基于全局网络状态编码的协调局部动作分配。

Result: 在模拟环境中对多扇区和多载波进行节能特性编排实验，该算法在满足QoS约束的同时提高了硬件休眠时间，性能优于最先进的基于图的基线和竞争性基于规则的控制器。

Conclusion: TC-GQN算法成功实现了移动网络的协调控制，不仅改善了能效，而且学习到的表示能够实现对变化意图的快速适应。

Abstract: Mobile networks continue to grow in complexity and next generation networks are expected to support both increasing traffic loads and more diverse services. As network complexity rises, optimizing antenna parameters under dynamic or changing objectives becomes increasingly challenging. We propose a novel multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm for high-level control and orchestration of mobile networks.
  The Temporally Consistent Graph Q-Network (TC-GQN) algorithm learns a self-predicting representation of the whole network that is task-independent and aggregates information from all base-stations. A graph neural network is trained using a global reward function to assign coordinated local actions based on the learned encoding of the global network state. We evaluate the algorithm in a simulated environment to orchestrate an energy-saving feature across multiple sectors and multiple carriers under different quality of service (QoS) constraints. The proposed algorithm outperforms state-of-the-art graph-based baselines and a competitive rule-based controller by improving hardware sleep time while maintaining QoS. Moreover, the learned representation enables rapid adaptation to changing intents.

</details>


### [24] [ARTSN: Exact and Adaptive Self-triggered Traffic Scheduling for ARTS Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13899)
*Ruide Cao,Shuangping Zhan,Jiashuo Lin,Yan Liu,Chenxi Ling,Yi Wang,Guoming Tang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对ARTS网络中自触发（ST）流量面临的波动性和缺席性挑战，提出了基于TSN的ARTSN调度框架，通过离线精确调度和在线自适应时隙释放机制，显著提升了调度性能和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ARTS网络中，自触发（ST）流量由内部决策触发而非固定调度，日益普遍且对实时自主行为至关重要。然而现有网络调度器无法有效支持ST流量，主要面临两大固有挑战：波动性（有界处理抖动导致到达时间不确定）和缺席性（ST流量未出现时预留资源利用率低）。

Method: 提出ARTSN调度范式，基于时间敏感网络（TSN）构建，包含两项关键技术：（1）精确离线调度方法，利用ST流量的可推断到达信息进行精确时隙预留；（2）自适应在线时隙释放机制，在ST流量缺席时动态回收未使用的预留资源。

Result: 在TSN仿真器和实际测试平台上的大量实验表明，ARTSN在可调度性、可扩展性和效率方面显著优于最先进方法，同时保持可靠的传输保障。

Conclusion: ARTSN成功解决了ST流量的波动性和缺席性挑战，为ARTS网络提供了一种高效的ST定制调度解决方案，在保证传输可靠性的同时大幅提升了网络资源利用效率。

Abstract: Autonomous real-time systems (ARTS), such as self-driving vehicles and robotic assembly lines, are increasingly deployed to improve efficiency, accuracy, and responsiveness with reduced human intervention. In ARTS networks, self-triggered (ST) traffic-initiated by internal decision-making rather than fixed schedules or external events-is becoming prevalent and plays a critical role in enabling timely autonomous actions. However, existing network schedulers do not adequately support ST traffic due to two inherent challenges: volatility, where bounded processing jitter leads to uncertain arrival times, and absence, where reserved network resources remain underutilized when ST traffic does not materialize. To address these challenges, we propose ARTSN, an ST-tailored scheduling paradigm built upon time-sensitive networking (TSN). ARTSN introduces two key techniques: (1) an exact offline scheduling method that leverages the inferable arrival information of ST traffic for precise time-slot reservation, and (2) an adaptive online slot-release mechanism that dynamically reclaims unused reservations when ST traffic is absent. Extensive experiments on both a TSN simulator and a real-world testbed show that ARTSN significantly improves schedulability, scalability, and efficiency over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining reliable transmission guarantees.

</details>


### [25] [Agon: A Semi-Supervised Framework for Robust Satellite Interference Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14147)
*Boyu Yang,Chunyu Yang,Zhe Chen,Kun Qiu,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agon框架，一种半监督卫星干扰检测方法，通过两阶段混合学习范式解决了现有机器学习方法中固定阈值导致的高误报率和跨域依赖缺失问题，实现了25.3%的AUC提升和超过90%的调制分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着非静止轨道(NGSO)卫星的快速扩张与现有静止轨道(GSO)系统的共存，频谱拥堵和系统间干扰日益严重。现有基于机器学习的重构模型受限于固定阈值，AUC仅为0.83，导致不可接受的误报率；同时解耦训练范式忽略了跨域依赖性，使时域和频域AUC分别限制在0.83和0.71，无法满足下一代通信网络实时干扰管理的需求。

Method: 提出了Agon半监督卫星干扰检测框架，采用新颖的两阶段混合学习范式：1)第一阶段整合了掩码自编码器(MAE)预训练双注意力变换器(DAT)；2)第二阶段通过多任务微调优化直接二分类器，有效消除了不稳定阈值。此外，引入高阶统计量(HOS)增强注意力和小波正则化来增强噪声鲁棒性和结构保真度。

Result: 在公共NGSO-GSO数据集和高保真NGSO-NGSO数据集上的广泛验证表明：1)Agon实现了最先进的检测性能，AUC提升25.3%；2)多任务学习框架实现了超过90%的调制分类准确率；3)在不同离轴角和干扰噪声比(INR)的多样化场景中均能保持最优检测性能。

Conclusion: Agon框架成功解决了卫星干扰检测中固定阈值和跨域依赖的关键问题，通过创新的半监督学习和多任务学习方法显著提升了检测性能，为NGSO-GSO共存网络提供了可靠的实时干扰管理解决方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of non-geostationary orbit (NGSO) satellites alongside existing geostationary orbit (GSO) systems has intensified spectrum congestion and inter-system interference, placing stringent demands on real-time interference management to sustain reliable coexistence in next-generation communication networks. While existing machine learning (ML)-based reconstruction models have made strides, they remain constrained to an area under the curve (AUC) of 0.83 due to fixed thresholds, causing unacceptable false alarm rates that undermine critical link reliability. Additionally, their decoupled training paradigm neglects cross-domain dependencies, limiting time and frequency-domain AUCs to 0.83 and 0.71, respectively. To address these limitations, this paper introduces a semi-supervised satellite interference detection framework named Agon, employing a novel two-stage hybrid learning paradigm. Agon integrates masked autoencoder (MAE) pre-training of a dual attention transformer (DAT) with multi-task fine-tuning to optimize a direct binary classifier, effectively eliminating unstable thresholds. Furthermore, it incorporates high-order statistics (HOS)-augmented attention and wavelet regularization to bolster noise robustness and structural fidelity. Extensive validation on public NGSO-GSO dataset and a high-fidelity NGSO-NGSO dataset demonstrates that Agon achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) detection performance, with a 25.3% improvement in AUC. Moreover, the multi-task learning (MTL) framework facilitates accurate modulation classification with accuracies exceeding 90%, while simultaneously maintaining optimal detection performance across diverse scenarios characterized by varying off-axis angles and interference-to-noise ratios (INRs).

</details>


### [26] [Aidos: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm for Beam Hopping Scheduling in NGSO Mega-Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14151)
*Lingkai Zhao,Zhe Chen,Kun Qiu,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Aidos算法，用于解决非静止轨道卫星星座波束跳变时隙规划问题。在1127个服务小区规模下，算法将吞吐量提升79.2%，延迟降低99.45%，计算时间仅需9.3秒（相比遗传算法的67.8秒），实现了卫星过境窗口内的在线重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波束跳变时隙规划（BHTP）生成方法存在显著局限性：传统轮询策略对长尾分布的实际流量分配不合理（10%热点小区产生超50%需求）；遗传算法虽吞吐量提升80.7%，但计算时间达67.8秒，难以在300秒低轨卫星可见窗口内多次在线重算；前沿多智能体深度强化学习（MADRL）在小区数超过200时无法收敛。大规模星座场景下亟需高效实时BHTP生成方案。

Method: 提出Aidos波束调度算法，采用两项核心创新：1）将流量感知随机键编码集成到多目标元启发式搜索框架中；2）在自适应分布进化过程中应用滑动窗口Beta重采样策略。该方法同时优化搜索效率和解的质量。

Result: 在1127个服务小区规模下：吞吐量相比传统基准提升79.2%；延迟降低99.45%；平均计算时间9.3秒（遗传算法需67.8秒），满足300秒卫星过境窗口内在线重规划需求。

Conclusion: Aidos算法成功突破了大规模卫星星座波束跳变实时规划的工程瓶颈，在保证高吞吐量与低延迟性能的同时，将计算效率提升约7倍，为非静止轨道星座的资源调度提供了实用的在线优化解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of non-geostationary orbit (NGSO) mega-constellations, beam hopping (BH) has become indispensable for resource scheduling in multi-satellite, multi-coverage scenarios. By dynamically adjusting spot beam power and pointing within each time slot, BH enables highly efficient spectrum utilization. A principal engineering challenge is the real-time generation of beam hopping time plans (BHTP). Traditional algorithms, such as the round-robin strategy, distribute beams evenly across all service cells in a round-robin fashion. However, real traffic follows a long-tail distribution; the most active 10% of hotspot cells generate more than 50% of the aggregate demand, making uniform allocation inadequate. To address this issue, existing frameworks adopt a genetic algorithm (GA), whose throughput is approximately 80.7% higher than the traditional baseline. Operational satellite footprints encompass more than 1,000 service cells. The GA requires 67.8 s to generate a BHTP for 1,127 cells. With a 550 km LEO satellite providing only a 300 s visibility window, multiple online recomputations are impractical. State-of-the-art algorithms, such as multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL), fail to converge once the cell count exceeds 200. To overcome these challenges, we propose a novel BH scheduling algorithm Aidos. The algorithm integrates traffic-aware random-key encoding into a multi-objective metaheuristic search, and then applies a sliding-window Beta resampling strategy during adaptive distribution evolution, to improve both the search efficiency and the solution quality of the BHTP. Experiments demonstrate that Aidos improves throughput by 79.2% and reduces latency by 99.45%. Its average computation time is 9.3 s, enabling online replanning within a 300 s satellite overpass window.

</details>


### [27] [StreamRTPS: Increasing DDS Bandwidth Efficiency by Reducing Protocol Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14214)
*David Philipp Klüner,Stefan Kowalewski,Alexandru Kampmann*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种RTPS协议扩展机制（流协商、载荷聚合、预测性心跳抑制），在保持兼容性的前提下，将带宽消耗降低高达27.9%（流头部）和22.7%（心跳抑制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对数据分发服务(DDS)底层的实时发布订阅(RTPS)线协议存在的带宽效率低下问题，尤其是在IP/UDP头部开销和心跳控制流量方面的冗余。

Method: 提出三种扩展机制：1)流协商机制在发现阶段交换静态头部信息，运行时用2字节标识符替换完整RTPS头部；2)载荷聚合方案将相同样本聚合到单个UDP包中，减少IP/UDP头部开销；3)预测性心跳抑制策略针对周期性通信模式省略心跳，检测到丢包或时序违规时回退。所有机制通过扩展DDS发现过程在支持时激活，保持RTPS兼容性。

Result: 实验结果表明：在尽力传输下，流头部机制相比传统RTPS降低带宽消耗高达27.9%；在可靠传输下，心跳抑制机制在流头部基础上进一步降低22.7%；两种情况下均保持传输延迟不变。

Conclusion: 所提出的三种机制有效提升了RTPS协议的带宽效率，在保持协议兼容性和传输延迟的前提下显著降低了开销，对实时分布式系统的通信优化具有重要价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose three extensions to the Real-Time Publish Subscribe wire protocol, on which Data Distribution Service (DDS) is based, to improve bandwidth efficiency. First, a stream negotiation mechanism exchanges static header information during discovery, replacing the full RTPS header at runtime with a compact 2 B identifier. Second, a payload aggregation scheme aggregates samples for the same locator into single UDP packets, reducing IP and UDP header costs. Third, a predictive heartbeat suppression strategy reduces control traffic by omitting heartbeats for periodic communication patterns, falling back upon detected loss or timing violations. All three mechanisms preserve Real-Time Publish Subscribe(RTPS) compatibility by extending DDS discovery to activate these features when supported. Experimental results show that stream headers reduce bandwidth consumption by up to 27.9 % compared to conventional RTPS under best-effort transport, and that heartbeat suppression yields a further 22.7 % reduction on top of stream headers under reliable transport, while preserving transmission latency in both cases.

</details>


### [28] [Selective Field Transmission: Bandwidth Efficient Communication under Standardized Message Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14228)
*David Philipp Klüner,David Murach,Stefan Kowalewski,Alexandru Kampmann*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFT中间件机制，通过动态适配传输内容解决发布-订阅系统中标准消息类型与自定义类型的权衡问题，在保持标准接口不变的前提下显著降低带宽消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业和机器人开发者在消息类型选择上面临两难：使用标准消息类型（如ROS 2、COVESA）可获得可重用性和互操作性，但会传输冗余数据；使用定制消息类型可减少带宽，但牺牲了标准化优势。现有方案无法兼顾这两方面需求。

Method: 提出选择性字段传输（SFT）中间件机制，实现传输内容与静态消息类型的解耦。接收方声明或自动推导所需的消息字段并传递给发布方，发布方针对每个接收方仅序列化和传输所需的字段子集，整个过程只需最少的开发者干预。

Result: 评估结果表明，SFT在无可测量的每消息延迟开销情况下，实现了显著的带宽缩减，节省量与未使用字段的数量和大小成正比，成功实现了带宽优化目标。

Conclusion: SFT有效解决了标准消息类型与带宽优化之间的固有矛盾，使开发者能够同时受益于标准接口的可重用性/互操作性和定制消息类型的带宽效率，为发布-订阅系统提供了一种实用的优化方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and evaluate Selective Field Transmission (SFT), a middleware mechanism that decouples transmission content from statically defined message types in publish-subscribe systems. Industrial and robotics developers often face a dilemma: They can follow established best practices and use standard message types, such as in the Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) and COVESA projects, to benefit from reusable and interoperable interfaces, or they can introduce proprietary, project-specific message types tailored to receiver requirements to reduce bandwidth. SFT resolves this trade-off by dynamically adapting the transmitted message components to each receivers actual needs while preserving unmodified standard interfaces. Receivers declare or automatically derive the required message components, which are communicated to the publisher. The publisher then serializes and transmits only the required component subset per receiver with minimal developer intervention. Our evaluation shows that SFT achieves significant bandwidth reductions without measurable per-message latency overhead, with savings proportional to the number and size of unused fields.

</details>


### [29] [When the UE Turns Adversary: Real-Time Uplink Jamming from Within 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14465)
*Rosolino Alaimo,Alessandra Dino,Ilenia Tinnirello,Domenico Garlisi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对5G NR网络PUSCH信道的隐蔽选择性反应式干扰攻击框架STORM-RJ，利用SDR实现时频对齐的精确干扰，揭示了低层无线电控制对满足微秒级响应要求的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对5G NR网络物理上行共享信道(PUSCH)遭受隐蔽、选择性反应式干扰攻击的深入分析，需要设计能够精确时频对齐、满足严格时序约束的干扰框架来评估此类攻击的实际可行性。

Method: 设计并实现基于SDR的STORM-RJ框架：通过解码下行控制信息(DCI)识别上行授权(UL-Grants)，动态调整噪声突发的带宽和中心频率，实现与目标UE资源块的精确同步；优化软硬件处理和RF前端的延迟源；比较高层级与低层级无线电控制策略的响应性能。

Result: 仅低层级无线电控制能提供满足5G-NR时序约束的微秒级响应能力；成功实现了选择性干扰的实际可行性验证；揭示了SDR灵活性、处理延迟和同步精度之间的关键权衡。

Conclusion: 在现实硬件和时序约束下，选择性反应式干扰在技术上可行且隐蔽性强；提出了包括HARQ异常检测在内的潜在缓解策略，为5G网络安全性研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: This paper presents an investigation of a novel class of stealthy and selective reactive jamming attacks targeting the Physical Uplink Shared Channel (PUSCH) in 5G New Radio (NR) networks. We design and implement STORM-RJ (Stealthy Timing Obstruction and Radio Manipulation -- Reactive Jamming), a Software-Defined Radio (SDR)-based adversarial framework that enables highly precise, time-frequency aligned interference by dynamically adapting the bandwidth and center frequency of injected noise bursts in real time. STORM-RJ leverages decoded Downlink Control Information (DCI) to identify Uplink-Grants (UL-Grants) and synchronizes interference exactly with the resource blocks allocated to a target User Equipment (UE). We further characterize and mitigate the dominant latency sources -- both at the software processing and hardware Radio Frequency (RF) frontend levels -- to achieve a rapid jamming response upon grant detection. We conduct a comparative analysis of high-level versus low-level radio control strategies, demonstrating that only low-level tuning provides the microsecond-scale responsiveness necessary to meet 5G-NR timing constraints for effective reactive jamming. We analyze the practical feasibility of such selective jamming under realistic hardware and timing constraints, highlighting key trade-offs between SDR flexibility, processing latency, and synchronization accuracy. Finally, we discuss potential mitigation strategies, including Hybrid Automatic Repeat reQuest (HARQ) anomaly detection.

</details>

### [30] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度策略，在小规模实例上训练后可直接泛化至大规模问题（40×40至100×100），性能优于多种经典调度启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放车间调度问题（OSSP）在大规模场景下计算复杂度高，精确方法难以求解，而经典调度规则和元启发式算法在大规模问题中往往需要大量参数调整才能保持解的质量。为解决上述问题，本文旨在开发一种无需复杂特征工程、泛化能力强且易于应用的基于学习的调度策略。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构构建调度策略，利用多头注意力机制；仅以加工时间矩阵为输入，在Taillard基准实例（4×4至10×10）上训练模型；随后将训练好的策略直接应用于随机生成的40×40至100×100的大规模实例，并与SPT、LPT、MWKR、EST等经典调度启发式算法进行对比评估。

Result: 模型生成的调度方案其完工时间通常在已知最优解的15-30%范围内。在大规模实例测试中，Transformer相对于标准下界的平均偏差为12.89-15.12%，与EST性能相当，且显著优于SPT和LPT。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的调度策略在小规模实例上训练后具备良好的可扩展性，可泛化至大规模OSSP问题，为传统调度规则提供了一种轻特征、基于学习的有效替代方案。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [31] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 2026年最佳AI代理在WorkBench上的任务完成率从43%提升至89%，意外有害行为率从26%降至2.5%，能力与安全性呈正相关而非权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪并分析AI代理在WorkBench基准测试上从2024年3月至2026年6月的能力提升与安全性表现变化，揭示能力与安全性的关系以及开源模型对成本的影响。

Method: 对比分析GPT-4（2024年3月最佳）与Claude Opus 4.8（2026年6月最佳）在WorkBench基准测试上的表现，发布包含数据、代码质量改进和新增模型分数的更新版本。

Result: 任务完成率从43%提升至89%，意外有害行为率从26%降至2.5%；能力与安全性正相关；部分错误类别已消除但基础错误仍存在；开源模型大幅降低了同等性能的使用成本。

Conclusion: 前沿AI代理实现了能力与安全性的双重进步，二者并不冲突而是相互促进；开源模型的兴起降低了高性能AI的使用门槛，但基础错误导致的不可逆伤害仍是待解决的关键问题。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>

*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: UP-NRPA框架成功实现了无需训练机制即可适应多样化用户特征的对话策略定制，为对话系统提供了更加灵活和高效的适应性解决方案。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.
