Result: 所提出的方法在不同评估设置下持续优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 成功引入了药物-疾病治疗关系适用条件提取这一新任务，为临床决策支持提供了重要资源和方法，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Identifying conditions that a certain drug takes therapeutic effect on a target disease is crucial for clinical decision-making support. However, most existing biomedical information extraction methods have focused on identifying only relations between drugs and diseases, while largely overlooking the context-specific conditions where such relations can apply. To address this problem, we introduce the task of applicability condition extraction for therapeutic drug--disease relations from biomedical research literature. We create the first dataset that has manually annotated triples of drugs, diseases, and applicability conditions on biomedical paper abstracts with 1,119 drug-disease pairs. Using this dataset, we systematically evaluate the performance of a range of existing methods. In addition, we propose a new method that enhances LoRA to consider relations between drugs and diseases. Our method consistently outperforms strong baselines across different evaluation settings. The source code and dataset of this paper can be obtained from: https://github.com/guantingluo98/Drug-ACE

</details>


### [51] [FactoryLLM: A Safe and Open-Source AI Playground for Evaluating LLMs in Smart Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14119)
*Yash Pulse,Yong-Bin Kang,Abhik Banerjee,Abdur Forkan,Prem Prakash Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FactoryLLM，一个用于评估基于大语言模型（LLM）的检索增强生成（RAG）模型的安全开源AI平台，旨在解决智能工厂中跨多机器手册的故障诊断与恢复问题。通过案例研究验证，所有模型在跨机器文档推理中的基础性得分均超过0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂中的故障诊断与恢复具有挑战性，因为关键信息分散在通过制造过程相互关联的多台机器手册中。大语言模型（LLM）为解决这一问题提供了有前景的途径。

Method: 提出FactoryLLM平台，允许用户配置LLM并通过双重评估设置（使用RAGAS和NVIDIA的LLM-as-a-Judge指标）评估其在多文档推理上的性能。该平台支持运行本地或开源LLM，确保不共享敏感工业数据。通过涉及自主智能车辆及其移动规划器软件的案例研究，评估三个LLM在来自约600页跨机器文档的30个维护查询上的表现。

Result: 案例研究表明，FactoryLLM在跨机器文档推理方面有效：每个模型的基础性得分均超过0.88。

Conclusion: FactoryLLM为制造业特定场景提供了一个安全、可控的实验环境，其代码和文档已公开发布，供社区测试使用。

Abstract: Fault diagnostics and recovery in smart factories is challenging because critical information is dispersed across manuals of multiple machines which are interconnected through the manufacturing process. Large Language Models (LLMs) can provide a promising approach. In this paper, we propose FactoryLLM, a safe and open-source AI playground designed for evaluating different LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) models by analysing documents from multiple machines across the manufacturing process. FactoryLLM enables the user to configure the LLM, and assess performance when reasoning over multiple documents, through a dual evaluation setup using both RAGAS and NVIDIA's LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. FactoryLLM is safe because it allows users to run local or open-source LLMs without sharing sensitive industrial data, providing a controlled environment for experimentation. We demonstrate the efficacy of FactoryLLM through a case study which involves an Autonomous Intelligent Vehicle and its Mobile Planner software, evaluating three LLMs across 30 maintenance queries derived from approximately 600 pages of cross-machine documentation. The results suggest that FactoryLLM is effective in cross-machine document reasoning: every model achieved a groundedness score above 0.88. The full code and documentation for community to test FactoryLLM with their manufacturing specific scenarios are publicly available.

</details>


### [52] [VeriGeo: Controllable Geometry Question Generation with Numerical and Analytical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14176)
*Xiaoxian Duan,Zequn Liu,Yingce Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了VeriGeo框架，通过可执行推理轨迹和三阶段验证流程，实现了可控且可靠的几何问题生成，生成的合成数据在多个几何推理基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何问题生成对AI辅助教育和多模态数学推理很有价值，但可靠合成仍然困难，因为问题陈述、图表、约束和解答需要相互一致。现有方法往往在可控性和可靠性之间存在权衡：基于种子的重写灵活但验证性弱，而图表优先构建提高了有效性但不适合任意用户指定的约束。

Method: 引入VeriGeo框架，包含Author代理和Solver代理。Author代理根据用户约束（如目标概念和难度）生成问题和图表，Solver代理生成对齐证明的解答。两个代理使用共享动作序列，将自然语言、图表、几何约束和证明步骤连接成可验证的表示。三阶段流水线检查数值一致性、分析可实现性和全局一致性，使用验证引导的反思来修复可恢复的失败并拒绝不可恢复的失败。

Result: 在五个LLM骨干网络上，原始生成经常无法通过检查，而VeriGeo修复了大量无效尝试。在VeriGeo生成的8.7k样本上进行监督微调，在端到端多模态基于LLM的求解器中取得了最佳的GeoQA性能，并在PGPS9K和MathVista-GPS上获得了强劲结果。

Conclusion: 验证过的合成数据对改进多模态几何推理具有有效性，VeriGeo框架成功实现了可控且可靠的几何问题生成，为AI辅助教育提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Geometry problem generation is useful for AI-assisted education and multimodal mathematical reasoning, but reliable synthesis remains difficult because the problem statement, diagram, constraints, and solution should be mutually consistent. Existing methods often trade off controllability and reliability: seed-based rewriting is flexible but weakly verifiable, whereas diagram-first construction improves validity but is less suited to arbitrary user-specified constraints. We introduce VeriGeo, a controllable geometry generation framework grounded in executable reasoning traces. Given user constraints such as target concepts and difficulty, an Author agent generates a problem and diagram, and a Solver agent produces a proof-aligned solution. Both agents use a shared action sequence that connects natural language, diagrams, geometric constraints, and proof steps into a verifiable representation. A three-stage pipeline checks numerical consistency, analytical realizability, and global consistency, using verification-guided reflection to repair recoverable failures and reject unrecoverable ones. Across five LLM backbones, raw generations frequently fail these checks, while VeriGeo repairs a substantial fraction of the invalid attempts. Supervised fine-tuning on 8.7k examples generated by VeriGeo achieves the best reported GeoQA performance among end-to-end multimodal LLM-based solvers, and obtains strong results on PGPS9K and MathVista-GPS, demonstrating the effectiveness of verified synthetic data for improving multimodal geometry reasoning.

</details>


### [53] [When Should Agent Trust Be Conditional? Characterizing and Attacking Skill-Conditional Reputation in Agent Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14200)
*Yihan Xia,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能条件信任R(i|k)替代单一全局信任评分，在异构LLM代理池中实现技能特化路由。在特定条件下（高异构性、稀疏证据、技能相关）获得性能提升，但发现跨技能证据共享存在安全漏洞：攻击者可通过零成本证据劫持路由器，将路由后悔度从0提升至0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放平台中异构LLM代理的能力在不同技能间差异显著——某技能优秀的代理在其他技能可能毫无用处。传统全局信任评分忽略了专业化价值，将所有任务路由至全局最优代理，未能利用技能特化优势。需要一种技能条件化的信任评估方法，以实现基于技能的精准路由。

Method: 1) 提出技能条件信任R(i|k)形式化框架；2) 通过相图分析确定条件信任的有效边界（高异构性、稀疏证据、技能相关）；3) 设计耦合强度β控制跨技能证据借用；4) 在14个真实异构AppWorld代理池验证；5) 发现安全漏洞并提出零证据门控机制；6) 引入条件信息价值测试(CIVT)进行安全评级。

Result: 在真实异构代理池中落入有利区间，获得小幅但真实的性能增益，各技能最优代理确实因技能而变。但发现严重安全漏洞：攻击者用单技能廉价证据可在目标技能零证据情况下劫持路由器，路由后悔度从0升至0.94。CIVT评级为GREEN的池仍可被攻击，受污染的信任判读为-0.06而非真实的+0.19。零证据门控可约束但无法消除攻击。

Conclusion: 技能条件信任在特定条件下（高异构性、稀疏证据、技能相关）优于全局信任，但其核心机制（跨技能证据借用）具有双重用途：既是数据效率的来源，也是攻击者利用的洗白通道。研究量化了效率与安全间的权衡，揭示了条件信任框架的安全边界，为异构代理池的安全路由提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Open platforms increasingly route tasks among heterogeneous LLM agents--differing in base model, scaffold, and tool stack--whose competence varies sharply by skill: an agent excellent at one skill may be useless at another. The standard reputation approach summarizes each agent by a single global trust score, but that scalar is the wrong object here, because routing every task to the globally most-trusted agent leaves the value of specialization unclaimed. We study skill-conditional trust R(i | k)--the trust to place in agent i for a task requiring skill k, rather than one score per agent--and pose three falsifiable questions: when is conditioning worth it, how much cross-skill evidence should be borrowed, and whether that borrowing is safe. A controlled phase-diagram analysis answers the first two: conditional trust wins only in a specific regime--high agent heterogeneity, sparse per-skill evidence, and correlated skills--and the coupling strength beta that buys this data efficiency is dual-use, because the same cross-skill borrowing is also a laundering channel. On a public benchmark of 14 genuinely heterogeneous AppWorld agents, real pools land inside the beneficial regime--a small but genuine gain, with the per-skill best agent genuinely changing across skills. We then show that an attacker with cheap evidence in one skill and none in a target skill hijacks the conditional router, driving routing regret from 0 to 0.94 on a pool our zero-cost Conditional Information Value Test (CIVT) rates GREEN--while the ungated trust verdict it contaminates reads -0.06 instead of the honest +0.19. A zero-evidence gate bounds the attack but does not eliminate it; we characterize the residual cost under an explicit budget. We do not claim Sybil-resistance--we quantify the trade-off.

</details>


### [54] [Closing the Reflection Gap: A Free Calibration Bonus for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14211)
*Yinglun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现LLM智能体存在“反思差距”，即结合环境反馈后易错误评估自身表现，标准强化学习无法解决；提出RefGRPO方法，通过添加自我反思与实际结果对比的校准奖励项及动态系数，在无额外监督下提升反思校准度和任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在与外部环境交互并观察执行反馈时，应当能正确评估自身表现，但研究发现其存在持续性的“反思差距”：即使答案正确也易误判，标准RL因信用分配不匹配而难以改善。

Method: 提出RefGRPO，在标准RL算法基础上增加两部分：1）通过对比智能体自身反思与实际结果计算免费校准奖励项（无需额外奖励模型、LLM评判或外部标注）；2）对该校准项系数进行动态调度。

Result: 在文本到SQL的五个基准测试上，与标准RL基线相比：反思校准显著提升（例如，不自信率从44.4%降至7.7%），任务准确率同步提高（如75.1%升至76.5%）。校准后的反思使智能体成为基于环境反馈的自我验证者，进一步支持无结果监督的自改进和更有效的测试时选择性预测。

Conclusion: RefGRPO通过简单有效的方式弥合了反思差距，同时提升了反思校准与任务性能，使LLM智能体能更好地利用环境反馈进行自我评估与改进，且无需额外标注成本。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that interact with external environments and observe feedback such as execution results, error messages, and tool outputs. A well-functioning agent should be able to leverage this feedback to accurately assess its own performance. Yet we find a persistent reflection gap: LLM agents tend to mis-assess their own outputs after observing concrete environment feedback -- even for questions they correctly answered -- and standard RL barely helps due to a credit-assignment mismatch. To close this gap, we propose RefGRPO, a simple yet effective fix that augments standard RL algorithms with two key ingredients: a free calibration bonus computed by contrasting the agent's own reflection with the actual outcome (requiring no additional reward model, LLM judge, or external annotation), and a dynamic schedule on its coefficient. Compared to standard RL baselines, our method simultaneously improves reflection calibration (e.g., reduces underconfidence rate $44.4\% \to 7.7\%$) and task accuracy (e.g., $75.1\% \to 76.5\%$) on text-to-SQL across five benchmarks. The resulting calibrated reflection turns the agent into its own verifier grounded in environment feedback, which further enables (i) better self-improvement that uses reflections as pseudo-rewards without outcome supervision, and (ii) more effective test-time selective prediction by committing only to rollouts flagged as correct.

</details>


### [55] [SkillAudit: Ground-Truth-Free Skill Evolution via Paired Trajectory Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14239)
*Haowen Gao,Haoran Chen,Can Wang,Shasha Guo,Liang Pang,Zhaoyang Liu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillAudit框架，通过成对轨迹审计和无真值反馈的方式演化智能体技能，在89个专业领域任务上平均任务奖励达73.9%，显著超越无技能基线(40.9%)和静态专家技能(56.7%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能在部署后往往因边缘案例、API变化和部署约束而失效，技能演化成为实际需求。现有方法依赖特权反馈（如验证分数、隐藏测试结果、环境奖励），但实践者通常仅有任务描述和工作空间数据，无法获取这些信号。

Method: 提出SkillAudit框架：1）成对轨迹审计：每次迭代执行同一任务时分别使用和不使用候选技能，隔离技能对行为的改变；2）过程对齐对比评估(PACE)：评估器集群将轨迹差异映射到与技能文档特定段落相关的诊断信号；3）结构验证器：从任务规范编译后固定，检查任务约束并回滚有害更新；4）双管道编辑：Refine移除广泛有用技能中的噪声或不相关指导，Repair替换与任务冲突的段落。

Result: 在跨越8个专业领域的89个容器化任务上，SkillAudit实现73.9%的平均任务奖励，优于无技能智能体(40.9%)和静态专家技能(56.7%)。这些收益在演化过程中无需访问隐藏测试、参考解决方案或外部评分函数。

Conclusion: SkillAudit成功实现了无真值反馈条件下的智能体技能演化，证明了通过行为差异分析而非外部标签来改进技能的可行性，为实际部署场景中的技能维护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Agent skills are structured procedural packages that guide frozen LLM agents in specialized workflows. Skills rarely remain sufficient after deployment: edge cases, API changes, and deployment constraints become visible only through use, making skill evolution a practical necessity. Existing methods depend on privileged feedback such as held-out validation scores, hidden test outcomes, or environment rewards -- signals often unavailable when a practitioner has only a task description and workspace data. We introduce SkillAudit, a framework for evolving agent skills without ground-truth feedback. The key idea is paired trajectory auditing: at each iteration, the same task is executed with and without the candidate skill, isolating how the skill changes agent behavior without external labels. To turn behavioral differences into edit guidance, SkillAudit uses Process-Aligned Contrastive Evaluation (PACE), a cluster of evaluators that maps trajectory divergences to diagnostic signals linked to specific passages in the skill document. A structural verifier, compiled once from the task specification and then fixed, checks task constraints and rolls back harmful updates. SkillAudit routes edits through two pipelines: Refine removes noisy or irrelevant guidance from broadly useful skills, while Repair replaces passages that conflict with the task. Across 89 containerized tasks spanning 8 professional domains, SkillAudit achieves 73.9% average task reward, outperforming an agent without skills (40.9%) and the static expert skill (56.7%). These gains are obtained without accessing hidden tests, reference solutions, or external scoring functions during evolution.

</details>


### [56] [AFFORDANCE20Q: Evaluating Affordance Reasoning from Physical Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14240)
*Yifan Jiang,Meige Yang,Zitong Li,Jay Pujara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Affordance20Q基准，通过20 Questions游戏形式评估LLM的物理属性推理能力（不暴露物体身份），发现模型比人类性能低约20分；开发的KARI方法利用知识库生成推理规则，将开源LLM性能提升最多15.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有affordance基准在评估设置中暴露物体身份，使模型依赖记忆的物体-功能映射而非基于物理属性的推理；缺乏对模型真正的物理属性推理能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出Affordance20Q基准（1009个游戏，454个物体，59种affordance），通过20 Questions游戏形式让模型通过询问物理属性的yes/no问题推断隐藏物体的功能；开发KB-Anchored Rule Induction (KARI)流程，基于知识库证据生成affordance推理规则来增强LLM。

Result: 15个SOTA LLM实验显示模型与人类存在约20分的显著性能差距；KL-based信息增益分析表明模型在游戏进行中未能提出有效区分问题；KARI方法使开源LLM性能提升最多15.2分，但知识库覆盖度限制进一步改进。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在基于物理属性的affordance推理方面仍显著落后于人类；通过知识库锚定的规则归纳可以有效提升模型性能，但知识库覆盖度是主要瓶颈；Affordance20Q为评估真正的物理推理能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Affordance reasoning, the inference of an object's action possibilities from its physical properties (e.g., shape and material), is fundamental to human physical understanding and increasingly critical for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing affordance benchmarks largely expose explicit object identities in the evaluation setup, allowing models to rely on memorized object-affordance mappings rather than reasoning over physical properties. To address this gap, we introduce Affordance20Q, a novel affordance reasoning benchmark formulated as a 20-Questions game without exposing the object's identity. In each game, the model identifies a hidden object's affordance from a candidate set by asking yes/no questions about its physical properties. Affordance20Q comprises 1,009 games over 454 objects and 59 affordances, all manually filtered, refined, and annotated. We conduct comprehensive experiments with 15 state-of-the-art LLMs and find a substantial gap (~20 points) compared to human performance. A KL-based information-gain (IG) analysis further shows that models fail to ask discriminating questions as the game progresses. To close the gap, we develop KB-Anchored Rule Induction (KARI), a pipeline based on LLMs that generates affordance rules grounded in evidence from knowledge bases (KBs). KARI improves open-source LLMs by up to 15.2 points, while the limited coverage of KBs hinders further gains. We release all our code and data at https://github.com/1171-jpg/Affordance20Q.git

</details>


### [57] [HarnessX: A Composable, Adaptive, and Evolvable Agent Harness Foundry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14249)
*Tingyang Chen,Shuo Lu,Kang Zhao,Weicheng Meng,Hanlin Teng,Tianhao Li,Chao Li,Xule Liu,Jian Liang,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie,Heng Qu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HarnessX框架，通过组合式、自适应的运行时工具链（harness）设计与演化，在五个基准测试上平均提升14.5%（最高44.0%），证明了除了模型扩展外，工具链优化也是提升AI智能体性能的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体的运行时工具链（包括提示词、工具、记忆和控制流）主要依赖手工设计且静态不变，每次遇到新模型或任务都需要定制化搭建，且执行过程中的丰富轨迹很少被用于系统性改进。作者希望打破这种手工定制的瓶颈，实现工具链的自动化组装与演化。

Method: 提出HarnessX框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过替换代数组装类型化的工具链原语；(2) 设计AEGIS引擎，基于轨迹驱动的多智能体演化机制，建立符号适应与强化学习之间的操作镜像；(3) 将执行轨迹转化为工具链更新和模型训练信号，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在五个基准测试（ALFWorld、GAIA、WebShop、tau^3-Bench和SWE-bench Verified）上取得平均+14.5%的性能提升，最高达+44.0%。在基线性能最低的任务上提升最为显著，证明了工具链优化的有效性。

Conclusion: AI智能体的进步不必仅依赖模型规模扩展，从执行反馈中组合和演化运行时接口是一个可操作且互补的优化杠杆。通过自动化工具链设计，可以显著提升智能体性能，为智能体系统设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: AI agent performance depends critically on the runtime harness, comprising the prompts, tools, memory, and control flow that mediate how a model observes, reasons, and acts. Yet today's harnesses remain largely hand-crafted and static: each new model or task still demands bespoke scaffolding, and the rich traces produced during execution are rarely distilled back into systematic improvement. We introduce HarnessX, a foundry for composable, adaptive, and evolvable agent harnesses. HarnessX assembles typed harness primitives via a substitution algebra, adapts them through AEGIS, a trace-driven multi-agent evolution engine grounded in an operational mirror between symbolic adaptation and reinforcement learning, and closes the harness-model loop by turning trajectories into both harness updates and model training signal. Across five benchmarks (ALFWorld, GAIA, WebShop, tau^3-Bench, and SWE-bench Verified), HarnessX yields an average gain of +14.5% (up to +44.0%), with gains largest where baselines are lowest. These results suggest that agent progress need not come from model scaling alone: composing and evolving runtime interfaces from execution feedback is an actionable and complementary lever. The complete codebase will be open-sourced in a future release.

</details>


### [58] [Communication Policy Evolution for Proactive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14314)
*Xinbei Ma,Jiyang Qiu,Yao Yao,Zheng Wu,Yijie Lu,Xiangmou Qu,Jiaxin Yin,Xingyu Lou,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Zhuosheng Zhang,Hai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理的多模态通信策略，发现文本交互利于任务执行，UI交互提升回复质量，提出混合方法和自演化框架CPE，仅通过提示优化实现了最佳任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理已发展为自主系统，但在用户与代理之间存在持续的信息鸿沟：通信成本高昂，而用户相同偏好进一步限制了信息交换。如何在不同模态间进行有效通信成为关键问题。

Method: 1）形式化定义通信策略（Communication Policy），建立文本和UI两类策略；2）构建信息不对称的两个互补设置（用户-代理、规划器-执行器）；3）提出混合方法结合文本与UI优势；4）设计通信策略演化（CPE）自演化框架，通过rollout和提示级演化优化策略。

Result: 文本交互促进任务性能，结构化UI提升代理回复质量和人设符合度；混合方法整合了两者的优势；CPE框架在不修改模型的情况下，仅通过提示优化在多个设置中实现了最佳任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通信行为是LLM代理设计中关键但未被充分探索的设计维度，需要进一步研究多模态通信策略的优化方法。

Abstract: LLM agents have rapidly evolved into autonomous systems, yet a persistent information gap remains between users and agents: communication is costly, while users' identical preferences further limit information exchange. To investigate how agents should communicate across modalities, this paper formalizes Communication Policy, establishes textual and UI-based policies, and then evaluates communication policies across diverse environments, personas, and model combinations. Building information asymmetry for proactive agents, we set up two complementary settings, User-Agent and Planner-Executor. Experimental results reveal complementary strengths between interaction channels: text-based interaction often facilitates task performance, while structured UI improves agents' response quality and persona compliance. Motivated by that, a hybrid method combines these advantages. We further propose Communication Policy Evolution (CPE), a self-evolution framework for refining communication policies through rollout and prompt-level evolving. Without model modification, CPE achieves the best task success across multiple settings using prompt refinement alone. Our findings identify communication behavior as a critical yet underexplored design dimension for LLM agents.

</details>


### [59] [CSPO: Constraint-Sensitive Policy Optimization for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14415)
*Ayoub Belouadah,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束敏感策略优化（CSPO）方法，通过在原始目标中引入基于到安全边界最短有符号距离的约束敏感修正项，解决安全强化学习中原始对偶方法存在的延迟约束修正问题，实现更快的安全恢复和更高的约束回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原始对偶方法在深度强化学习中虽具有良好的扩展性，但存在延迟约束修正问题，导致振荡行为和长期的安全违规。这种延迟使得算法难以快速响应安全约束，影响学习效率和安全性。

Method: 提出约束敏感策略优化（CSPO），一种一阶原始对偶方法。该方法将局部约束敏感性融入策略更新中，通过在原始目标中增加基于到安全边界最短有符号距离推导的约束敏感修正项，实现更智能的安全恢复步骤，补偿延迟的拉格朗日乘子更新，减少边界附近的振荡，同时保持原始约束问题的KKT解。

Result: 在导航和运动控制基准测试上的实验表明，CSPO相比最先进的原始对偶方法和基于惩罚的方法，实现了更快的安全恢复速度和更高的奖励保留，从而获得更高的约束回报。

Conclusion: CSPO通过引入约束敏感修正项有效解决了传统原始对偶方法的延迟约束修正问题，在保证满足安全约束的同时实现了更高的性能，为安全强化学习提供了一个更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (Safe RL) aims to maximize expected return while satisfying safety constraints, typically modeled as Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs). While primal-dual methods scale well to deep RL, they often suffer from delayed constraint correction, leading to oscillatory behavior and prolonged safety violations. In this paper, we propose Constraint-Sensitive Policy Optimization (CSPO), a first-order primal-dual method that incorporates local constraint sensitivity into policy updates. CSPO augments the primal objective with a constraint-sensitive correction derived from the shortest signed distance to the safety boundary, enabling smarter recovery steps back to safety, compensating for delayed Lagrange multiplier updates, reducing oscillations near the boundary, and preserving the KKT solutions of the original constrained problem. Experiments on navigation and locomotion benchmarks demonstrate that CSPO achieves faster safety recovery and high reward preservation, resulting in higher constrained returns compared to state-of-the-art primal-dual and penalty-based methods

</details>


### [60] [Causal Object-Centric Models for Planning with Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14418)
*Rodion Vakhitov,Leonid Ugadiarov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COMET，一种基于模型的强化学习算法，通过在槽结构潜在空间中进行蒙特卡洛树搜索，并引入动作-槽融合机制和对象因果注意力，在多个视觉和动态多样化的任务中实现了更快的训练收敛和更高的早期训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MuZero风格的潜在规划方法缺乏显式的对象级归纳偏置，难以有效处理包含多个对象的复杂视觉场景。作者旨在通过引入对象为中心的表示和机制，使决策过程能够集中于任务相关的实体，从而提高样本效率和早期训练性能。

Method: COMET结合了一个冻结的无监督对象为中心编码器和一个基于Transformer的世界模型。核心创新包括：(1) 动作-槽融合机制，将动作绑定到对象上用于槽转移预测；(2) 对象因果注意力机制，通过学习每个槽的相关性分数来调节token交互，使决策集中于任务相关实体。这种方法将显式的对象级归纳偏置引入MuZero风格的潜在规划中。

Result: 在来自Object-Centric Visual RL基准、ManiSkill、Robosuite和VizDoom的八个视觉和动态多样化任务中，COMET在训练早期阶段相比对象为中心和单体基线方法获得了更高的平均标准化分数。

Conclusion: 通过引入对象为中心的归纳偏置，COMET成功地将MuZero风格的潜在规划扩展到了多对象场景，显著提高了训练早期的性能，证明了对象级表示和注意力机制在视觉强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce COMET (Causal Object-centric Model for Efficient Tree search), a model-based reinforcement learning algorithm that performs Monte Carlo Tree Search in a slot-structured latent space. COMET pairs a frozen unsupervised object-centric encoder with a transformer-based world model, in which actions are bound to objects through a novel action-slot fusion mechanism that is used in slot transition prediction. Policy and value heads use object-causal attention, modulating token interactions by learned per-slot relevance scores so that decision-making concentrates on task-relevant entities. COMET adds an explicit object-level inductive bias to MuZero-style latent planning. Across eight visually and dynamically diverse tasks from the Object-Centric Visual RL benchmark, ManiSkill, Robosuite, and VizDoom, COMET achieves a higher mean normalized score during the early stages of training compared to object-centric and monolithic baselines.

</details>


### [61] [GitOfThoughts: Version-Controlled Reasoning and Agent Memory You Can Replay, Diff, and Merge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14470)
*Pavan C Shekar,Abhishek H S,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GitOfThoughts将LLM推理树存储为git仓库实现版本控制，但大规模实验表明：对新颖问题，任何记忆格式（包括git）都无法提升准确性；只有当检索案例与当前问题高度相似（>0.8）时准确性才显著提升，且这种增益源于答案检索而非方法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理过程是短暂的：思维链随上下文窗口消失、搜索分支无记录、记忆缓冲区无法差异化/合并/审计。其他复杂软件过程（代码、基础设施、数据、实验）都有版本控制，但推理没有。作者希望解决推理的可追溯性和审计问题，并探究记忆是否真能提升准确性。

Method: 1) 设计GitOfThoughts系统：将智能体的推理树存储为git仓库，每个评分思维是一个commit，分数是notes，结果是tags，检索通过"git log"实现；2) 进行大规模系统性研究：跨越五种记忆底物（无、markdown、向量、图、git）、两个基准测试、两种模型规模和预注册复现实验，检验记忆对准确性的影响。

Result: 1) 对新颖问题，没有记忆格式能可靠提升准确性；一个早期有希望的结果在预注册复现中失效；2) 发现"可复制性阈值"现象：当检索案例与当前问题相似度>~0.8时准确性急剧提升，低于此阈值则无效；3) 这种增益是答案检索而非方法迁移：4.5倍更大的模型能使近重复案例的收益翻倍，但仍无法从工作示例中提取可迁移方法；4) 唯一有效的通用杠杆是测试时采样；5) Git作为底物的价值在于审计性、溯源性和可合并性，准确性持平。

Conclusion: GitOfThoughts以近乎零工程成本实现了推理的可重放、可审计和跨智能体可合并。但研究结论令人清醒：记忆对新问题无益，只有高度相似案例才能提升性能，且这种提升来自答案复制而非方法学习。作者记录了一个撤回结果和一个被证伪假设，展示了严格的评估标准。Git版本控制的价值在于审计和溯源，而非准确性提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) reasoning is ephemeral: chains of thought vanish with the context window, pruned search branches leave no record, and memory buffers cannot be diffed, merged, or audited. Every other complex software process (code, infrastructure, data, experiments) is version-controlled; reasoning is not. We introduce GitOfThoughts, which stores an agent's reasoning tree as a git repository: every scored thought is a commit, scores are notes, outcomes are tags, and retrieval is "git log" over the agent's own history. This makes reasoning replayable, auditable, and mergeable across agents at near-zero engineering cost.
  We then ask the harder question: does memory, in any substrate, actually improve accuracy? Across five substrates (none, markdown, vector, graph, git), two benchmarks, two model scales, and pre-registered replications, the answer for novel problems is no. No memory format reliably helps, and a promising early result collapsed under its own pre-registered replication. Memory pays only above what we call the copyability threshold: when the retrieved case is a near-duplicate of the current problem (similarity >~ 0.8), accuracy jumps sharply; below it, nothing. The gain is answer retrieval, not method transfer: a 4.5x larger model doubles the near-duplicate payoff yet still cannot extract a transferable method from a worked example. The only general lever we find is test-time sampling. The case for git-as-substrate is therefore auditability, provenance, and mergeability at accuracy parity. We document a retracted result and a refuted hypothesis to model the evaluation standard we hold ourselves to.

</details>


### [62] [When the Tool Decides: LLM Agents Defer Blindly to Graph Neural Network Tools, and Stronger Backbones Defer More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14476)
*Zhongyuan Wang,Pratyusha Vemuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM代理在配备GNN工具时几乎完全顺从工具输出(97.6-99.2%一致率)，未能发挥判断力；这种顺从随模型能力增强而加剧，导致错失更优策略。选择性地调用工具需显式设计而非依赖规模涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设LLM代理在使用GNN工具时会发挥判断力，自主决定何时以及多大程度上依赖该工具。然而这一假设缺乏直接验证，作者旨在实证检验代理是否真正行使判断还是仅仅服从工具。

Method: 将一个冻结的GNN作为显式工具暴露给ReAct风格的LLM代理，在文本属性图(ogbn-arxiv和WikiCS)上进行节点分类任务。测量代理的预测与原始GNN预测的一致性，并在不同规模的backbone(Qwen2.5 0.5B-7B)上验证。设计per-node oracle以评估最优行动选择的性能上限，并测试简单的选择性调用门控机制。

Result: 代理与原始GNN预测一致率达97.6-99.2%，表现为"GNN鹦鹉"——全盘采用工具输出。顺从性随模型能力增强而上升(1.5B到7B从0.60增至0.98)。Per-node oracle比顺从策略在3B时高0.09-0.18，在7B时高0.12-0.22；在高同质性场景下差距约翻倍。在7B模型上，简单的邻居标签工具在高同质性时超越GNN(0.81 vs 0.71)，但代理仍顺从GNN。选择性调用门仅能恢复高同质性场景约一半的性能差距(0.71到0.83)，且无全局净增益。

Conclusion: LLM代理+工具系统的评估不能假设代理会在工具之上添加判断力；选择性调用机制必须显式设计，而非期望其从模型规模增长中涌现。可靠的选择性调用受限于可用信息，不仅仅是路由器设计问题。

Abstract: A growing line of work equips large language model (LLM) agents with graph neural networks (GNNs) as callable tools, assuming the agent exercises judgment over when and how much to rely on such a tool. We test this directly. We expose a frozen GNN to a ReAct-style LLM agent as an explicit tool and measure, on node classification over a text-attributed graph (ogbn-arxiv, replicated on WikiCS), whether the agent uses the tool or merely obeys it. We find the agent does not exercise judgment: its predictions agree with the raw GNN's 97.6-99.2% of the time (5 seeds), collapsing into a GNN parrot that adopts the tool's output wholesale and bypasses its own reasoning. Sweeping backbone capability (Qwen2.5 0.5B-7B), the deference is not a weak-model artifact: among models able to invoke the tool, agreement rises with capability (0.60 to 0.98 from 1.5B to 7B). Crucially, the cost of deference does not shrink as capability grows and grows where alternatives emerge: a per-node oracle over the available actions beats the parrot by 0.09-0.18 at 3B and 0.12-0.22 at 7B, roughly doubling at high homophily, because the parrot is pinned to the frozen GNN while the agent's alternatives improve; at 7B a simple neighbour-label tool overtakes the GNN at high homophily (0.81 vs 0.71) yet the agent still defers. A simple selective-invocation gate recovers about half of that high-homophily gap (0.71 to 0.83) but yields no net global gain, and held-out estimates bound the best achievable gate over standard test-time features to at most a third of the oracle headroom: reliable selective invocation looks limited by available information, not merely router design. Our results are a cautionary measurement: evaluations of agent+tool systems cannot assume the agent adds judgment on top of the tool, and selective invocation must be designed in rather than expected to emerge from scale.

</details>


### [63] [From Chatbot to Digital Colleague: The Paradigm Shift Toward Persistent Autonomous AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14502)
*Yongheng Zhang,Ziang Liu,Jiaxuan Zhu,Shuai Wang,Xiangqi Chen,Haojing Huang,Jiayi Kuang,Siyu Chen,Ao Shen,Hao Wu,Qiufeng Wang,Qian-Wen Zhang,Junnan Dong,Wenhao Jiang,Ying Shen,Hai-Tao Zheng,Yinghui Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文阐述了大语言模型从聊天机器人向数字同事的根本性转变，提出在认知核心层面从"快思考"进化到思考型LLM，在工具增强任务执行层面从临时调用工具的Agent发展为配备持久工作空间的OpenClaw工作站系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正在经历从对话生成器向集成AI系统（具备推理、行动、记忆和自我改进能力）的根本性转变，需要系统性地理解和组织这一转型过程。

Method: 沿着两个紧密耦合的维度组织转型：(1)认知核心层面，通过推理时计算、思维链推理、反思、过程监督和强化学习支持更审慎可靠的认知；(2)工具增强任务执行层面，采用"工作空间+技能"范式，通过状态持久化、可复用程序、任务闭环和经验重用实现类同事的工具使用。

Result: 识别出数据构建从指令-响应对向状态-动作-观察轨迹的转变，评估从静态基准向沙箱化、可审计、自演化AI生态系统的演进，实现了从临时工具调用向持续性工作范式的转变。

Conclusion: LLM正在从聊天机器人时代的"快思考"系统向数字同事转型，通过认知能力深化和工作站系统集成，实现了从对话式回答到持续性工作的根本性跨越。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are undergoing a fundamental transformation from conversational generators into integrated AI systems capable of reasoning, action, memory, and self-improvement. We conceptualize this transition as a shift from Chatbot to Digital Colleague: from conversational answers to persistent work. We organize this transition along two tightly coupled dimensions. First, at the cognitive core level, LLMs are advancing from Chatbot-era "fast thinking" systems driven by next-token prediction toward Thinking LLMs that leverage inference-time computation, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, reflection, process supervision, and reinforcement learning to support more deliberate and reliable cognition. Second, at the tool-augmented task execution level, LLMs are progressing from tool-calling Agents that invoke external resources in an ad hoc manner toward OpenClaw-style workstation systems (OpenClaw) equipped with persistent Workspaces, skills, verification loops, and governance. The "Workspace + Skill" paradigm makes episodic tool use colleague-like via state persistence, reusable procedures, task closure, and experience reuse. We examine data construction shifts from instruction-response pairs to State-Action-Observation trajectories and evaluation from static benchmarks to sandboxed, auditable, self-evolving AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [64] [Dense Coordinate-List Fine-Tuning Induces a Controllable Interference Surface in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14507)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Boguang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型微调生成密集坐标列表时出现的输出重复问题，发现高容量LoRA适配器会引发重复尾部压力，并提出了一种对象级别的重复停止机制，在保持性能的同时有效消除重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者发现微调视觉-语言模型生成密集坐标列表虽然提升了视觉定位能力，但同时也改变了模型序列化、重复和终止结构化输出的方式。这种生成和控制行为需要被深入理解和解决。

Method: 在Gemma 4 12B模型上使用高容量q/k/v/o LoRA进行实验，通过rank扫描（4-64）分析容量持久性，设计对象级别的重复停止机制，并使用结构轴探针定位效应。同时在不同模型（Qwen3-VL-8B）和数据集（COCO 2017）上进行验证。

Result: 高容量LoRA将类别感知F1@0.3从0.007提升至0.448，但引入重复问题（重复率0.080，最大重复23次）。对象级别重复停止机制成功消除重复（重复率0.000，最大重复1），同时保持F1性能（0.494→0.490）并改善F1@0.5（0.381→0.385）。效应定位在bbox坐标对象列表，其他结构保持清洁。

Conclusion: 密集坐标列表适应创建了一个结构绑定、跨模型家族的干扰表面，这种现象可以测量和控制。研究发现重复问题集中在bbox坐标对象列表，可以通过对象级别机制有效解决而不损失性能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models to emit dense coordinate lists improves visual grounding but also changes how models serialize, repeat, and terminate structured outputs. We study this behavior as a generation and control surface. In Gemma 4 12B, high-capacity q/k/v/o LoRA raises class-aware F1@0.3 from 0.007 to 0.448 while inducing repeated-tail pressure (duplicate rate 0.080, max repeat 23). A q/v rank sweep keeps max repeat at 21-22 across ranks 4-64, showing capacity persistence. The target signal is separable: object-level repeat-stop removes exact repeated records (duplicate rate 0.000, max repeat 1) while preserving F1 (0.494 to 0.490) and stricter F1@0.5 (0.381 to 0.385). Structure-axis probes localize the effect to bbox-coordinate object lists; dense non-bbox and spatial/count JSON remain repeat-clean, including under high-capacity adapters. Qwen3-VL-8B reproduces a clean controlled endpoint (F1@0.3 0.318, duplicate rate 0.000), and COCO 2017 reproduces acquisition plus duplicate pressure. Dense coordinate-list adaptation therefore creates a structure-bound, cross-family interference surface that can be measured and controlled.

</details>


### [65] [Every Eval Ever: A Unifying Schema and Community Repository for AI Evaluation Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14516)
*Jan Batzner,Sree Harsha Nelaturu,Anastassia Kornilova,Jon Crall,Tommaso Cerruti,Yanan Long,Yifan Mai,Sanchit Ahuja,Asaf Yehudai,Marek Šuppa,John P. Lalor,Oluwagbemike Olowe,Jatin Ganhotra,Brian H. Hu,Eliya Habba,Andrew M. Bean,Chang Liu,Sander Land,Steven Dillmann,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Elron Bandel,Saki Imai,James Edgell,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,Jenny Chim,Patrick Meusling,Asteria Kaeberlein,Venkata Ramachandra Karthik Chundi,Manasi Patwardhan,Martin Ku,Austin Meek,Leon Knauer,Brian Wingenroth,Srishti Yadav,Usman Gohar,Felix Friedrich,Michelle Lin,Jennifer Mickel,Arman Cohan,Stella Biderman,Irene Solaiman,Zeerak Talat,Anka Reuel,Mubashara Akhtar,Gjergji Kasneci,Avijit Ghosh,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Every Eval Ever，这是首个AI评估结果的共享模式和众包存储库，旨在解决评估结果格式不兼容、分散存储以及不同评估框架产生分歧分数的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估结果存在两大主要问题：一是结果以不兼容的格式保存在排行榜、论文、博客文章等多种来源中；二是不同评估框架产生的结果存在差异且元数据记录不一致，这阻碍了分析比较、跨社区评估科学发展、成本降低和重用。

Method: 引入Every Eval Ever系统，包括：(i)社区治理的元数据模式和实例级模式；(ii)从流行格式、评估工具和排行榜到统一模式的自动转换器；(iii)托管在Hugging Face上的众包社区数据库。该模式将评估标准化为统一的单一JSON文档，设计上具有源无关性，可从评估工具和论文中摄取结果。

Result: 成功构建了首个AI评估结果标准化的系统和数据库，目前数据库已涵盖22,235个模型、2,273个独特基准测试和31种评估格式，实现了评估结果的统一存储和管理。

Conclusion: Every Eval Ever为AI评估领域提供了首个标准化解决方案，通过统一的模式和社区众包的方式，有效解决了评估结果分散、格式不兼容和比较困难的问题，为AI评估科学的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: AI evaluations are widely used for testing and understanding progress. However, the diverse evaluators bring with them inconsistencies that challenge analysis and comparison. First, results are saved in incompatible formats, scattered across leaderboards, papers, blog posts, evaluation harness logs, and custom repositories. Second, results are created by different evaluation frameworks, which produce divergent scores for nominally identical evaluations and record metadata inconsistently, hindering comparison, cross-community evaluation science, cost reduction, and reuse. We introduce Every Eval Ever, the first shared schema and community-crowdsourced repository for AI evaluation results. The schema standardizes how evaluations are represented in a unified, single JSON document. It is source-agnostic by design, ingesting results from evaluation harnesses and papers alike, and optionally stores per-instance outputs for fine-grained analysis. We contribute: (i) a community-governed metadata schema with a companion instance-level schema, the first standardization effort of its kind; (ii) automatic converters from popular formats, evaluation harnesses, and leaderboards to the unified schema; and (iii) a crowdsourced community database hosted on Hugging Face, currently spanning to date 22,235 models, 2,273 unique benchmarks, and 31 evaluation formats.

</details>


### [66] [StreamMemBench: Streaming Evaluation of Agent Memory for Future-Oriented Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14571)
*Guanming Liu,Yuqi Ren,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Weihang Wang,Jiahao Liu,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StreamMemBench，一个针对个人智能体记忆的流式基准测试，用于评估智能体如何将观察到的证据和交互反馈转化为未来的辅助能力。实验表明，现有记忆系统难以有效利用观察到的证据或将反馈转化为可靠的后续行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆基准测试通常孤立地评估对话回忆或任务改进，而未充分测试从流式观察到后续辅助的完整轨迹。个人智能体的记忆核心作用是将存储的信息和先前的交互转化为面向未来的辅助能力，因此需要评估智能体如何利用观察线索和用户交互反馈来辅助未来相似任务。

Method: 作者提出StreamMemBench流式基准测试，基于EgoLife自我中心流构建两步任务序列：初始任务测试证据使用能力，后续任务测试反馈和交互经验的重用能力。该基准设计了四个评估指标：证据回忆、初始证据使用、反馈整合和后续重用，全面诊断记忆系统的各项能力。研究在两种骨干网络上测试了八种记忆系统。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前记忆系统即使在证据被存储或反馈被局部整合的情况下，仍经常无法有效利用观察到的证据或将其转化为可靠的后续行为。这表明现有系统在将观察证据和交互反馈转化为面向未来辅助能力方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个关键问题：尽管证据可能被存储或反馈被局部整合，现有记忆系统在将观察证据和交互反馈转化为可靠的后续行为方面存在根本性缺陷。StreamMemBench为评估和改进记忆系统提供了标准化平台，相关资源已公开发布以促进该领域研究发展。

Abstract: A central role of personal-agent memory is to turn stored information and prior interactions into future-oriented assistance. In daily use, useful cues come from what the agent observes and how the user interacts with the agent, and the agent must carry them forward from the current request to similar future tasks. Existing memory benchmarks usually test dialogue recall or task improvement in isolation, leaving the trajectory from streaming observations to later assistance largely untested. We introduce StreamMemBench, a streaming benchmark that constructs a two-step task sequence around each evidence anchor from EgoLife egocentric streams. The initial task tests evidence use, while the follow-up task tests whether feedback and interaction experience are reused. Four metrics diagnose evidence recall, initial evidence use, feedback incorporation, and follow-up reuse. Experiments with eight memory systems across two backbones show that current systems often fail to use observed evidence or turn feedback into reliable follow-up behavior, even when evidence is stored or feedback is incorporated locally. StreamMemBench is publicly available at https://github.com/landian60/StreamMemBench.

</details>


### [67] [VISTA: View-Consistent Self-Verified Training for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14579)
*Xinyu Qiu,Yunzhu Zhang,Heng Jia,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VISTA框架，通过多视角裁剪构建对比组来解决GRPO在GUI定位任务中因单一视角导致的样本优势缺失问题，在ScreenSpot-Pro上将Qwen3-VL各型号精度提升8-13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GRPO在GUI定位任务中从单一截图视角采样，导致困难样本组全失败或简单样本组全成功，组内无相对优势可供学习，限制了强化学习训练效果。

Method: 提出VISTA框架：1）对同一GUI实例进行保持目标的裁剪，生成语义等价但几何不同的多视角输入构建对比组；2）引入自验证跨视角锚点，使用优势加权损失优化oracle答案，仅当模型产生最大奖励时激活，避免退化为无条件模仿。

Result: 在五个GUI定位基准和多个Qwen骨干网络上持续提升精度。在ScreenSpot-Pro上，Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B分别从55.5/52.7/53.7提升至63.4/65.8/67.0。鲁棒性分析显示最差视角精度提升且预测翻转率降低。

Conclusion: VISTA通过多视角对比有效解决了GRPO在GUI定位中的训练不稳定性问题，显著提升了模型精度和跨视角鲁棒性，为GUI定位任务提供了一种有效的强化学习训练范式。

Abstract: When applying Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for GUI Grounding, rollouts are sampled from a single screenshot view; groups often become either all failures on difficult instances or all successes on easy ones, yielding no useful relative advantage. We propose VISTA (View-Consistent Self-Verified Training), a GRPO-based training framework that constructs each comparison group from multiple target-preserving views of the same GUI instance.Each view is generated by a crop that keeps the target element visible and remaps its box exactly, so model rollouts are compared across semantically equivalent but geometrically different inputs. To stabilize short coordinate generation without turning reinforcement learning into unconditional imitation, VISTA further adds a self-verified cross-view anchor: an oracle answer optimized with an advantage-weighted loss, excluded from the group baseline and activated only when the model has produced a maximum-reward rollout. Across five GUI-grounding benchmarks and multiple Qwen backbones, VISTA consistently improves grounding accuracy.On ScreenSpot-Pro, it raises Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B from 55.5/52.7/53.7 to 63.4/65.8/67.0. Robustness analyses further show higher worst-view accuracy and lower prediction flip rates.

</details>


### [68] [A Temporal Planning Framework for Disruption Aware Dynamic Route Optimization in Heterogeneous Railway Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14582)
*Pollob Chandra Ray,Sabah Binte Noor,Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对多轨距异构铁路网络，提出了一个基于时序规划的动态路径优化与中断管理框架，能够自动生成无冲突的操作计划并处理多种中断场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构多轨距铁路网络中，不同列车速度、停靠模式和基础设施兼容性约束增加了协调复杂性，特别是在单轨系统中。随机的中断事件（如轨道阻塞、列车故障、引擎故障和速度减缓）引入了额外的操作不可预测性，导致时刻表偏离。现有研究主要关注高层时刻表编排，忽略了轨道切换协调等操作细节，将决策留给人工操作员，增加了安全风险。

Method: 提出基于时序规划的框架，使用PDDL 2.1将铁路运营建模为时序规划问题，显式建模轨距兼容性约束和多样化的中断场景。框架生成无冲突的带时间戳操作计划，明确指定优化的调度和可执行的动作序列。使用包含200个实例（多达1,000个轨道点和120列火车）的基准问题集进行评估，采用两个最先进的时序规划器和一个计划验证器进行测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够有效地为异构铁路系统生成时序操作计划，处理多轨距约束和中断事件，减少对人工决策的依赖。框架在包含多达1,000个轨道点和120列火车的200个基准实例上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了异构铁路系统的动态路径优化和中断管理自动化，通过显式建模轨距兼容性约束和中断场景，生成可执行的操作计划，提高了铁路运营的安全性和可靠性，同时降低了人工决策的风险。

Abstract: Efficient route optimization play a vital role in ensuring both safety and punctuality in railway operations. It is very crucial particularly in heterogeneous multi-gauge railway networks with varying train speed, stopping pattern, infrastructure compatibility constraints increase coordination complexity. In single-track systems these challenges are further intensify due to all trains to share the same track and requires frequent track switching.Stochastic disruptions events including blocked tracks, blocked trains, engine failure and speed slowdowns introduces additional unpredictability in operations and deviate the timetable. However, existing studies predominantly focuses on high-level timetabling, omitting operational details such as track switching coordination. As a result leaving decision to human operators, increasing safety risks into railway operations. This study proposes a framework based on temporal planning for dynamic route optimization and disruption management in heterogeneous railway systems. The framework formulates railway operations as a temporal planning problem using PDDL 2.1 with explicitly modeling gauge compatibility constraints and diverse disruption scenarios. It generates conflict-free timestamped operational plans specifying both optimized schedules and executable action sequences. To evaluate the proposed framework, we developed a benchmark problem set with 200 instances using up to 1,000 track points and 120 trains. Two state-of-the-art temporal planners and a plan validator were employed to assessed the framework. The experimental results demonstrate that the framework effectively generates temporal operational plans for heterogeneous railway systems and handles multi-gauge constraints, disruptions, and reduces dependence on manual decision making.

</details>


### [69] [Abstracting Cross-Domain Action Sequences into Interpretable Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14654)
*Gaurav Verma,Scott Counts*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WorkflowView框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）将低层级行为日志抽象为高层级活动，有效解决了传统深度学习方法对噪声敏感和跨应用泛化能力差的问题，在任务描述重建、学生辍学预测和工作流分析等三个任务上均取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序交互日志虽然客观记录了数字应用的使用情况，但其粒度细和噪声大的特点往往掩盖了人们工作中有意义的洞察。这些洞察对于基于真实用户交互来改进数字产品至关重要。先前的深度学习聚类方法对噪声高度敏感，且难以跨应用泛化，亟需一种更稳健、更通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出WorkflowView框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）的语义理解能力，将低层级行为序列抽象为高层级活动描述。该方法无需大量训练数据，支持零样本和少样本学习场景，同时考虑了隐私保护和计算效率等实际部署问题。

Result: 在三个不同领域的挑战性任务上验证了方法的有效性：（a）浏览器日志零样本任务描述重建，语义相似度达0.91；（b）基于MOOC交互日志的少样本学生辍学预测，仅用5个示例即达到加权F1=0.90；（c）Microsoft Word中AI工具集成的匿名隐私保护工作流分析。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的抽象方法是转化低层级行为数据为高层级、可解释、可操作洞察的稳健高效途径，为日志基础设施中的实际部署提供了切实可行的方案，同时平衡了计算效率与用户隐私保护的需求。

Abstract: Sequential or time-stamped interaction logs provide objective records of digital application usage, yet their granularity and noise often obscure meaningful insights into people's work. Such insights are essential for improving digital products in ways grounded in real-world user interactions. Prior research has applied deep learning models to cluster user actions into high-level activities, but these approaches are highly sensitive to noise and struggle to generalize across applications. To address this limitation, we introduce WorkflowView, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to abstract low-level action sequences into high-level activities. We establish the effectiveness and generality of our approach across three distinct, challenging sequential tasks and diverse domains: (a) zero-shot task description reconstruction from browser logs (achieving high semantic similarity, $μ_{sim} = 0.91$), (b) few-shot student dropout prediction using MOOC interaction logs (reaching weighted $F_1 = 0.90$ with only five few-shot examples), and (c) anonymized, privacy-preserving analysis of AI tool integration within document workflows in Microsoft Word. Our work demonstrates that LLM-based abstraction is a robust and efficient path forward for transforming low-level behavioral data into high-level, interpretable, and actionable insights. We also discuss practical considerations for deploying LLM-based inferences within logging infrastructures, including computational efficiency and user privacy.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Direct Latent-Space Synthesis for Parallel Branches in LLM-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14672)
*Shikun Liu,Mufei Li,Dongqi Fu,Haoyu Wang,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Parallel-Synthesis框架，通过直接消费并行worker代理的KV缓存而非拼接文本来实现高效合成，在7/9个数据集上匹配或超越文本合成方法，并将首token时间减少2.5-11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理系统通过串行文本接口消费上下文，但现代结构化代理工作流中存在独立的并行分支（探索子任务、检索证据、生成候选方案），现有系统通过拼接文本输出合并分支会丢失并行结构并导致冗余预填充计算，存在接口不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Parallel-Synthesis即插即用框架：1）缓存映射器校准独立生成的分支KV缓存；2）微调合成器适配器使其能从非顺序缓存接口生成；使用三类数据训练：暴露合成器于并行缓存上下文、教授跨缓存分支聚合、从标准文本拼接合成中蒸馏推理行为。

Result: 在数学、科学问答、代码生成、GAIA和多代理数据库诊断等9个下游数据集上，7个数据集匹配或超越基于文本的合成方法，其余2个接近；首token时间减少2.5-11倍。

Conclusion: 直接基于缓存的合成是更原生且高效的并行代理分支合成接口，具有实际应用前景。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as execution engines for agentic systems, yet they still consume context through a sequential text interface. This creates a mismatch with modern structured agent workflows, in which independent branches explore subtasks, retrieve evidence, or generate candidate solutions before a final synthesis step. Existing systems typically merge these branches by concatenating their textual outputs, which discards the parallel structure and incurs redundant prefill computation. In this work, we introduce Parallel-Synthesis, a plug-and-play framework that enables a synthesizer to directly consume the KV caches produced by parallel worker agents. Parallel-Synthesis combines a cache mapper that calibrates independently generated branch caches with a fine-tuned synthesizer adapter that enables generation from this non-sequential cache interface. We train Parallel-Synthesis using data that exposes the synthesizer to parallel cache contexts, teaches aggregation across cached branches, and distills reasoning behavior from standard text-concatenation-based synthesis. Across nine downstream datasets spanning math, science QA, code generation, GAIA, and multi-agent database diagnosis, Parallel-Synthesis matches or outperforms text-based synthesis on seven datasets and remains close on the other two. It also reduces time-to-first-token by 2.5x-11x, suggesting that direct cache-based synthesis is a promising interface for more native and efficient synthesis over parallel agent branches.

</details>

### [71] [Can Editing 1 Neuron Fix Repetition Loops in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13705)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Aman Sharma,Dylan Bates,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gemma 4指令调优模型在长事实枚举任务中存在严重的重复循环问题。研究发现这些问题可追溯到少量MLP神经元（或MoE专家），通过权重编辑（如单神经元符号反转）可有效消除循环，同时保持基准性能，但无法解决因知识缺失导致的更深层次"末日循环"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gemma 4指令调优模型在长事实枚举提示（如列举电视剧集、星座、宝可梦等）中表现出严重的重复循环失败模式，发生率高达95%，且在提示重述、推理引擎更改和采样调整后仍然存在。研究者希望探究这种行为是否足够局部化，从而可以通过权重编辑来移除。

Method: 使用逐层消融（per-layer ablation）和逐神经元归因（per-neuron attribution）技术定位循环行为的因果源，通过全生成扫描确认最强候选，然后使用静态权重编辑抑制识别出的少量MLP神经元（或MoE模型中的路由专家）。编辑规模从单神经元符号反转到更大范围不等。

Result: 循环模式可追溯到少量MLP神经元或路由专家，通过小规模权重编辑（如E2B模型中单神经元符号反转）可有效消除，且能保持通用基准分数。编辑规模随模型增大而增长，但在所有情况下都能在正常生成预算内解决循环问题。对于更长思考预算下的"末日循环"（非收敛自校正循环），编辑可减少但无法完全消除该问题。

Conclusion: 研究证明了具体的生成病理可以被定位到少量参数并通过编辑移除的可行性，同时也划定了该方法的边界：权重手术可以删除循环，但无法提供缺失的事实。残留的失败本质上是知识精度问题而非可移除的电路问题，这揭示了模型编辑技术的能力范围和局限。

Abstract: Yes. Can it cure doom loops? Probably not.
  The Gemma 4 instruction-tuned models share a reproducible failure: on long factual enumeration prompts, such as listing every episode of a TV series, the 88 IAU constellations, or the 151 original Pokemon, they collapse into repetition, either a tight verbatim loop or a list whose entries decay onto a single answer. These loops occur at rates as high as 95% and survive prompt rewording, inference-engine changes, and most sampling adjustments. In this paper we explore whether this behavior is localized enough to remove by weight edits. To localize the cause, we use per-layer ablation and per-neuron attribution, then confirm the strongest candidates with full-generation sweeps. The loops trace to a small set of MLP neurons (or, in the 26B-A4B Mixture-of-Experts model, a few routed experts) which we suppress with static weight edits. These "surgeries" can be as small as a single sign-inverted neuron (in the E2B model). The size of the effective edits grows with model scale, but in all cases, the loop patterns can be addressed at normal generation budgets while preserving general-purpose benchmark scores. However, the edits do not solve everything: we also study longer thinking budgets, where the two larger models most visibly enter doom looping, i.e. a non-convergent regime in which the model self-corrects in circles over a fact it cannot recall, exhausting the budget without committing to a final answer. We show this residual failure is reduced but not eliminated by the same edits, and argue it is fundamentally a knowledge-precision problem rather than a removable circuit; weight surgery can delete a loop, but it cannot supply a missing fact. Our results are both a feasibility demonstration, that is, evidence that a concrete generation pathology can be localized to a few parameters and edited out, and a delineation of where that approach stops.

</details>


### [72] [Efficient On-Device Diffusion LLM Inference with Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13740)
*Tuowei Wang,Yanfan Sun,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了llada.cpp，首个面向智能手机NPU的扩散大语言模型(dLLM)推理加速框架，通过多块推测解码、双路径渐进修正和交换优化内存运行时三项技术，将LLaDA-8B的生成延迟相比CPU基线降低了17-42倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)通过并行去噪多个token来加速生成，在延迟敏感的移动推理场景中具有吸引力，但重复去噪过程在智能手机上引入了巨大的计算开销。虽然移动神经处理单元(NPUs)提供高吞吐量的密集矩阵计算，但高效利用它们面临三个挑战：token提交导致每块有效工作负载缩小、token修订使KV缓存复用复杂化、有限的NPU可见地址空间导致昂贵的重映射和数据传输开销。

Method: 提出llada.cpp框架，通过三项技术将块级dLLM推理与移动NPU的执行特性对齐：(1) 多块推测解码：用推测的未来块token填充当前块解码后期的缩小工作负载；(2) 双路径渐进修正：保持已提交token可修订直到稳定，并通过CPU端路径刷新不稳定token而不阻塞密集NPU执行；(3) 交换优化内存运行时：压缩NPU可见地址布局，将数据暂存与NPU计算重叠以减少重映射和传输开销。

Result: 将llada.cpp实现为端到端框架，在多种硬件平台和dLLM工作负载上进行评估。在支持前缀KV缓存复用的情况下，llada.cpp将LLaDA-8B的生成延迟相比CPU基线降低了17-42倍，同时保持了生成质量。

Conclusion: llada.cpp成功解决了移动NPU上dLLM推理的关键挑战，实现了显著的延迟降低，证明了NPU感知优化对移动端扩散大语言模型推理加速的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate generation by denoising multiple tokens in parallel, making them attractive for latency-sensitive mobile inference. However, repeated denoising introduces substantial computation on smartphones. Mobile neural processing units (NPUs) offer high-throughput dense matrix computation, but efficiently exploiting them remains challenging: token commitment shrinks per-block effective workloads, token revision complicates KV cache reuse, and limited NPU-visible address space incurs costly remapping and data transfer overheads.
  In this paper, we propose llada.cpp, the first NPU-aware inference framework for accelerating dLLMs on smartphones. llada.cpp aligns block-wise dLLM inference with the execution characteristics of mobile NPUs through three techniques. (1) Multi-Block Speculative Decoding fills the shrinking workload in late-stage current-block decoding with speculative future-block tokens. (2) Dual-Path Progressive Revision keeps committed tokens revisable until stable and refreshes unstable tokens through a CPU-side path without stalling dense NPU execution. (3) Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime compacts NPU-visible address layouts and overlaps data staging with NPU computation to reduce remapping and transfer overheads. We implement llada.cpp as an end-to-end framework and evaluate it across diverse hardware platforms and dLLM workloads. llada.cpp reduces LLaDA-8B generation latency by 17x-42x over the CPU baseline with prefix KV cache reuse, while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [73] [High-Frequency Pricing at Scale for E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13741)
*Stefan Birr,Tobias Huelden,Mones Raslan,Adele Gouttes,Andreas Schmitt,Mateusz Koren,Johannes Stephan,Robert Streek,Manuel Kunz,Tim Januschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对时尚电商促销活动的“预测-优化”定价算法，通过日度需求预测和多目标优化框架，将定价决策时间从小时级缩短至分钟级，在Zalando的23项A/B测试中实现约6%的利润提升，并已成功部署至生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚电商促销活动面临需求波动剧烈、定价决策需快速响应、需平衡短期营收与长期利润等独特挑战；现有的周粒度系统存在决策时效性不足等局限，难以满足促销期间海量商品（超过500万件）的高效定价需求。

Method: 采用“预测-优化”架构：首先使用梯度提升树模型进行日分辨率需求预测，随后构建多目标优化框架同时最大化长期利润和净商品价值（NMV）。该系统取代了原有的人工-算法混合模式，实现了自动化快速决策。

Result: 在2023-2024年Zalando的促销活动中，跨越12个市场进行了23项A/B测试验证。新定价系统相比原方案实现约6%的利润提升，同时在销量和营收方面保持同等表现，定价决策时间从小时级降至分钟级。

Conclusion: 该算法已成功部署至生产环境，目前负责Zalando促销活动的大部分算法定价决策，验证了该方法在工业场景的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and implementation of a specialized forecast-then-optimize algorithmic pricing tool for sales campaigns in fashion e-commerce. Sales events present unique challenges for pricing including volatile demand patterns, rapid pricing decisions, and the need to balance short-term revenue with long-term profitability. We describe our approach combining daily-resolution demand forecasting using gradient-boosted trees with a multi-objective optimization framework that maximizes both long-term profit and net merchandise value for more than 5 million articles. Our solution addresses key limitations of existing weekly-granularity systems by implementing a forecast-then-optimize architecture that reduces pricing decision time from hours to minutes. We validate our approach through 23 A/B tests across 12 markets during 2023-2024 sales campaigns at Zalando, one of Europe's leading online fashion retailers. Experimental results demonstrate that the new pricing system achieves approximately 6% higher profit while maintaining equivalent performance on sales and revenue compared to the previous manual-algorithmic hybrid approach. Based on these results, the algorithm was successfully deployed to production and now handles the majority of algorithmic pricing decisions for sales campaigns at the company.

</details>


### [74] [A fully GPU-based workflow for building physics emulators of hypersonic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13742)
*Fabian Paischer,Dylan Rubini,Deniz A. Bezgin,Aaron B. Buhendwa,David Hauser,Florian Sestak,Johannes Brandstetter,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Nikolaus A. Adams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于GPU的全流程框架，结合可微分高保真求解器(JAX-Fluids)与神经模拟器，通过残差优化实现了对高超声速流场的精确预测，解决了传统降阶模型难以捕捉激波等陡峭梯度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速流动中激波位置和强度的精确预测对工程设计至关重要，但传统降阶模型和神经模拟器难以在保持物理一致性的前提下捕捉流动状态的陡峭梯度，限制了其在工业应用中的可靠性。

Method: 开发了一个完全基于GPU的工作流程，集成了可微分高保真求解器(JAX-Fluids)用于快速数据集生成，并训练带有不确定性量化和物理感知优化的神经模拟器；采用基于残差的优化方法增强物理一致性。

Result: 残差优化方法能够在仅有网格和输入参数的情况下进行训练，大幅降低残差并改善物理一致性；不同iable仿真与残差优化的结合使物理模拟器在训练分布之外仍保持可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功构建了能够在训练分布外保持可靠性的物理模拟器，满足了在真实工程设计循环中部署代理模型的关键要求，为高超声速流动等复杂物理现象的高效高保真模拟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ability to resolve complex physical phenomena with high fidelity and at low computational cost is central to addressing key challenges in modern engineering. A prime example lies in hypersonic flows, where the precise prediction of the full flowfield topology, in particular with respect to shock wave location and intensity, is critical. Yet supersonic and hypersonic flows continue to be a stumbling block for traditional reduced-order models and neural emulators that struggle to capture steep gradients in flow states with physical consistency in applications of industrial relevance. To that end, we introduce a fully GPU based workflow that integrates accelerated data generation with the training of neural emulators augmented by uncertainty quantification and physics-aware refinement. Our workflow is enabled by a differentiable high-fidelity solver (JAX-Fluids) which we employ for rapid dataset creation and residual-based improvement of the neural emulator to enhance physical consistency. Building on this framework, we first present a suite of model architectures and analyze their scaling behavior to expose their strengths and shortcomings. We then show that residual-based refinement enables training on cases where only mesh and input parameters are available, substantially reducing residuals and improving physical consistency. Together, differentiable simulation and residual-based refinement yield physics emulators that remain reliable beyond their training distribution, a key requirement for deploying surrogates in real-world engineering design loops.

</details>


### [75] [Contract-Based Compositional Shielding for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14130)
*Omar Adalat,Edwin Hamel-De le Court,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在去中心化执行下实现多智能体安全协调的方法，通过同时认证的局部LTL安全义务元组，在不牺牲端到端安全性的前提下恢复团队最优安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体强化学习中，当全局安全无法由任何智能体单方面强制执行时会出现安全协调问题：一个智能体动作的可接受性可能取决于其他智能体的动态。现有去中心化防护机制通过纯分解权限往往排除了仅通过协调才能实现的最优团队安全行为，因此需要在去中心化执行约束下恢复团队最优安全行为。

Method: 智能体共享线性时序逻辑安全片段(LTL_safe)的全局规范φ，从局部LTL安全义务元组中进行选择，这些元组的合取蕴含全局规范φ。每个智能体可依赖其他智能体的局部义务作为假设，因为整个契约元组被同时认证并允许投影到局部动作掩码。在学习阶段，采用非平稳多臂老虎机在局部LTL安全义务库中选择优化团队奖励的元组，同时保持端到端安全性。

Result: 该方法在6个环境和15种算法变体上进行了评估验证，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了去中心化执行下的多智能体安全协调问题，在无需集中式运行时控制的情况下恢复了团队最优安全行为，实现了安全性保证与最优团队性能的统一。

Abstract: Safe coordination problems surface in multi-agent reinforcement learning when global safety cannot be enforced by any agent unilaterally: the admissibility of one agent's action may depend on the dynamics of other agents. Decentralised shields can enforce safety at runtime, but purely factorised permissions often exclude optimal team behaviour that is safe only through coordination. We study deterministic safety guarantees for agents trained and deployed under decentralised execution, recovering team-optimal safe behaviour without centralised runtime control. Agents have a shared global specification $φ$ in the safety fragment of Linear Temporal Logic ($\mathsf{LTL}_{\mathsf{safe}}$ ), and select among tuples of local $\mathsf{LTL}_{\mathsf{safe}}$ obligations whose conjunction implies the global specification $φ$. Each agent may rely on the other agents' local obligations as assumptions because the whole contract tuple is certified simultaneously and allows projection into local action masks. At learning time, a non-stationary multi-armed bandit chooses among a library of local $\mathsf{LTL}_{\mathsf{safe}}$ obligations to select the tuple that optimises team reward, all without forgoing end-to-end safety. We evaluate the approach across 6 environments and 15 algorithmic variants.

</details>


### [76] [FedSPC: Shared Parameter Correction for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13748)
*Kannanthodath Induchoodan Ajay Menon,Christian Prehofer,Yunfei Xu,Toru Hirano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedSPC方法，通过对共享参数应用控制变量校正来解决个性化联邦学习中共享参数更新不一致的问题，在多个数据集和模型上提升了现有PFL方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习方法将模型分为共享参数和个性化参数进行联合训练，但共享参数会被优化不同本地目标的客户端更新，导致共享更新不一致，从而削弱共享表示能力。

Method: 提出联邦共享参数校正(FedSPC)方法，这是一种模块化校正方法，仅对给定PFL方法的共享参数应用控制变量校正，保持个性化参数不变。该方法可集成到三种常见PFL设置：共享特征提取器、共享分类器以及带本地正则化的全共享模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上使用ViT、ResNet-34和VGG-11模型进行实验，FedSPC在FedPer、FedRep、FedBABU、LG-FedAvg和Ditto等代表性PFL方法上均取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: FedSPC通过针对性的共享参数校正有效解决了PFL中的优化不一致问题，作为一种模块化方法可以灵活集成到多种PFL设置和方法中，具有广泛的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is one of the important approaches in federated learning for addressing statistical heterogeneity while enabling client-specific adaptation. Many PFL methods split the model into shared and personalized parameters, which are jointly trained on each client. However, this creates an optimization issue: shared parameters are updated by clients optimizing different local objectives, which can lead to inconsistent shared updates and weaken the shared representation. To address this problem, we propose Federated Shared Parameter Correction (FedSPC), a modular correction method for PFL. FedSPC applies control-variate correction only to the shared parameters of a given PFL method, while leaving personalized parameters unchanged. It can be integrated into three common PFL settings: shared feature extractors, shared classifiers, and fully shared models with local regularization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet with ViT, ResNet-34, and VGG-11 show that FedSPC improves performance across representative PFL methods, including FedPer, FedRep, FedBABU, LG-FedAvg, and Ditto.

</details>


### [77] [The Weight Norm Sets the Grokking Timescale: A Causal Delay Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13753)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Doan Hoang Viet,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过干预训练过程中的权重范数，证实了权重范数是导致Grokking延迟的关键因素：自由训练时网络在达到临界范数Wc时发生Grokking，而固定范数时延迟呈指数规律增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象中权重范数是否导致泛化延迟存在争议：有研究报道在转换点存在临界范数，也有研究观察到Grokking时没有固定范数。现有研究仅观察权重范数而非主动干预，因此需要通过干预实验来明确权重范数在Grokking延迟中的因果作用。

Method: 采用干预而非仅观察的方法：1）自由训练设置下观察权重范数变化；2）将权重范数固定在临界值Wc的rho倍并保持不变，观察对Grokking延迟的影响；3）在四个模数上进行实验验证；4）通过添加LayerNorm验证权重尺度与网络函数解耦的效果。

Result: 自由训练时网络在权重范数达到Wc时发生Grokking，Wc在不同种子和学习率间变异系数仅1-2%，且随模数基呈幂律增长。固定范数时延迟T_grok与exp(αρ)成正比，单一指数α≈7.5在四个模数上拟合优度R²=0.996。权重范数对延迟的影响约为19倍，而学习率仅影响约2倍。LayerNorm通过解耦权重尺度和网络函数消除了这种依赖关系。

Conclusion: 权重范数确实是导致Grokking延迟的关键因素。固定范数延迟呈指数规律，是自由训练中对数延迟的指数对应形式。将权重范数保持在Wc以上会减缓而非阻止Grokking，这为理解神经网络泛化的动态过程提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Grokking is the delayed onset of generalization in neural networks, arising long after they fit the training data. Whether the weight norm causes this delay is disputed: some studies report a critical norm at the transition, others observe grokking with no fixed norm at all. We settle this by intervening on the norm during training rather than only observing it. Under free training with weight decay, networks grok when the weight norm reaches a value Wc that varies little across seeds and learning rates (CV 1 to 2 percent) and grows with the modular base as a power law. When we instead clamp the norm to a fixed multiple rho of Wc and hold it there, the network still groks, but the delay follows T_grok proportional to exp(alpha rho). One exponent, alpha near 7.5, fits this delay across four moduli (R^2 = 0.996). Over the swept ranges the held norm moves the delay by about 19x and the learning rate by only about 2x, and holding the norm above Wc slows grokking rather than preventing it. A final LayerNorm removes the dependence by decoupling weight scale from the network function; without it the exponential law returns. This pinned-norm delay is the exponential counterpart to the logarithmic delay predicted for a freely contracting norm.

</details>


### [78] [D2H-AD: A Hybrid Model Utilizing Hyperdimensional Computing for Advanced Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13754)
*Ghazal Ghajari,Elaheh Ghajari,Ashutosh Ghimire,Saeid Ataei,Faris Alsulami,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D2H-AD，一种基于超维度计算(HDC)的轻量级异常检测框架，在基准数据集上优于五种基线方法，适用于资源受限和实时应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习和深度学习方法在异常检测中依赖大量标注数据、计算成本高，且在边缘和高维场景中面临可扩展性挑战。

Method: 提出D2H-AD框架，基于超维度计算范式，将基于距离的相似性和密度感知编码集成到统一框架中，使用高维分布式向量表示信息。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上验证，D2H-AD在F1-score和ROC-AUC上均优于HDAD、ODHD、One-Class SVM、Isolation Forest和Autoencoders五种基线；仅超维编码即可使ROC-AUC提升高达5.4%；对类别不平衡、噪声和数据复杂性具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D2H-AD具有轻量级、可解释、计算高效、内存占用小和低延迟等特性，特别适合TinyML和边缘AI部署，展示了HDC在动态环境中实现准确、可解释且节能的异常检测的潜力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a fundamental component of intelligent systems with applications in healthcare, cybersecurity, smart grids, and IoT environments. Although conventional machine learning and deep learning methods have demonstrated effectiveness in identifying anomalies, they often rely on large labeled datasets, incur high computational costs, and face scalability challenges in edge and high-dimensional settings. This paper presents D2H-AD, a novel anomaly detection framework based on Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), a brain-inspired paradigm that represents information using high-dimensional distributed vectors. Unlike existing HDC-based methods, D2H-AD integrates distance-based similarity and density-aware encoding within a unified framework, improving anomaly representation and detection performance. Ablation studies show that hyperdimensional encoding alone yields up to 5.4% higher ROC-AUC than applying the same density-distance scoring directly in the original feature space. Furthermore, D2H-AD consistently outperforms five established baselines, namely HDAD, ODHD, One-Class SVM, Isolation Forest, and Autoencoders, across all evaluated datasets. The framework is lightweight, interpretable, and computationally efficient, making it suitable for resource-constrained and real-time applications. We validate D2H-AD on five benchmark datasets and demonstrate superior F1-score and ROC-AUC performance, together with robustness to class imbalance, noise, and data complexity. In addition to improved accuracy, D2H-AD offers scalability, a small memory footprint, and low-latency operation enabled by binary computations and a compact design. These properties make it particularly attractive for TinyML and edge AI deployments. The proposed framework highlights the potential of HDC for accurate, interpretable, and energy-efficient anomaly detection in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond LoRA: Is Sparsity-Induced Adaptation Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13767)
*Elijah Cadenhead,Cristian McGee,Xin Li,El Houcine Bergou,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cheap LoRA (cLA)及其变体，通过在LoRA中引入稀疏性（固定一个低秩因子）来实现更高效、更低成本的参数高效微调，在保持竞争力的性能同时减少训练时间和GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA及其变体提供了内存和计算高效的微调方案，但关于这些方法的泛化能力比较以及低秩更新的结构限制如何保持有效的适应性能仍存在疑问。现有方法在效率与性能之间的权衡仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出两类方法：(1) Cheap LoRA (cLA)：训练单个低秩因子而固定另一个（确定性或随机化变体），将其框架化为非对称LoRA的结构化实例；(2) 链式循环变体c³LA。推导了信息论泛化误差上界。在10个预训练模型、14个数据集上评估了11种微调方法，使用损失景观和谱分析等工具分析性能和泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，尽管微调模型对预训练模型、数据集等因素敏感，将基于LoRA的PEFT方法的适应限制在稀疏、结构化的列空间中，在任务上仍与参数匹配的基线保持竞争力，同时减少高达10%的训练时间和15%的峰值GPU内存（即使使用非优化的稀疏实现）。理论和实证泛化指标提供了比常用分析工具更一致、更有原则的成本效益适应评估方法。

Conclusion: 通过在现有LoRA变体中引入稀疏性，可以在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低计算成本和内存需求。理论推导的泛化误差边界和实证分析为参数高效微调方法提供了更可靠的评估框架，证明了结构化、稀疏的列空间限制是一种有效的成本-性能权衡策略。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants provide a memory- and compute-efficient alternative to full fine-tuning of pre-trained models. However, questions remain about the comparative generalizability of these approaches and how the structural restrictions on low-rank updates preserve effective adaptation performance. We present a historical framing, covering the past (full fine-tuning and original LoRA), the present (different variants of LoRA), and propose simpler, cheaper, parameter-efficient extensions by inducing sparsity within existing LoRA variants: Cheap LoRA (cLA), training a single low-rank factor with the other fixed (deterministically or, in its randomized variant, stochastically), and the chained circulant variant, ${c}^3$LA. We frame cLA as a structured instance of asymmetric LoRA, serving as a controlled column-subspace restriction of full fine-tuning. We derive information-theoretic generalization error bounds for these variants, marking one of the first endeavors in this area. Empirically, we evaluate 11 fine-tuning methods across 10 pre-trained models and 14 datasets, analyzing the fine-tuned models' performance and generalization using tools such as loss landscapes and spectral analysis. Despite the sensitivity of fine-tuned models to the pre-trained model, datasets, and other factors, our study suggests that restricting LoRA-based PEFT methods' adaptation to a sparse, structured column space remains competitive across tasks with their parameter-matched baselines while reducing up to 10% training time and peak GPU memory up to 15%, even with a naïve, non-optimized, sparse implementation. Our theoretical and empirical generalization measures provide a more consistent and principled approach to their cost-effective adaptation than commonly used analytical tools. Overview and code are available at: https://elicaden.github.io/Beyond_LoRA/.

</details>


### [80] [Diffusion Policy Optimization without Drifting Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13795)
*Haozhe Jiang,Haiwen Feng,Pieter Abbeel,Jiantao Jiao,Angjoo Kanazawa,Nika Haghtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiPOD框架，通过在策略梯度更新中添加on-policy ELBO正则化器来解决扩散策略后训练中的不稳定问题，实现了更稳定的训练和更高的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略梯度方法在强化学习后训练中存在不稳定性问题，无法实现可靠的策略改进。作者识别出这是由于"双漂移现象"导致的：优化变分代理会使ELBO与真实对数似然分离，从而导致代理策略梯度与期望回报的真实策略梯度错位。

Method: 提出DiPOD（Diffusion Policy Optimization）框架，通过交替进行自蒸馏和策略改进梯度更新来在整个训练过程中保持紧边界行为。具体算法为：在每个扩散策略梯度更新中添加on-policy ELBO正则化器。

Result: 在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制扩散策略实验中，DiPOD显著稳定了训练过程，并达到了比以往方法更高的奖励值。

Conclusion: DiPOD成功解决了扩散策略后训练中的不稳定性问题，通过保持ELBO与真实对数似然的紧密联系，实现了可靠的策略改进，为扩散策略的强化学习后训练提供了一个简单实用的算法。

Abstract: RL post-training has become increasingly pivotal for improving diffusion policies, but existing diffusion policy-gradient methods are often unstable and cannot achieve reliable policy improvement. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion policy optimization framework that maintains tight-bound behavior throughout training by interleaving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. This leads to a simple and practical algorithm: augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient update with an on-policy ELBO regularizer. Across diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control diffusion policies, DiPOD substantially stabilizes training and reaches higher rewards than previous methods.

</details>


### [81] [Neural Variability Enhances Artificial Network Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13801)
*Robin Preble,Praveen Venkatesh,Stefan Mihalas,Kameron Decker Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现人工神经网络中具有结构化的相关噪声能够显著提升模型对对抗攻击和自然图像变化的鲁棒性，为构建更稳健的神经网络提供了一种生物学上合理的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑皮层神经元对重复刺激的反应存在显著的试次间变异性，而外周感觉神经元的反应则更加一致，这引发了关于随机性是否具有功能意义的思考。已有研究表明噪声和信号相关性可能有助于动物的辨别能力，人工神经网络研究也显示了噪声在机器学习任务中的益处，但大多数研究忽视了相关性的影响。本文旨在探究相关噪声是否能提高人工神经网络对对抗攻击和自然图像变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 研究者通过比较网络在修改输入（对抗攻击或自然图像变化）与干净输入下激活值的协方差来分析噪声结构。实验中系统性地评估了不同类型的噪声结构对网络鲁棒性的影响，包括自然图像变化和不同类型的对抗攻击，并考察了噪声结构在不同修改类型之间的迁移能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，结构化噪声能够显著提高网络的鲁棒性。对于自然图像变化的鲁棒性，结构化的噪声带来的益处最大，但这种结构在不同修改类型之间的迁移能力较差。相比之下，从对抗攻击中学习的噪声结构可以泛化到其他类型的攻击。这些发现表明，人工神经网络激活中的结构化噪声普遍能够提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了结构化噪声作为提高人工神经网络鲁棒性的有效手段，该方法仅依赖局部信息，具有生物学上的合理性，为创建更加鲁棒的人工神经网络提供了一种新的策略。这一发现不仅深化了对神经网络噪声功能性的理解，也为设计更具生物启发性的鲁棒网络架构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Neural responses in cortex exhibit substantial trial-to-trial variability in response to repeated stimuli, while peripheral sensory neurons respond far more consistently, leading many to wonder whether stochasticity may carry meaning. Existing work has argued that noise and signal correlations may be optimized for discrimination in animals, whereas artificial neural network (ANN) studies have shown similar benefits of noise in machine learning tasks, although most ANN work has neglected the effects of correlations. Here we investigate whether correlated noise improves the robustness of artificial neural networks to adversarial attacks and naturalistic image modifications. Using the covariance of activations under modified versus clean inputs, we find that structured noise may significantly improve network robustness. Robustness to naturalistic image modifications benefits most from structure, but this structure transfers poorly across modification types. In contrast, noise structure from adversarial attacks can generalize to other kinds of attacks. These results suggest that structured noise in ANN activations generally improves robustness, establishing a biologically plausible strategy for creating robust artificial neural networks that only relies on local information.

</details>


### [82] [Neural Slack Variables for Shape Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13803)
*Ruben Wiedemann,Antoine Jacquier,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经松弛变量方法，通过联合学习辅助网络将函数不等式约束（如单调性、凸性）转化为回归问题，实现了约束的严格满足，并在金融波动率曲面的无套利学习中取得突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业和科学应用中，神经网络强制执行函数不等式约束（如单调性和凸性）是一个基础性挑战。传统方法存在缺陷：单边惩罚方法和原始-对偶方法仅在约束违规处提供梯度，导致约束满足脆弱；而保证可行性的架构方法主要局限于简单情况且引入额外归纳偏置。

Method: 引入神经松弛变量（neural slack variables），这是一种深度学习原生的原始侧方法。该方法通过将主网络与联合学习的辅助网络耦合，将约束强制转化为回归问题。辅助网络作为主网络约束量的有效目标，诱导可行性和正则性。

Result: 在密集网格单调性和凸性测试用例上，神经松弛变量实现了零测量违规，而惩罚和原始-对偶基线方法仍存在残留违规。此外，该方法成功实现了波动率曲面的无套利学习，解决了量化金融中的一个开放工业挑战。

Conclusion: 神经松弛变量方法克服了传统约束执行方法的局限性，提供了一种有效且通用的深度学习约束满足框架，具有重要的理论和实践价值，特别是在需要严格约束满足的工业应用中。

Abstract: Enforcing functional inequality constraints such as monotonicity and convexity in neural networks is a fundamental challenge in many industrial and scientific applications. Classical one-sided penalty methods, along with primal-dual methods gated by complementary slackness, provide constraint gradients only at violated locations, resulting in fragile satisfaction. Architectures that guarantee feasibility by construction, on the other hand, remain largely limited to elementary cases and impose additional inductive biases. We introduce neural slack variables, a deep learning native primal-side approach that converts constraint enforcement into a regression problem by coupling the primary network with a jointly learned auxiliary network. The auxiliary network serves as a valid target for the primary network's constraint quantities, inducing feasibility and regularity. Neural slack variables achieve zero measured violations on dense-grid monotonicity and convexity test cases, where penalty and primal-dual baselines leave residual violations, and enable arbitrage-free learning of volatility surfaces, an open industrial challenge in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [83] [Uncertainty Estimation and Generalization Bounds for Modern Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13818)
*Luis A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文从贝叶斯视角研究深度学习的泛化与不确定性量化问题。方法论上提出DVIP框架及VaLLA、FMGP两种后验方法；理论上构建统一概率框架，通过多样性、平滑性和随机性三个机制解释过参数化神经网络的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络具有卓越的预测性能，但其泛化能力和不确定性量化机制仍未被充分理解。这一理论空白限制了对深度学习系统的可靠应用和进一步改进，亟需从概率视角建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 方法论层面：(1)提出深度变分隐过程(DVIP)，将隐过程扩展至深度架构的可扩展贝叶斯框架；(2)开发变分线性化拉普拉斯近似(VaLLA)和固定均值高斯过程(FMGP)两种后验方法，为预训练确定性网络提供校准的不确定性估计。理论层面：构建统一概率框架，融合贝叶斯推断、函数空间建模和大偏差理论，利用PAC-Bayesian理论分析泛化机制。

Result: 成功建立了连接贝叶斯推断、函数空间建模和大偏差理论的统一概率视角。方法贡献包括可扩展的DVIP框架以及两种有效的后验不确定性量化方法。理论贡献在于识别并统合了多样性、平滑性和随机性三个关键机制，为过参数化神经网络的泛化能力提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯原理能够有效深化对现代深度学习系统的理解。通过将方法论创新与理论分析相结合，本论文不仅提供了实用的不确定性量化工具，还为理解过参数化神经网络的泛化机制建立了坚实的理论基础，推动了深度学习从经验成功向理论可解释性的转变。

Abstract: This thesis investigates how Bayesian principles can deepen our understanding of modern deep learning systems. While neural networks achieve remarkable predictive performance, their ability to generalize and to quantify uncertainty remains only partly understood. This thesis approaches this challenge from both methodological and theoretical angles: unifying Bayesian inference, function-space modeling, and large-deviation theory under a common probabilistic perspective.
  On the methodological side, the thesis introduces the Deep Variational Implicit Process (DVIP), a scalable Bayesian framework that extends implicit processes to deep architectures. Complementing this, two post-hoc methods -- the Variational Linearized Laplace Approximation (VaLLA) and the Fixed-Mean Gaussian Process (FMGP) -- are proposed to equip pretrained deterministic networks with calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  The theoretical contributions focus on one of the central open questions in modern machine learning: why do large, over-parameterized neural networks generalize so well? To address this, the thesis develops a unified probabilistic framework that connects three key mechanisms -- diversity, smoothness, and stochasticity -- within the language of PAC-Bayesian and large-deviation theory.

</details>


### [84] [Attention-Based Estimation of the Individual Treatment Benefit Probability under Dose Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13821)
*Lev V. Utkin,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Stanislav K. Kogan,Natalya M. Verbova,Maksim I. Goriunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Dose-AIPTB框架，将个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计从二元治疗扩展到多离散剂量水平，通过将问题重构为个体治疗效应符号的二元分类，利用协变量相似的成对比较构建伪标签，并通过注意力机制聚合，为个性化剂量选择提供了基于个体获益概率的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计方法主要局限于二元治疗场景，但临床实践中剂量变化干预非常普遍。因此需要开发能够处理多离散剂量水平的IPTB估计方法，为临床医生提供更直观的个体化治疗决策依据。

Method: 提出Dose-AIPTB框架，将IPTB估计问题转化为对未观测个体治疗效应符号的二元分类问题。通过协变量相似的成对比较构建伪标签，并使用注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归进行聚合。该方法自然地适应多个离散剂量水平，突破了二元治疗的限制。

Result: 在协变量偏移、不同样本量和异质性结果条件下，通过真实世界和合成数据的数值实验表明，基于注意力的聚合方法持续优于核回归替代方案，验证了该方法在多种场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于个体获益概率的个性化剂量选择奠定了基础，扩展了IPTB估计的应用范围，代码已公开发布，为临床个性化治疗决策提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: Estimating the probability that a treatment outperforms a control for an individual patient, called the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB), offers a clinically intuitive alternative to population-average metrics. However, existing methods for IPTB estimation are largely confined to binary treatment settings, despite the prevalence of dose-varying interventions in clinical practice. We propose a general framework for IPTB estimation with ordinal outcomes under discrete dose assignments, called Dose-AIPTB (Dose Attention-based IPTB). Our approach recasts the problem as binary classification over the unobserved sign of the individual treatment effect, constructing pseudo-labels from covariate-similar pairwise comparisons and aggregating them via attention mechanisms or Nadaraya-Watson kernel regression.
