This formulation naturally accommodates multiple discrete dose levels, extending beyond the binary treatment paradigm. Through numerical experiments on real-world and synthetic data under covariate shift, varying sample sizes, and heterogeneous outcomes, we demonstrate that attention-based aggregation consistently outperforms kernel alternatives. The framework provides a foundation for personalized dose selection grounded in individual-level benefit probabilities. Codes implementing the model are publicly available at https://github.com/NTAILab/AIPTBDose.

</details>


### [85] [A Stationarity-and-Coupling Criterion for Training-Free Time-Lagged Spectral Embeddings of Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13823)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需训练的多变量时间序列描述符D(τ)，更重要的是建立了一个可证伪的适用性判据，通过预检验预测该方法是否适用于特定数据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望不仅评估无训练描述符的性能，更要明确其适用的边界条件。现有方法缺乏对描述符适用范围的先验判断，这导致在不适用的场景中盲目使用而失败。作者试图建立一个操作性的预检验准则，在应用前就能预测描述符是否能工作。

Method: 构建D(τ)描述符：基于时滞相关矩阵，在Marchenko-Pastur边缘截断以保留信号特征值，通过余弦相似度与类别中心分类，零学习参数。从平稳高斯VAR(1)模型推导适用性判据，要求信号近似平稳且类别信息存在于跨通道时间耦合中，而非边际功率。设计两部分预检验：增强Dickey-Fuller平稳性检验和功率基线饱和检验。

Result: 在满足判据的四个数据集（Sleep-EDF、BCI-IV-2a、MIT-BIH、ESC-50）上，D(τ)以极低成本达到与强基线相当的竞争力，在Sleep-EDF上达到88.5±4.5%准确率。在违反判据的三个场景（非平稳ERP、功率区分的金融波动性和可穿戴压力数据）中，方法如预测般失败，这些负面结果更具信息价值。

Conclusion: D(τ)的价值不在于最高精度，而在于提供了一个紧凑、无训练的嵌入表示，其有效域可事先预知。预检验准则成功预测了方法的适用性，验证了理论推导的正确性，为无训练描述符的实际应用提供了可靠的事前指导。

Abstract: We study training-free fixed-length descriptors for multivariate time series and ask not merely whether such a descriptor performs well, but when it can be expected to work at all. Our object of study is $D(τ)$, built from a time-lagged correlation matrix truncated at the Marchenko-Pastur edge so that only signal-bearing eigenvalues survive and classified by cosine similarity to class centroids with zero learned parameters. The central contribution is not the descriptor but a falsifiable applicability criterion for it. Working from a stationary Gaussian VAR(1) model, we argue that $D(τ)$ separates two classes when the signals are approximately stationary and the class information lives in their cross-channel temporal coupling rather than in marginal per-channel power. We derive, semi-formally, three consequences: a distinguishability condition, why the static ($τ=0$) covariance collapses to chance, and why a stationary but power-discriminated paradigm defeats the descriptor. The criterion is operational: a two-part pre-flight test -- an augmented Dickey-Fuller stationarity check and a power-baseline saturation check -- predicts applicability before any training. We validate both halves on a mixed assortment. On four paradigms that satisfy the criterion (Sleep-EDF, BCI-IV-2a, MIT-BIH, ESC-50) the descriptor is competitive with strong baselines at a fraction of their cost, reaching $88.5\pm4.5\%$ under 20-subject leave-one-subject-out on Sleep-EDF on a single CPU thread. On three that violate it -- non-stationary ERPs, and financial-volatility and wearable-stress regimes that are power-discriminated -- it fails exactly as the pre-flight predicts, and these negatives are the more informative half. We are explicit that $D(τ)$ is not the most accurate representation; its value is a compact, training-free embedding whose domain of validity is known in advance.

</details>


### [86] [SuperThoughts: Reasoning Tokens in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13862)
*Zheyang Xiong,Shivam Garg,Max Yu,Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Haoyu Zhao,Anastasios Kyrillidis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperThoughts通过将连续CoT token对压缩为潜在表示并使用MTP模块每次解码两个token，在训练时保留离散监督信号，实现了约20-30%的CoT长度缩减，同时仅导致1-2个百分点的精度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维(CoT)推理虽能提升LLM问题解决能力，但顺序token生成带来高昂计算成本。现有连续潜在空间推理方法存在训练不稳定性和缺乏监督信号的问题，难以扩展到复杂长时序任务。

Method: 提出SuperThoughts框架：(1)将连续CoT token对压缩为单个潜在表示；(2)通过轻量级多token预测(MTP)模块每步解码两个token；(3)引入基于置信度的自适应机制，不确定时回退至标准解码。该方法在训练时保留离散token监督信号，推理时吞吐量翻倍。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math系列模型(1.5B/7B/14B)上进行微调，在MATH500、AMC、OlympiadBench和GPQA-Diamond基准上评估。SuperThoughts实现约20-30%的CoT长度缩减，同时保持准确率，大多数任务仅有1-2个百分点的精度下降。

Conclusion: SuperThoughts成功平衡了计算效率与推理质量，通过压缩token对和双token解码机制，在大幅降低推理长度的同时保持了模型性能，为高效CoT推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves LLM problem-solving but is computationally expensive due to sequential token generation. While recent works explore reasoning in continuous latent spaces to bypass discrete token generation, they often struggle with training stability and fail to scale to complex, long-horizon tasks due to lack of supervision signal. We propose SuperThoughts, which compresses pairs of consecutive CoT tokens into single latent representations and decodes two tokens per step via a lightweight Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module. This preserves discrete token supervision at training time while doubling throughput at inference time. We finetune Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-14B-Instruct, and evaluate on MATH500, AMC, OlympiadBench, and GPQA-Diamond. With a confidence-based adaptive mechanism that falls back to standard decoding when uncertain, SuperThoughts achieves $\sim$20--30\% CoT length reduction while maintaining accuracy with minimal degradation (1-2 points accuracy drop on most tasks).

</details>


### [87] [Muon$^p$: Muon with Fractional Spectral Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13867)
*Yihe Dong,Will Sawin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Muon^p优化器，通过分数谱幂更新在Muon和梯度下降之间插值，在十亿级模型微调任务上显著提升了性能，并从理论上证明了其最优性和计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过用极因子UV^⊤替换梯度G=USV^⊤来展平奇异值谱，但这种完全展平会丢弃对自适应可能重要的奇异值信息。作者希望保留部分奇异值信息，在Muon和梯度下降之间找到更好的平衡点。

Method: 提出Muon^p优化器，使用分数谱幂更新US^pV^⊤（有理数p∈(0,1)），实现Muon和梯度下降之间的插值。理论证明分数谱幂无法通过任何固定单变量多项式迭代计算，推导出仅使用矩阵乘法的低阶奇双变量递推公式来近似US^pV^⊤，保持了Muon的计算复杂度结构。

Result: 理论方面：证明Muon^p在Schatten q-范数（q=1+1/p）下最大化损失的线性改进。实验方面：在十亿级模型微调任务上，Muon^p显著改善了验证困惑度和下游任务性能。通过谱几何视角分析了Muon^p的不适用场景。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了保留奇异值谱何时能带来显著增益的重要见解，并提供了一种有原则的方法来实现这些增益，为优化器设计提供了新的理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Muon is an increasingly widely used optimizer that replaces a gradient $G=USV^\top$ with its polar factor $UV^\top$, thereby flattening the singular spectrum. However, full flattening discards singular-value information that may matter for adaptation. We introduce Muon$^p$, a Muon-style optimizer that instead uses fractional spectral-power updates $US^pV^\top$ for rational $p\in(0,1)$, interpolating between Muon and gradient descent. To make it practical, we prove that fractional spectral powers cannot be computed by any fixed univariate polynomial iteration, and furthermore derive low-degree odd bivariate recurrences that approximate $US^pV^\top$ using only matrix multiplications, preserving Muon's matrix-multiplication-only structure and compute complexity. We show that Muon$^p$ maximizes the linear improvement in loss under the Schatten $q$-norm for $q=1+\frac{1}{p}$. Empirically, Muon$^p$ is especially effective for finetuning: on billion-scale models, Muon$^p$ improves validation perplexity and downstream task performance. We further analyze when Muon$^p$ is less suitable, through the lens of spectral geometry. Our results reveal important insights on when preserving the singular spectrum can bring significant gains, and introduce a principled way to achieve them.

</details>


### [88] [Natively Unlearnable Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13873)
*Gaurav R. Ghosal,Pratyush Maini,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NULLs模型，通过共享主干神经元和稀疏激活的sink池，实现源特定贡献隔离与跨源联合学习的统一，支持高效精确的源级遗忘且保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘旨在移除特定训练数据源的影响，但因不同源的贡献在模型中纠缠，难以分离和移除。既需隔离各源贡献便于遗忘，又需跨源联合学习共享信息，二者目标冲突，现有方法难以兼得。

Method: 设计NULLs（原生可遗忘大语言模型）：训练一组共享主干神经元和一个稀疏激活的sink池。训练时，源特定信息自然集中到该源对应的sink中，跨源共享信息则积累到主干中。遗忘时仅禁用对应sink，无需梯度更新或访问保留数据。

Result: 在Wikipedia约600万篇文章规模验证，每篇作为独立源。遗忘单篇文章可移除其特定知识，保留与语义相关文章共享的事实，效果接近从头重训练。遗忘Harry Potter书籍的案例中，NULLs对对抗性提取和逆转遗忘的重学习均具鲁棒性。下游基准测试中通用语言能力与标准Transformer相当。

Conclusion: 源级遗忘不必作为事后补救，可原生嵌入LLM训练过程，实现源贡献隔离与共享表示学习优势兼顾，为可追溯、可审计、可遗忘的大模型训练提供新范式。

Abstract: Unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data sources, but this has proved challenging because the contributions of different sources are entangled within the model. Isolating source contributions to disjoint parameters makes removal easier, though it obstructs joint learning across sources. We propose NULLs (Natively Unlearnable LLMs), a model class that satisfies the two opposing goals of isolating source-specific contributions and learning jointly across sources, by training a set of shared backbone neurons alongside a pool of sparsely activated sinks. During training, information specific to a source naturally concentrates in its sinks while information shared across sources accumulates in the backbone. A source is then unlearned at deployment by disabling its corresponding sinks, with no gradient updates and no access to the retained data. We show that NULLs scales to Wikipedia's ~6M articles, isolating each as an independent source. Unlearning a single article removes knowledge specific to it while preserving facts shared with semantically related articles, closely matching retraining from scratch. We note that unlearning with NULLs is also robust: in a case study of unlearning the Harry Potter books, NULLs resists both adversarial extraction and relearning that reverses post-hoc unlearning. Finally, NULLs preserves general language capabilities, matching a standard transformer on downstream benchmarks. Together, these results suggest that source-level unlearning need not be an afterthought. It can be built natively into LLM training while retaining the benefits of shared representation learning.

</details>


### [89] [A Longitudinal Attribute-Conditioned Neural Network for Modeling Health-State Transition Probabilities in Temporally Irregular Data: The LANTERN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13880)
*Bright Kwaku Manu,Beckett Sterner,Petar Jevtic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对不规则纵向健康数据的多状态转移概率估计器，用于长期护理保险定价和准备金评估，在严重残疾判别和转移矩阵误差方面优于传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的精算多状态模型（如马尔可夫、半马尔可夫或比例风险模型）在处理具有非线性老化模式和不规则观测间隔的纵向健康数据时存在局限性，且难以充分利用异质性协变量历史信息，因此需要开发更灵活、校准更准确的转移概率估计方法。

Method: 开发了一种结构化机器学习估计器，能够学习个体健康历史，纳入观测间隔时间，并以人口统计学和社会经济属性为条件，生成包含健康、轻度残疾、重度残疾和死亡四个状态的有效概率分布，然后按年龄组和起始状态聚合个体概率形成与精算队列投影兼容的转移矩阵。

Result: 使用HRS纵向数据，与逻辑回归、梯度提升树、循环神经网络和持续基准模型对比，该方法在严重残疾判别能力上优于逻辑回归和梯度提升树，保持良好的校准性，并在留出测试集中产生最低的聚合转移矩阵误差。

Conclusion: 结构化机器学习估计器能够在校准性和投影保真度方面支持长期护理转移建模，证明了其在精算应用中的价值不仅限于判别能力，还能提供可靠的转移概率估计。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of long-term care transition probabilities is central to disability insurance pricing, reserving, and solvency assessment. Classical actuarial multi-state models commonly rely on Markov, semi-Markov, or proportional-hazard specifications, which provide a direct connection to cohort projection but may be restrictive for irregular longitudinal health data with nonlinear aging patterns and heterogeneous covariate histories. This paper develops a well-calibrated estimator of multi-state transition probabilities for irregular longitudinal health data. The model learns from individual health history, incorporates the time elapsed between observations, and conditions transition probabilities on demographic and socioeconomic attributes. It produces a valid probability distribution over the next observed health state, with four possible states: healthy, mild disability, severe disability, and death. Individual probabilities are aggregated by age group and origin state to form transition matrices compatible with actuarial cohort projection. Using longitudinal data from the Health and Retirement Study, we compare the proposed estimator with logistic regression, gradient-boosted trees, a recurrent neural network, and a last-state persistence benchmark. The evaluation considers probabilistic accuracy, endpoint discrimination and calibration for severe disability and death, risk concentration, and transition matrix error after aggregation. The proposed estimator improves severe disability discrimination relative to logistic regression and gradient-boosted tree benchmarks, maintains strong calibration, and yields the lowest transition matrix error among the evaluated models in the held-out test analysis. Results show that a structured machine learning estimator can support long-term care transition modeling when judged by calibration and projection fidelity, beyond discrimination.

</details>


### [90] [Gefen: Optimized Stochastic Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13894)
*Nadav Benedek,Tomer Koren,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gefen，一种内存高效优化器，通过在参数块间共享二阶矩估计并使用学习码本量化一阶矩，将AdamW的内存占用降低约8倍，同时保持相同的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW作为现代深度学习的默认优化器，其一阶和二阶矩状态需要增加约两个参数大小的缓冲区，导致训练内存消耗巨大。理论分析表明，大型混合Hessian矩阵元素将平方梯度的比值约束为1，这意味着Hessian对齐的参数是共享二阶矩统计量的天然候选者。

Method: Gefen通过两个核心技术实现内存优化：(1)基于初始平方梯度推断块结构，自动在参数块间共享二阶矩估计；(2)使用学习的直方图动态规划量化码本对一阶矩进行量化，并重用相同的块进行一阶矩缩放。该方法不需要架构特定的元数据或超参数。

Result: 在多样化实验中，Gefen实现了在保持AdamW级别性能的同时，达到比较的AdamW类方法中最低的峰值优化器内存。每十亿参数减少约6.5 GiB内存占用。在FSDP和DDP训练中，减少的内存占用使得更大的microbatch成为可能，显著提升了相对于AdamW的吞吐量。

Conclusion: Gefen提供了一个实用的AdamW直接替代方案，具有更低的内存使用量，可以提高吞吐量、支持训练更大模型或使用更大批次大小，且无需架构特定调整或额外超参数设置。

Abstract: AdamW is a default optimizer for modern deep learning, but its first and second moment states add roughly two parameter-sized buffers to training memory. We propose Gefen, a memory-efficient optimizer that automatically shares second-moment estimates across parameter blocks and quantizes the first moment using a learned codebook, thereby reducing AdamW's memory footprint by ~8x while maintaining the same performance, corresponding to a reduction of 6.5 GiB per billion parameters. The method is motivated by a theoretical result showing that large mixed Hessian entries constrain the ratio of squared gradients toward one, suggesting that Hessian-aligned parameters are natural candidates for sharing second-moment statistics. Since computing Hessians is impractical at scale, Gefen infers block structure from the initial squared gradients, requiring no architecture-specific metadata or hyperparameters beyond AdamW defaults. Gefen learns an exact histogram-based dynamic-programming quantization codebook and reuses the same blocks for first-moment scaling. Across diverse experiments, Gefen achieves the lowest peak optimizer memory among the compared AdamW-like methods while maintaining AdamW-level performance. In FSDP and DDP training, the reduced memory footprint enables larger microbatches and improves throughput significantly over AdamW, providing a practical drop-in replacement with lower memory usage that can increase throughput and enable training larger models or using larger batch sizes. We provide the complete Python implementation, including fused CUDA kernels at https://github.com/ndvbd/Gefen

</details>


### [91] [SpikF-GO: Spiking Fourier Graph Operators for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13901)
*Jafar Bakhshaliyev,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpikF-GO方法，首次将图结构引入脉冲神经网络进行多变量时间序列预测，通过频谱域的稀疏频率选择和复数LIF门控机制，在保持预测精度的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的时间序列预测方法独立处理各变量，缺乏对变量间依赖关系的显式建模机制。在多变量场景中，跨变量相关性包含重要的预测信息，这一局限性严重影响了模型的预测能力。

Method: 提出SpikF-GO方法：1)构建超变量图结构，将每个标量观测值作为图节点；2)引入Hard Concrete频率门实现可学习的稀疏频率选择；3)设计Complex LIF门控，对傅里叶实部和虚部分别应用独立的脉冲神经元，在频谱域保持二值事件驱动计算；4)提出集成中央模式发生器位置编码的变体以增强长程时序建模。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上的统一实验协议下，SpikF-GO在所有SNN方法中获得最佳平均排名，以更低能耗超越其ANN对应模型FourierGNN。即使嵌入维度显著缩小，仍保持有竞争力的精度，实现显著的能耗降低。

Conclusion: 首次将基于图的多变量建模引入脉冲神经网络时间序列预测领域，并首次在统一实验协议下对SNN预测架构进行系统比较，证明了SNN在多变量时序预测任务中兼具高效性和准确性的潜力。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as an energy-efficient alternative to conventional neural networks, demonstrating strong performance in computer vision and robotics. More recently, SNNs have been applied to time series forecasting (TSF), with methods exploring spiking temporal backbones, spike-compatible positional encodings, Fourier-domain processing, and redesigned neuron dynamics. However, existing SNN forecasting approaches process variables independently, lacking explicit mechanisms for modeling inter-variable dependencies. This is a critical limitation in multivariate settings, where cross-variable correlations carry substantial predictive information. We propose Spiking Fourier Graph Operators (SpikF-GO), which addresses this gap by combining a hypervariate graph formulation in which every scalar observation becomes a graph node with spike-driven spectral processing. SpikF-GO introduces a Hard Concrete frequency gate for learnable sparse frequency selection and a Complex LIF gate that applies independent spiking neurons to real and imaginary Fourier components, preserving binary, event-driven computation throughout the spectral domain. We further present a variant incorporating Central Pattern Generator-based positional encodings for stronger long-range temporal modeling. Evaluated on eight benchmarks under a unified experimental protocol, SpikF-GO achieves the best average rank among all SNN methods and outperforms its ANN counterpart, FourierGNN, at reduced energy cost. SpikF-GO maintains competitive accuracy even at substantially smaller embedding dimensions, thereby achieving significant energy reductions. To our knowledge, this is among the first works to bring graph-based multivariate modeling into the spiking domain for TSF and the first to provide a unified comparison across SNN forecasting architectures under a common experimental protocol.

</details>


### [92] [Smoothing Dark Areas in Molecular Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13955)
*Xi Wang,Jiahan Li,Yuxuan Xia,Yingcheng Wu,Shaoyi Zheng,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopVAE，一种拓扑优化的变分自编码器，通过在训练中内化结构和化学约束来减少潜在空间中的“黑暗区域”，显著提升潜在扩散模型在3D分子生成任务中的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子变分自编码器(VAE)通常通过重建目标训练，无法保证潜在空间在后验样本之外保持平滑、有效和可导航，导致产生"黑暗区域"——这些区域在扩散采样过程中可达，但解码后产生断开或化学无效的分子。与图像生成不同，分子解码需要严格的结构和化学精确度，即使很小的潜在扰动也可能导致灾难性失败。

Method: 提出TopVAE（拓扑优化VAE），通过使解码器在训练过程中内化结构和化学约束来减少黑暗区域，从而在解码阶段就能保证分子的有效性，消除了测试时化学修正的需求。该方法专注于优化潜在空间的拓扑结构，确保生成分子的结构连通性和化学有效性。

Result: TopVAE显著提升了后验外鲁棒性。与标准DiT结合后，在QM9数据集上FCD-3D降低77%，V&C指标达到最高；在GEOM-Drugs数据集上FCD-3D降低52%；在零样本支架修复任务上，生成稳定且连通的分子数量提升1.29倍。

Conclusion: TopVAE成功解决了潜在扩散分子生成中的黑暗区域问题，通过在训练阶段内化化学约束而非依赖测试时修正，实现了更鲁棒和高质量的3D分子生成，为可扩展的3D分子生成提供了一个更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Latent diffusion is a promising framework for scalable 3D molecular generation, but it requires a latent space that remains smooth, valid, and navigable beyond posterior samples. Existing molecular VAEs, however, are typically learned through reconstruction-based objectives, which do not guarantee such a latent space. We show that this leads to dark areas: regions of latent space that are reachable during diffusion sampling but decode to disconnected or chemically invalid molecules. Unlike in image generation, molecular decoding requires strict structural and chemical precision, so even small latent perturbations can produce catastrophic failures. We therefore propose TopVAE, a topology-optimized VAE that reduces dark areas by making the decoder internalize structural and chemical constraints during training, eliminating the need for test-time chemical correction. TopVAE greatly improves off-posterior robustness, and when paired with a standard DiT, achieves $77\%$ lower FCD-3D on QM9, the highest V&C, $52\%$ lower FCD-3D on GEOM-Drugs, and $1.29{\times}$ more stable and connected molecules on zero-shot scaffold inpainting.

</details>


### [93] [Can Machine Learning Forecast Rice Yields in Data-Constrained Settings? Satellite Climate Data, National Crop Statistics, and Lessons from Sierra Leone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13959)
*Ibrahim Denis Fofanah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次将机器学习应用于塞拉利昂作物产量预测，发现仅靠作物统计无法超越基准模型，但结合免费卫星气候数据后，XGBoost模型将预测误差降低了三分之一，且早期季节降雨是主导预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塞拉利昂农业部门缺乏数据驱动的决策支持，且尚无机器学习研究关注该国的作物产量。本研究旨在探索利用该国现有数据预测水稻产量的可行性，以填补研究空白并支持国家农业政策制定。

Method: 使用FAOSTAT 2000-2024年间九种主要作物的25年生产数据，在严格的反泄露协议下，采用扩展窗口前向验证法（七个保留年）训练XGBoost、梯度提升和随机森林模型，以朴素持续性模型为基准进行对比；随后整合免费卫星气候数据（CHIRPS降雨、NASA POWER温度）进行模型增强。

Result: 仅使用作物统计数据的模型无法超越基准模型；加入卫星气候数据后，纯气候XGBoost模型将预测误差降低三分之一（RMSE 284 vs 428 kg/ha），该结果在线性模型中同样成立且对排除2018年异常季节具有鲁棒性；早期季节（5-6月）降雨为主导预测因子，但没有任何模型预测到2018年的产量崩溃（其根源为制度性因素）。

Conclusion: 研究表明免费卫星气候数据可显著提升塞拉利昂水稻产量预测能力，季节性产量风险在收获前数月即可观测；研究据此为塞拉利昂"Feed Salone Strategy"提供了政策建议，并建立了完全开源的数据处理流程。

Abstract: Sierra Leone's agriculture operates with almost no data-driven decision support, and no published machine learning study has examined the country's crop yields. We ask whether rice yield can be forecast from data Sierra Leone currently has. Using 25 years of FAOSTAT production data (2000-2024) for nine major crops, we train XGBoost, Gradient Boosting, and Random Forest under a strict anti-leakage protocol with expanding-window walk-forward evaluation across seven held-out years, benchmarked against naive persistence. No model trained on crop statistics alone outperforms persistence. Augmenting with free satellite climate data (CHIRPS rainfall, NASA POWER temperature) reverses this result: a climate-only XGBoost reduces forecast error by one third (RMSE 284 vs 428 kg/ha), a gain that holds for a linear model and is robust to excluding the anomalous 2018 season. Early-season (May-June) rainfall is the dominant predictor, implying seasonal yield risk is observable months before harvest. No model anticipated the 2018 collapse, whose origins were institutional rather than climatic. We translate the findings into policy recommendations for Sierra Leone's Feed Salone Strategy, with a fully open-source pipeline.

</details>


### [94] [PostDeg: Placement Beats Parameterization in LayerNorm GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14022)
*Yash Tomar,Aryav Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm会消除节点选择策略所需的拓扑信号，确定了消除发生在LayerNorm之前的位置，提出了在LayerNorm后插入逆度缩放(PostDeg)的无参数方法，在三个任务上平均提升+3.9%，证明了位置比参数化更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LayerNorm的图神经网络(GNN)会常规性地擦除节点选择策略应该依赖的拓扑信号（如度、中心性、k-core），但现有文献尚未定位这种擦除在残差块中的具体发生位置。作者旨在回答这一根本问题。

Method: 通过理论分析定位到：在LayerNorm前插入的正标量会被除掉（仅剩稳定项），而在LayerNorm后插入的相同标量能以表示幅值形式到达评分头。基于此发现，作者提出PostDeg——一种无参数的LayerNorm后逆度缩放方法，并设计了四个证伪器（图级标量、额外LayerNorm、同槽容量表达、主干无关来源）来验证规则的普适性。

Result: PostDeg在影响力最大化、网络拆解和最大独立集三个任务上分别获得+3.5%/+2.5%/+5.6%的提升，每任务10/10配对种子全胜。四个证伪器均未触发，验证了规则的稳健性。

Conclusion: 核心发现是：位置而非参数化决定了性能增益——这是一个适用于任何规范化残差栈中任何正拓扑标量的小型不变性检验。该工作为GNN中拓扑信号的保留提供了理论指导和简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LayerNorm-based GNNs routinely erase the topology signals (degree, centrality, $k$-core) that node-selection policies should depend on, but the literature has not located where in the residual block the erasure happens. We answer that question: a positive per-node scalar inserted before LayerNorm is divided out up to a stabilizer term, while the same scalar inserted after LayerNorm reaches the score head as representation magnitude. The surviving slot is the post-LayerNorm position. We instantiate it with PostDeg, a parameter-free post-LayerNorm inverse-degree scale, and pre-register four falsifiers (graphwise scalars, extra LayerNorm, expressive same-slot capacity, backbone-agnostic source) that would reject the rule. PostDeg gains $+3.5\%/+2.5\%/+5.6\%$ over the LN backbone on influence maximization, network dismantling, and maximum independent set, with $10/10$ paired-seed wins per task; none of the four falsifiers fires. The takeaway is that placement, not parameterization, carries the gain -- a small invariance check that generalizes to any positive topology scalar in any normalized residual stack.

</details>


### [95] [Utility-Constrained Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14029)
*Mehrdad Moghimi,Bernardo Avila Pires*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对效用约束MDP(UCMDPs)的实用解决方案，解决了传统CMDPs不支持风险敏感约束的问题，在Safety Gymnasium基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDPs)虽然被广泛用于强化学习的安全性约束，但存在两个关键缺陷：(1)不支持风险敏感约束，可能导致最优解混合罕见灾难性事件和频繁过度保守行为；(2)先前的经验结果表明，即使最终进行风险中性评估，采用更严格的风险敏感约束反而能提升性能。效用约束MDP(UCMDPs)是引入风险敏感约束的自然框架，但此前缺乏实用解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种简单而强大的方法来解决UCMDPs和约束强化学习问题。该方法的核心优势是：(1)支持风险敏感约束；(2)不需要在训练智能体之前固定约束限制，只需知道合理的范围即可，这增加了策略灵活性；(3)允许在不产生额外训练成本的情况下调整约束限制。

Result: 在多个Safety Gymnasium基准任务中，该方法始终匹配或超越现有基线方法的性能。得益于框架的通用性，智能体在实际应用中表现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了UCMDPs缺乏实用解决方案的空白，提供的框架既支持风险敏感约束，又具有训练前无需固定约束限制的灵活性。实证结果表明该方法在安全性强化学习任务中具有实用价值和优越性能。

Abstract: Constrained MDPs (CMDPs) are a widely adopted framework for incorporating safety into RL agents; however, the framework does not support risk-sensitive constraints. This can be problematic: For example, CMDPs allow for optimal solutions that, in order to satisfy the risk-neutral constraints, mix infrequent catastrophic behaviors and frequent, overly conservative ones. Moreover, prior empirical results suggest that enforcing stricter, risk-sensitive constraints can improve performance even under risk-neutral evaluation. The natural framework to incorporate risk-sensitive constraints is utility-constrained MDPs (UCMDPs), but no practical solutions for this problem existed. In this work, we introduce a simple yet powerful methodology for UCMDPs and constrained RL. Besides allowing for risk-sensitive constraints, our framework does not require us to fix constraint limits in advance of training the agent, provided that a sensible range is known. This increases policy flexibility and, in practice, allows for adjustments to these limits at no extra training cost. Besides benefiting from the generality of the framework, our agent shows strong performance in practice, consistently matching or outperforming existing baselines in several Safety Gymnasium benchmark tasks.

</details>


### [96] [Decompose Sparsely Where You Should, Absorb Densely Where You Should No](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14040)
*Ruixuan Deng,Zehao Jin,Zekun Wang,Zihan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现残差流中存在低秩密集的“计算支架”组件，标准SAE难以高效表示。通过引入小秩线性瓶颈吸收该组件，可将密集潜变量减少84%，并提升稀疏探针性能。该组件结构可识别、因果必要，且被稀疏字典冗余编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）通常假设所有激活内容都适合稀疏、单义分解。作者质疑该假设，提出激活中存在低秩、密集组件，对模型计算重要但本质上不适合稀疏表示，这是训练后SAE中持续出现密集潜变量的主要来源。

Method: 在标准SAE（BatchTopK和Matryoshka）旁并行添加小秩-r线性瓶颈，使密集结构在稀疏重建前被吸收。在Gemma-2-2B第12层使用秩-24瓶颈进行实验验证。

Result: 秩-24瓶颈使密集潜变量数量减少达84%，并在匹配稀疏度下提升稀疏探针与针对性探针扰动性能。吸收的组件被识别为顶部主成分与离群维度，移除它使下一词元交叉熵增加7.5倍（远超移除顶部24个PCA方向的2.8倍）。该组件被稀疏字典冗余编码：消融787个最大对齐稀疏特征仅使交叉熵增加2.9倍；消融2048个主题对齐特征对MMLU分类几乎无影响，但移除支架使准确率从98.7%降至随机水平。

Conclusion: 残差流激活中存在紧凑、语义丰富且因果重要的“计算支架”组件，标准稀疏字典对其表示效率低下。这表明基于稀疏的可解释性方法的适用范围需要重新审视，暗示SAE的稀疏假设有其局限。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are typically trained to reconstruct the \textbf{entire} residual stream through a sparse dictionary, implicitly assuming that all activation content is amenable to sparse, monosemantic decomposition. We question this assumption and hypothesize that activations contain a low-rank, dense component that is computationally important to the model yet inherently unsuitable for sparse representation, which serves as a major source of the persistent dense latents widely observed in trained SAEs. To test this, we add a small rank-$r$ linear bottleneck in parallel with standard SAEs (BatchTopK and Matryoshka), allowing dense structure to be absorbed before sparse reconstruction. On Gemma-2-2B layer 12, a rank-24 bottleneck reduces dense latent count by up to 84\% while improving sparse probing and targeted probe perturbation on both architectures at matched sparsity. The absorbed component is (i) \textbf{structurally identifiable} as the top principal components and outlier dimensions; (ii) \textbf{causally necessary}, with removing it raising next-token cross-entropy by 7.5$\times$, far exceeding the 2.8$\times$ from removing the geometrically near-identical top-24 PCA directions; and (iii) \textbf{redundantly encoded by sparse dictionaries}, with ablating 787 maximally aligned sparse features raising cross-entropy by only 2.9$\times$ and ablating 2,048 topic-aligned features leaving MMLU topic classification virtually unchanged, whereas removing the scaffold drops it from 98.7\% to chance. Together, our findings identify a compact, semantically informative and causally important component of residual stream activations (which we term a \textbf{computational scaffold}) that standard sparse dictionaries represent inefficiently, suggesting that the scope of sparsity-based interpretability methods warrants careful re-examination.

</details>


### [97] [Non-Parametric Machine Text Detection via Multi-View Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14060)
*Aleem Khan,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多视图非参数检测框架，通过聚合文档的多个互补特征视图，提高了机器生成文本检测器在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，并提供了可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器文本检测器在面对对抗性攻击（如改写和风格迁移）时准确率急剧下降。虽然文档包含多种互补信号，但参数化分类器在分布偏移（如新型攻击或未见过的语言模型）时容易产生错误预测，缺乏可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个多视图非参数检测框架：1) 从同一文档中提取多个互补的特征视图（风格特征、似然和排序特征、结构特征）；2) 使用高斯过程集成来聚合各视图的证据；3) 通过非参数方法提供校准的概率和原则性的弃权机制，支持高风险场景下的可靠部署。

Result: 在三个基准测试（DetectRL、RAID基准和PAN2025共享任务）上进行评估，涵盖多样化的生成器和攻击方式。该方法在对抗攻击下保持强劲性能，在面对未见过的攻击时优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 多视图检测框架通过要求攻击者同时击败多个独立的检测轴，显著提高了规避成本。高斯过程公式提供的校准概率和分布外输入的原则性弃权，为高风险环境中的可靠部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Adversarial conditions such as paraphrasing and targeted style transfer sharply degrade the accuracy of machine text detectors. A document, however, carries multiple complementary signals (e.g., stylistic features, likelihood and rank-order features, and structural features), and an attack that suppresses one may leave others intact. While a parametric classifier can learn to combine these features given sufficient supervision, classifiers are prone to making confidently incorrect predictions when the distribution shifts (e.g., novel attacks or unseen language models). To address this, we propose a multi-view, non-parametric detection framework that extracts complementary feature views from the same document and aggregates per-view evidence through a Gaussian process ensemble. By aggregating evidence across views, an adversary must simultaneously defeat multiple independent axes of detection, substantially raising the cost of evasion. The Gaussian process formulation additionally provides calibrated probabilities and principled abstention on out-of-distribution inputs, supporting reliable deployment in high-stakes settings. We evaluate on three benchmarks spanning diverse generators and attacks: the DetectRL and RAID benchmarks, and the PAN2025 shared task and demonstrate that our multi-view detector maintains strong performance under the considered attacks, outperforming existing approaches against held out attacks.

</details>


### [98] [Rethinking Backdoor Adversarial Unlearning through the Lens of Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14078)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yujiang Liu,Luping Wei,Wenbo Hou,Bin Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BI-BAU方法，通过持续学习视角将后门学习与遗忘建模为三阶段过程，利用盲反演问题生成对抗样本，有效且彻底地消除后门模型的攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御方法鲁棒性有限，对特定攻击类型往往失效；当前安全调优策略仅提供表面保护，无法完全消除后门效应。需要一种更彻底的后门消除方法。

Method: 将后门学习与遗忘形式化为持续学习视角下的三阶段序列过程，形式化定义完全后门遗忘并基于灾难性遗忘机制推导其必要条件；提出BI-BAU方法，将满足遗忘条件的对抗样本生成建模为盲反演问题，通过将对抗训练的双层优化过程整合到EM算法框架中优化MAP目标；扩展至未知目标类的无目标对抗场景及多模态对比学习任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种后门攻击场景下具有广泛适用性，能够有效且彻底地从后门模型中消除后门效应。

Conclusion: BI-BAU为后门消除提供了理论基础和实用解决方案，在多种攻击类型和实际部署场景（包括多模态学习）中展现出良好的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Existing studies reveal that current backdoor defenses exhibit limited robustness and often fail against specific types of attacks. More concerningly, prevailing safety tuning strategies tend to provide only superficial safety protection, as they fall short of completely eliminating the backdoor effects. In this work, we present a novel formulation of backdoor learning and unlearning as a sequential, three-stage process from a continual learning perspective. Within this framework, we formally define complete backdoor unlearning and further derive the necessary conditions for achieving it based on the mechanism of catastrophic forgetting. Guided by these insights, we propose Blind Inversion-Backdoor Adversarial Unlearning (BI-BAU), which formulates the generation of adversarial examples satisfying the unlearning conditions as a blind inversion problem. We solve this by integrating the bi-level optimization process of adversarial training into an Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm framework to optimize the maximum a posteriori (MAP) objective. Furthermore, BI-BAU is extended to untargeted adversarial scenarios with unknown target classes, as well as to multi-modal contrastive learning tasks, enhancing its applicability to real-world deployment scenarios where pre-trained models may be compromised. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method exhibits general applicability across a wide spectrum of backdoor attacks and can effectively and thoroughly eliminate the backdoor effects from a backdoor model.

</details>


### [99] [Deep Spectral Learning of Embedded Latent Transfer Operators for Stochastic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14079)
*Ryogo Tanaka,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度谱编码器(DSE)，一种基于算子的潜在状态空间模型，通过可学习的非线性特征映射在深度特征空间中学习随机非线性动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对随机非线性动力系统的学习问题，传统方法难以处理复杂非线性动力学，需要一种能结合深度学习非线性表示能力与谱分析方法理论优势的方法。

Method: 在可学习的Galerkin投影特征空间中进行泛函典型相关分析以获取状态坐标，利用时间不变的神经编码器实现从观测到特征的映射，通过岭正则化的闭式解估计转移算子和观测算子，算子估计与协方差算子的Galerkin投影相一致。

Result: 在多个场景的实验中，该方法在存在噪声和部分可观测性条件下表现出稳定且优于序贯贝叶斯滤波和动态模态分解基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地将序贯贝叶斯滤波和Koopman谱模态分解推广到特征空间，为非线性动力系统提供了一种有效的谱学习框架。

Abstract: We propose a spectral learning method for stochastic nonlinear dynamical systems represented with embedded latent transfer operators in deep feature spaces. We instantiate the method as Deep Spectral Encoder (DSE), an operator-based latent state-space model in which a time-invariant neural encoder implements learnable nonlinear feature maps from observations, and these features define Markovian latent states whose temporal evolution and observation mapping are described by the transfer and observation operators, respectively. Functional canonical correlation analysis in a learnable Galerkin-projected feature space provides state coordinates from past and future observations, and the two linear operators are estimated on the state coordinates as ridge-regularized closed-form solutions that coincide with Galerkin projections of the associated covariance operators. On this representation, we generalize sequential Bayesian filtering and Koopman spectral mode decomposition in feature space. Experiments on several scenarios show stable and superior performance with sequential Bayesian filtering and dynamic mode decomposition baselines even under noise and partial observability.

</details>


### [100] [Lyapunov-Based Sample Complexity Analysis for Weakly-Coupled MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14095)
*Tianhao Wu,Matthew Zurek,Weina Wang,Qiaomin Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平均奖励弱耦合马尔可夫决策过程和无休息老虎机的样本复杂度，证明在生成模型下可以多项式复杂度学习近最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱耦合马尔可夫决策过程在臂数量N增大时状态-动作空间呈指数增长，朴素归约到表格MDP会导致复杂度很高。本文旨在利用弱耦合结构，降低学习和计算复杂度。

Method: 采用plug-in方法：从数据估计经验模型，然后应用高效规划算法。提出基于Lyapunov的分析框架，通过漂移转移技术在真实模型和经验模型间进行分析，并进行线性规划松弛的细粒度扰动分析。

Result: 对于完全异质WCMDP，建立了首个有限样本PAC保证，复杂度关于N为多项式级别，最优性间隙为O(1/√N)；对于同质RB，在温和结构假设下可达到更小的最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在弱耦合结构下，平均奖励WCMDP和RB的学习问题可在多项式复杂度下解决，所提出的Lyapunov分析框架和LP扰动分析为分析LP类策略和弱耦合系统提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning in average-reward weakly-coupled Markov decision processes (WCMDPs) and Restless Bandits (RBs) under a generative model. Naive reduction to a tabular MDP leads to high complexity bounds as the state-action space is exponentially large in the number of arms $N$. By exploiting the weakly coupled structure, we show that near-optimal policies can be learned with sample and computational complexities that are polynomial in $N$. Specifically, we analyze the plug-in approach, which applies an efficient planning algorithm to an empirical model estimated from data. For fully heterogeneous WCMDPs, we establish the first finite-sample PAC guarantee with polynomial complexity and an $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ optimality gap. For homogeneous RBs, we further prove that a smaller optimality gap is achievable under mild structural assumptions. A primary technical contribution of our work is a novel Lyapunov-based analysis framework. Unlike classical approaches that rely on the difficult-to-control bias function, our framework uses an explicitly constructed Lyapunov function along with a drift transfer technique between the true and empirical models. A key step of independent interest in our framework is a fine-grained perturbation analysis for the underlying linear programming (LP) relaxation, which provides a general tool for analyzing LP-based policies and weakly-coupled systems.

</details>


### [101] [Numbers Already Carry Their Own Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14108)
*Suhyun Bae,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Adelic operation-preserved embeddings (AOE)，一种无需训练的表示方法，能同时捕获数值的实数值和模（p-adic）特征。该方法在代数组合基准测试中取得显著提升，首次在Weaving Pattern任务上达到完美准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统长期存在"数字问题"（number problem），即难以有效处理和理解数值信息。现有方法依赖任务特定的重新训练，且未能充分保留数值的数学结构。本文旨在通过设计一种保留加法和乘法结构的嵌入方法，为解决这一根本性问题提供新路径。

Method: 提出AOE（Adelic operation-preserved embeddings），一种基于Adelic理论的表示方法。核心创新包括：1) 同时捕获实数值和p-adic模特征；2) 按设计保留加法和乘法运算结构；3) 无需训练，可直接集成到现有架构中，实现即插即用。

Result: 在代数组合基准测试中取得一致性能提升，其中最具突破性的成果是在Weaving Pattern任务上首次达到100%完美准确率，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AOE提供了一种原则性的方法来克服AI中的"数字问题"，通过保留数学结构实现数值输入的语义表示。该方法无需任务特定训练的特点使其具有广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Adelic operation-preserved embeddings (AOE), a training-free representation that captures both a number's real value and its modular (p-adic) signatures. This construction preserves additive and multiplicative structure by design, turning numerical input into embeddings that "speak in the language of mathematics." Unlike prior approaches that rely on task-specific retraining, AOE is plug-and-play and drops seamlessly into existing architectures. On algebraic combinatorics benchmarks, it delivers consistent gains including the first-ever perfect accuracy on the Weaving Pattern task-while suggesting a principled path forward for overcoming the long-standing "number problem" in AI.

</details>


### [102] [DTVEM-RE: A Hierarchical Random-Effects Extension of the Differential Time-Varying Effect Model for Person-Specific Multi-Lag Estimation in Intensive Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14116)
*Amartya Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DTVEM-RE扩展模型，允许每个人拥有自己的滞后系数，解决了原DTVEM模型假设所有人共享相同滞后结构的问题，通过贝叶斯VAR和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck两种实现方式验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的DTVEM模型假设所有人共享相同的滞后结构，这与现代临床研究认为个体存在差异的前提相矛盾，且原作者将解决这一问题列为未来工作。

Method: 开发DTVEM-RE扩展模型，包含两个验证版本：(1)基于Stan的离散时间层次贝叶斯向量自回归模型(VAR)，实现跨个体信息汇聚并提供校准的不确定性；(2)基于ctsem的连续时间个体Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型，直接处理不均匀间隔的测量时点。

Result: (1)模拟研究表明贝叶斯版本恢复个体间差异参数tau_a的偏差低于0.01，覆盖率达90-93%；(2)在Fisher等人EMA数据集(N=40)上，个体特异性滞后-1效应在三个情绪条目上变化一个数量级，贝叶斯与GAMM估计高度一致(r=0.87-0.92)，DTVEM-RE在四种离散时间方法中实现最佳一步前预测；(3)多滞后版本显示所有九个tau_k值的可信区间均不包含零，且个体差异最大的滞后在不同条目间变化；(4)两个版本在个体特异性滞后-1估计上几乎完全一致(r≥0.995)。

Conclusion: DTVEM-RE是首个实现DTVEM风格滞后检测的个体特异性方法，将标准DTVEM作为特例包含其中，为密集纵向数据分析提供了更符合个体差异临床研究前提的工具。

Abstract: The Differential Time-Varying Effect Model (DTVEM) of Jacobson et al. (2019) is a popular tool for finding the best time lag in intensive longitudinal data, but it assumes everyone shares the same lag structure. The original authors named fixing this as future work, and it clashes with the premise of modern clinical research, which is that people differ. We present DTVEM-RE, an extension that lets each person have their own lag coefficients, with two versions of the confirmatory step: a discrete-time hierarchical Bayesian VAR in Stan, which pools across people and gives calibrated uncertainty, and a continuous-time per-person Ornstein-Uhlenbeck model in ctsem, which handles unevenly spaced beeps directly. We report four results. A simulation shows the Bayesian version recovers the between-person spread tau_a with bias below 0.01 and coverage of 90 to 93 percent. On the Fisher et al. (2017) EMA dataset (N=40), person-specific lag-1 effects vary by an order of magnitude across three mood items, the Bayesian and GAMM estimates agree closely (r=0.87 to 0.92), and DTVEM-RE gives the best one-step-ahead prediction among four discrete-time methods. A multi-lag version shows all nine tau_k values have credible intervals excluding zero, and the lag where people differ most changes across items, something lag-1-only methods like mlVAR cannot detect. Finally, the two versions agree almost exactly on person-specific lag-1 estimates (r >= 0.995), differing only as shrinkage predicts. DTVEM-RE is, to our knowledge, the first person-specific implementation of DTVEM-style lag detection, and it contains standard DTVEM as a special case.

</details>


### [103] [Recovering Stranded Discrimination in Knowledge Tracing: Per-Item Bias Correction via Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14123)
*Xiaoran Yan,Cheng Tang,Atsushi Shimada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLC方法，通过状态空间模型修正知识追踪中因架构限制和部署后偏移导致的逐项逻辑偏差，在四个数据集上显著提升AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的知识追踪模型在训练后通常被冻结，但由于骨干架构的逐项表达力有限以及部署后项目属性发生偏移，会出现系统性的逐项逻辑偏差，导致预测质量下降。现有的全局后验校准器（如Platt缩放、温度缩放等）虽然能改善概率估计，但由于仅进行单调分数变换，无法改变AUC所衡量的判别能力。恢复被掩盖的判别能力需要以项目身份为条件进行调整。

Method: 提出SLC（State-space Logit Correction）方法，包含三个关键步骤：1）通过Laplace/IRLS将二元观测转换为高斯伪观测；2）通过卡尔曼平滑器应用经验贝叶斯收缩；3）拟合偏移Platt链接函数。该状态空间公式还产生了一个可检测性界限，用于表征伯努利信息下界，解释了为何在当前数据密度下时间跟踪无法提供增益。

Result: 在四个数据集、五个骨干网络和三个随机种子的实验中，SLC在所有四个数据集上均提升了AUC，在三个数据集上改善了NLL（负对数似然），且优势主要集中在稀疏项目上。跨领域对照实验表明，当部署的骨干网络留下实体级偏差时，教育领域之外也可能出现相同现象。

Conclusion: SLC成功解决了知识追踪模型中的逐项逻辑偏差问题，通过状态空间建模恢复了对稀疏项目的判别能力。该方法不仅适用于教育领域，还可推广到其他存在骨干网络实体级偏差的场景。理论分析揭示的可检测性界限解释了当前数据密度下时间跟踪的局限性。

Abstract: Deployed knowledge-tracing models are typically frozen after training, yet systematic per-item logit bias arises, from limited per-item expressivity in backbone architectures and from post-deployment shifts in item properties, degrading prediction quality. Global post-hoc calibrators such as Platt scaling, temperature scaling, and isotonic regression improve probability estimates but leave discriminative ability, as measured by AUC, unchanged. This AUC invariance is a structural consequence of monotone score-only transforms; recovering the stranded discrimination requires conditioning on item identity. We propose SLC (State-space Logit Correction), which converts binary observations to Gaussian pseudo-observations via Laplace/IRLS, applies empirical-Bayes shrinkage through a Kalman smoother, and fits an offset-Platt link. The state-space formulation also yields a detectability bound that characterizes the Bernoulli information floor, explaining why temporal tracking provides no benefit at current data densities. Across four datasets, five backbones, and three seeds, SLC improves AUC on all four datasets and NLL on three, with the advantage concentrating on sparse items. Cross-domain controls suggest that the same phenomenon can arise beyond education when the deployed backbone leaves entity-level bias.

</details>


### [104] [Decoupled Latent Optimization of Diffusion Models for Full Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14139)
*Chen Min,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦潜在优化(DLO)方法解决全波形反演问题，通过二次惩罚目标函数分离物理变量与潜在变量，在OpenFWI、Marmousi和Overthrust基准上超越现有方法，兼具数据保真度与先验一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演(FWI)是求解严重病态非凸PDE约束优化问题的方法。传统正则化方法能够稳定反演但无法重建真实地质结构；近期基于扩散先验的方法虽然提高了真实性，但在数据保真度和先验一致性之间存在脆弱的权衡关系。需要一种既能保持数据保真度又能生成真实地质结构的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出解耦潜在优化(DLO)方法，将标准潜在优化形式松弛为关于辅助物理变量和潜在变量的二次惩罚目标函数。数据保真度梯度在物理空间中作用，扩散采样器仅通过解码的先验样本贡献，保留了经典FWI的标准平滑速度初始化。实现了物理空间优化与潜在空间采样的解耦。

Result: 在OpenFWI基准测试中，DLO在干净、噪声和缺失道采集条件下均优于传统正则化方法和现有基于扩散的方法。在70×70 OpenFWI模型上训练的先验可直接迁移到Marmousi和Overthrust基准，成功恢复复杂的断层结构，对初始化平滑和测量噪声保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DLO方法通过解耦物理变量与潜在变量的优化，有效平衡了数据保真度与先验一致性，避免了传统扩散先验方法的脆弱权衡问题，在全波形反演任务中实现了更优的性能和更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) recovers subsurface velocity from seismic recordings by solving a severely ill-posed, nonconvex PDE-constrained optimization. Classical regularizers stabilize the inversion but fail to reproduce realistic geological structures; recent diffusion-prior methods improve realism at the cost of a fragile trade-off between data fidelity and prior consistency. We propose Decoupled Latent Optimization (DLO), which relaxes the standard latent-optimization formulation into a quadratic-penalty objective over an auxiliary physical variable and a latent variable. The data-fidelity gradient acts in physical space, the diffusion sampler contributes only through a decoded prior sample, and the standard smoothed-velocity initialization of classical FWI is preserved. On the OpenFWI benchmark, DLO outperforms classical regularizers and existing diffusion-based methods under clean, noisy, and missing-trace acquisitions. The prior, trained on 70*70 OpenFWI models, transfers directly to the Marmousi and Overthrust benchmarks, where DLO recovers intricate fault structures and remains robust to initialization smoothing and measurement noise.

</details>


### [105] [Trust but Verify: Mitigating Medical Hallucinations via Post-Hoc Adversarial Auditing and Multi-Agent Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14149)
*Muhammad Osama,Maheera Amjad,Zartasha Mustansar,Arslan Shaukat,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种五代理"信任但验证"框架，有效将LLM在临床问答中推荐已禁用药物的幻觉错误率降低约53%，为医疗AI部署提供了一种模型无关的安全合规方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在医疗场景中应用日益广泛，但其幻觉倾向在临床决策中构成风险。具体而言，模型可能因参数化知识过时而推荐已被监管机构禁用或撤市的药物，这直接威胁患者安全。

Method: 构建了一个包含103道对抗性多选题（MCQ）的数据集，其历史正确答案现对应已禁用药物；开发了一个五代理"信任但验证"系统（使用单一LLM骨干）；在三种开源模型家族（GPT-OSS、Llama-3、Falcon-3）上对比评估了普通配置与代理配置的表现。

Result: 普通配置下，所有模型均表现出高幻觉率，倾向于选择与其训练数据模式匹配的禁用药物；代理架构将幻觉错误率（HER）平均降低约53%，点wise分数从-0.25（不安全推荐）向0.0（适当拒绝）偏移；安全审计机制即使面对模型参数化知识偏向禁用物质时也能拦截危险输出。

Conclusion: 所提出的多代理框架提供了一种模型无关的方法来强制执行监管合规，优先考虑患者安全而非流畅的文本生成；该方法展示了如何将实时监管数据集成到LLM流程中，为安全关键医疗场景中部署自主AI系统提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare settings, yet their tendency to hallucinate poses risks when clinical decisions are involved. This study examine whether LLMs recommend recently banned or withdrawn pharmaceuticals when answering clinical questions and tests an agent-based method for reducing such errors. We developed a five-agent "Trust but Verify" system using a single LLM backbone. To measure regulatory knowledge obsolescence, we created an adversarial dataset of 103 clinical MCQs where historically correct answers now refer to banned substances. This scale ensures statistical significance across various therapeutic classes. We evaluated three open-access model families (GPT-OSS, Llama-3, Falcon-3) under vanilla and agentic conditions. Performance was measured via pointwise score, label accuracy, Hallucination Error Rate (HER), and Component Fidelity (CF) score. We also observed clinical safety regression in proprietary models. In default configurations, all models showed high hallucination rates, consistently selecting banned drugs that matched training data patterns. Our proposed agentic architecture reduced HER by approximately 53% across models. Pointwise scores shifted from -0.25 (unsafe recommendation) toward 0.0 (appropriate refusal). The safety audit intercepted dangerous outputs even when models' parametric knowledge favored the banned substance. The proposed multi-agent framework offers a model-agnostic method for enforcing regulatory compliance that prioritizes patient safety over fluent text generation. Our work demonstrates a practical approach for deploying autonomous AI systems in safety-critical healthcare settings. It shows how real-time regulatory data can be integrated into LLM pipelines to support clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [106] [Small LLMs: Pruning vs. Training from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14150)
*Yufeng Xu,Taiming Lu,Kunjun Li,Jiachen Zhu,Mingjie Sun,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在有限训练token预算下，剪枝优于从头训练；当预算充足时，粗粒度剪枝可被从头训练匹配或超越，但细粒度剪枝仍保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证剪枝作为获取高性能小型语言模型捷径的承诺是否成立，探究在何种条件下从预训练大模型剪枝优于从头训练小模型。

Method: 以0.5-0.8的剪枝比例对Llama-3.1-8B进行剪枝，使用涵盖深度、宽度和稀疏粒度的六种剪枝方法，在两种token匹配的受控设置下进行实验比较。

Result: 相同训练token预算下，剪枝初始化始终优于随机初始化；当从头训练获得完整token预算时，细粒度剪枝仍保持优势，而粗粒度结构化剪枝可被匹配或超越。优势随训练预算增加和剪枝比例提高而收窄。

Conclusion: 明确建议：拥有大型预训练模型且训练token预算有限时，剪枝优于从头训练；当训练预算不受限时，粗粒度剪枝场景下从头训练具有竞争力，大型预训练父模型并非总是必需的。

Abstract: Pruning promises a shortcut to strong small language models. In this work, we examine this promise by pruning Llama-3.1-8B at pruning ratios of 0.5--0.8 with six methods spanning depth, width, and sparse granularities, under two controlled token-matched settings. (1) With the same training token budget, pruned initialization consistently outperforms random initialization. This shows that the parent model provides a strong starting point, although the advantage narrows as the training token budget grows and as the pruning ratio rises, nearly vanishing at the highest pruning ratio we study. (2) When training from scratch is instead given the full token budget consumed by the whole pipeline, pruning at finer granularities still retains an advantage, while coarser structured pruning can be matched or surpassed. This suggests that the parent model transfers knowledge that additional training tokens alone cannot fully recover, but only at fine granularity. Taken together, our results yield a clear recommendation: with a large pretrained model in hand and a limited training token budget, pruning is better than training from scratch; when the training budget is not limited, training from scratch can be competitive for coarser pruning, so a large pretrained parent is not always necessary.

</details>


### [107] [Graph-based Target Back-Propagation for Context Adaptation in Multi-LLM Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14155)
*Tan Zhu,Tong Yao,Kananart Kuwaranancharoen,Amit Singh,Yushang Lai,Deepa Mohan,Shankara Bhargava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GTBP框架，通过图结构的目标反向传播机制，解决了多LLM智能体系统上下文自适应中的信用分配不准确和收敛保证缺失问题，在三个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文自适应方法在扩展到多LLM智能体系统时存在两个关键问题：(1)信用分配不准确，难以定位各组件对最终输出的贡献；(2)缺乏收敛性保证，无法确保迭代优化过程的稳定性。这些局限性阻碍了自动化提示工程在复杂多智能体系统中的有效应用。

Method: 提出基于图的目标反向传播(GTBP)框架，将有向无环图建模的智能体工作流作为优化对象。GTBP通过工作流图反向传播局部目标输出，利用目标-输出差异引导分层提示更新机制，实现精确的信用分配和可控的优化过程。

Result: 理论证明GTBP的分层提示更新在迭代过程中趋于稳定，且具备足够能力的LLM优化器能够降低总体目标函数。实验结果表明，GTBP在三个基准测试中持续优于强基线方法，同时保持可比的计算成本。

Conclusion: GTBP为多LLM智能体系统的上下文自适应提供了理论保证和实用解决方案，通过图结构的反向传播机制实现了精确的信用分配和稳定的收敛特性，为复杂智能体工作流的自动化提示工程开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Context adaptation automates prompt engineering in LLM-based systems by iteratively revising tunable prompts from task feedback, without modifying model weights. Extending this paradigm to multi-LLM agentic systems is crucial: existing methods suffer from inaccurate credit assignment and lack convergence guarantees. We propose \textbf{G}raph-based \textbf{T}arget \textbf{B}ack-\textbf{P}ropagation (GTBP), a context adaptation framework for agentic workflows modeled as directed acyclic graphs. GTBP propagates local target outputs backward through the workflow graph and uses target--output discrepancies to guide a stage-wise prompt update mechanism. Theoretically, we show that GTBP's stage-wise prompt updates become stable over iterations, and that a sufficiently capable LLM optimizer can decrease the overall objective. Empirically, GTBP consistently outperforms strong baselines across three benchmarks while maintaining comparable computational cost.

</details>


### [108] [Learning High Coverage Discriminative Parsimonious Rulesets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14156)
*Mariamma Antony,Raman Sankaran,Chiranjib Bhattacharyya,Uma Satya Ranjan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDPR方法，首次通过子模最大化算法构建高准确且高可解释性的IF-THEN规则集，在分类任务上实现了比次优算法高2.5倍以上的覆盖率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于IF-THEN规则的分类算法虽然隐式地追求预测准确性，但往往在保证可解释性的质量指标（如规则集的覆盖率和简洁性）上表现不足。因此，需要开发能够同时实现高判别能力和高可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出了CDPR方法，引入了两种基于子模最大化的算法，这些算法不仅能提供可证明的覆盖率保证，还能生成既具有判别力又简洁的规则集。

Result: 实验证明，通过该方法学习到的规则集在准确性和可解释性方面均表现更优，且平均覆盖率比次优算法提高了2.5倍以上。

Conclusion: CDPR方法成功实现了在分类问题中创建高度准确且可解释的规则集的目标，这是该领域的首次尝试，为构建兼顾预测性能和可解释性的学习系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Learning systems based on IF-THEN rule representations readily offer interpretability, making them a crucial focus in contemporary AI research. A key objective for such rule sets is to achieve both high discriminative power and interpretability. While existing state-of-the-art algorithms implicitly prioritize predictive accuracy, they often fall short on one or more quality metrics that ensure interpretability, such as coverage and parsimony of rule sets. Motivated by this, this paper propose the development of CDPR, which aims to create highly accurate and interpretable rule sets for classification problems. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first attempt to establish such an approach. In this study, we introduce two algorithms rooted in submodular maximization, which not only provide provable guarantees on coverage but also yield rule sets that are both discriminative and parsimonious. We empirically demonstrate that rule sets learned through our approaches achieve higher accuracy and interpretability and has more than a 2.5-fold improvement in average coverage rates when compared to the next best algorithm.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Urban Access Costs from Origin-Destination Flows via Inverse Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14157)
*Paula Joy B. Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逆最优运输的框架，利用行政数据估计城市服务选择中的潜在成本函数，并在菲律宾教育补贴案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规划者通常只能观察到家庭选择服务设施的行为结果（如学校入学流向），而无法直接观测其背后的潜在成本函数，即家庭如何在距离、价格和机构可达性等因素间进行权衡。这一信息缺失导致难以设计有效的服务补贴和设施布局策略。

Method: 将学校间的入学流动视为熵最优运输问题，提出两种互补的逆最优运输模型来反演潜在选择成本：1）可解释的距离带状模型（含补贴项）；2）通过可微Sinkhorn前向传播训练的神经成本模型。该框架应用于菲律宾最 populous 地区的283,016条学习者出行数据和23,820条观察到的流量记录。

Result: 框架成功估计了补贴等效距离λ^(k)，该指标被解释为由补贴抵消的感知出行成本公里数。这一指标能够将行政起讫点数据转化为可解释的规划指标。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何将行政起讫点数据转化为可解释的规划指标，为感知可达性的补贴设计、设施选址和城市服务配置提供了决策支持工具，证明了逆最优运输方法在城市服务系统分析中的实用价值。

Abstract: Cities deliver basic services through mixed public-private facility networks, including schools, clinics, transit providers, and subsidized service points. In these systems, planners often observe where households go, but not the latent cost function through which they trade off factors such as distance, price, and institutional access. We study this urban problem through school choice in the Philippines, where the country's largest national education subsidy is intended to redirect learners from congested public schools to participating private schools. Treating school-to-school enrollment flows as an entropic optimal transport plan, we recover latent choice costs using two complementary inverse optimal transport models: an interpretable distance-banded model with a subsidy term, and a neural cost model trained through a differentiable Sinkhorn forward pass. Applied to 283{,}016 learner trips across 23{,}820 observed flows in the most populated region, the framework estimates a subsidy-equivalent distance, $λ^{(k)}$, interpreted as the kilometers of perceived travel cost offset by the subsidy. The case demonstrates how administrative origin-destination data can be transformed into interpretable planning metrics for accessibility-aware subsidy design, facility siting, and urban service allocation.

</details>


### [110] [Curvature-Guided Geometric Representation for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14159)
*Shuai Li,Chuan-Xian Ren,Yuhao Li,Ziqi Huang,Yue Pan,Mingzhe Tang,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RicciBind框架，通过融合Ricci曲率引导的层次结构学习与最优传输跨域对齐，有效建模蛋白质-配体结合亲和性，显著提升预测精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法难以同时刻画局部几何结构和全局协调的跨分子相互作用，限制了对复杂结合机制的建模能力。

Method: 利用Ricci曲率捕获分子结构内的局部相互作用紧密性，构建曲率感知的层次表示；通过最优传输聚类匹配机制，在几何约束下对齐蛋白质和配体簇，实现全局一致的跨域对应。

Result: 在PLA基准和虚拟筛选任务上取得优异的预测性能和泛化能力；消融实验验证了Ricci曲率在增强分子相互作用表示中的关键作用。

Conclusion: RicciBind通过曲率引导的结构编码与最优传输驱动的跨域对齐的耦合，有效建模复杂相互作用语义，显著提升结合亲和性预测的准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Protein-ligand binding affinity (PLA) prediction is critical in drug discovery. Despite the notable advancements in machine learning-based approaches, existing methods struggle to jointly characterize local geometric organization and globally coordinated cross-molecular interactions, limiting their ability to model complex binding mechanisms. Here, we propose RicciBind, a geometric representation framework that integrates curvature-guided hierarchical structure learning with optimal transport (OT)-based cross-domain alignment to model molecular interactions. Specifically, RicciBind leverages Ricci curvature to capture local interaction tightness within molecular structures, enhancing structural awareness and organizing atomic interactions into curvature-aware hierarchical representations. An OT-based cluster matching mechanism then aligns protein and ligand clusters across heterogeneous domains under geometric constraints, enabling globally consistent correspondences and revealing higher-order interaction patterns beyond local neighborhoods. By coupling curvature-guided structure encoding with OT-driven cross-domain alignment, RicciBind effectively models complex interaction semantics and substantially improves both the accuracy and interpretability of binding affinity prediction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RicciBind achieved superior predictive performance and generalization across PLA benchmarks and virtual screening tasks. Ablation studies further confirmed the essential role of Ricci curvature in enhancing molecular interaction representations.

</details>


### [111] [Machine Learning for Biomedical Raman Spectroscopy: From Spectral Acquisition to Clinical Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14169)
*Bogdan Oancea,Ana Maria Seciu-Grama,Nicoleta Siminea,Laura Mihaela Stefan,Alice Stoica,Joel Sjoberg,Marian Necula,Ana-Maria Prelipcean,Corneliu Ovidiu Vrancianu,Eduard Milea,Andrei Păun,Ion Petre,Mihaela Păun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习在生物医学拉曼光谱分析全流程中的应用，重点讨论了从预处理到临床诊断的关键技术，并指出了临床转化面临的主要障碍与未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱在癌症诊断、分子分型、微生物鉴定和术中决策支持等生物医学领域具有重要价值，但其数据具有高维度、高噪声、荧光背景干扰、采集变异性和生物异质性等特点，亟需稳健的计算分析方法来充分发挥其临床潜力。

Method: 系统综述机器学习在生物医学拉曼光谱分析流程中的各个环节的应用，包括：预处理与信号校正、无监督结构发现、有监督诊断与分子分层、表征与迁移学习、可解释性分析、生物标志物发现，以及与影像、病理和分子图谱的多模态整合。

Result: 识别了临床转化面临的主要障碍：数据集规模有限、仪器间变异性、预处理不一致、外部验证不足、可重复性问题以及软件、数据和元数据共享不足。强调了机器学习不仅应用于诊断分类，更重要的是实现生物学可解释和临床可操作的分析。

Conclusion: 拉曼-AI系统的临床部署需要在方法学创新的基础上，结合标准化、稳健验证、可解释性和即用型分析框架。未来的发展需要整合方法学、生物医学和转化视角，开发可靠且可临床部署的系统。

Abstract: Raman spectroscopy provides label-free, chemically specific characterization of biological systems and has become an important tool for cancer diagnosis, molecular subtyping, microbiological identification, and intraoperative decision support. Biomedical Raman spectra are, however, high-dimensional, noisy, and affected by fluorescence background, acquisition variability, and biological heterogeneity, making robust computational analysis essential.
  This review examines the role of machine learning across the biomedical Raman spectroscopy pipeline, from preprocessing and signal correction to unsupervised structure discovery, supervised diagnosis and molecular stratification, representation and transfer learning, explainability, biomarker discovery, and multimodal integration with imaging, pathology, and molecular profiling. Emphasis is placed on the use of machine learning not only for diagnostic classification, but also for biologically interpretable and clinically actionable analysis.
  We also discuss the main barriers to clinical translation, including limited dataset sizes, inter-instrument variability, inconsistent preprocessing, insufficient external validation, reproducibility concerns, and limited sharing of software, data, and metadata. We argue that progress will require methodological advances together with standardization, robust validation, explainability, and deployment-ready analytical frameworks. By integrating methodological, biomedical, and translational perspectives, this review outlines key directions for developing reliable and clinically deployable Raman-AI systems.

</details>


### [112] [Context-aware Modality-Topology Co-Alignment for Multimodal Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14172)
*Sirui Zhang,Xu Wang,Zhengyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Hongchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoMAG，一种统一的多模态属性图(MAG)骨干方法，通过学习任务自适应的可靠上下文和模态保持对齐，解决了现有方法中任务无关传播和过度压缩融合的问题，在图预测、模态匹配和图条件生成任务上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAG方法依赖固定的图上下文或均匀融合表示，导致任务无关的传播和过度压缩融合，无法满足多样化任务需求且难以保持模态特定证据。

Method: 提出CoMAG框架：1)可靠上下文学习——基于多模态语义一致性估计边可靠性，用语义邻居补充原始拓扑，通过任务感知门控选择上下文组件；2)模态保持跳令牌对齐——维护模态特定的多跳轨迹，跨模态匹配模态-跳令牌，解耦共享和私有表示。单次前向传播同时生成图表示和模态表示。

Result: 在9个OpenMAG数据集上与特征-only、图-only、多模态及统一MAG基线对比，CoMAG在图级预测、模态匹配和图条件生成任务上均取得最佳性能，同时保持稀疏边线性复杂度。

Conclusion: 任务自适应可靠上下文和模态保持对齐能有效提升结构预测、跨模态匹配和图条件生成性能，同时保留模态特定线索，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal Attributed Graphs (MAGs) model real-world entities by coupling graph topology with heterogeneous attributes such as text and images. They support graph-centric tasks requiring structural and class-discriminative representations, and modality-centric tasks requiring fine-grained cross-modal correspondence. However, existing MAG methods often rely on fixed graph contexts or uniformly fused representations, causing task-agnostic propagation and over-compressed fusion that hinder diverse task requirements and modality-specific evidence preservation. To address this, we propose CoMAG, a unified MAG backbone that learns task-adaptive reliable contexts and modality-preserving alignment within them. CoMAG first conducts Reliable Context Learning by estimating edge reliability from multimodal semantic consistency, complementing raw topology with semantic neighbors, and selecting context components through a task-aware gate. It then performs Modality-preserving Hop-token Alignment by maintaining modality-specific multi-hop trajectories, matching modality-hop tokens across modalities, and decoupling shared and private representations. Thus, CoMAG produces graph and modality representations from one forward pass while retaining modality-specific cues. We further analyze stable propagation, over-smoothing mitigation, and modality-collapse control. Experiments on nine OpenMAG datasets compare CoMAG with feature-only, graph-only, multimodal, and unified MAG baselines across graph-level prediction, modality matching, and graph-conditioned generation. Results show that CoMAG achieves the best reported performance, demonstrating that task-adaptive reliable contexts and modality-preserving alignment improve structural prediction, cross-modal matching, and graph-conditioned generation while retaining sparse edge-linear complexity.

</details>


### [113] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了简单约束稀疏装袋（SCSB）框架，通过优化方法同时实现装袋集成模型的压缩与概率校准，在保持或提升泛化精度的同时，最高可实现96%的模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的装袋集成方法（如随机森林、装袋SVM、装袋神经网络）对所有基估计器分配相同的投票权重。这种朴素的均匀先验忽略了基估计器在不同局部区域的性能差异，并导致模型过度自信。

Method: 将集成剪枝与概率校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化袋外（OOB）损失进行求解。为解决L1范数在单纯形上恒定无法实现剪枝的理论悖论，引入凹二次惩罚项以诱导稀疏性。

Result: SCSB方法与模型无关，可实现最高96%的集成压缩率，带来线性推理加速，并显著改善概率校准效果（降低期望校准误差），同时保持或提升泛化精度。

Conclusion: SCSB框架为装袋集成模型提供了一种数学严谨、模型无关的压缩与校准方案，有效解决了均匀投票权重的局限性，在压缩率、推理效率、校准质量及泛化能力方面均取得优异表现。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [114] [Zeta: Dual Whitening for Matrix Optimization via Coordinate-Adaptive Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14187)
*Kaiwen Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Linxiao Li,Ying Sun,Yuchen Li,Yifan Zhang,Bo Han,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Zeta优化器，通过引入双白化机制（坐标白化+谱白化）解决了现有矩阵感知优化器（如Muon）中Newton-Schulz迭代对输入条件数敏感的问题，在多种任务上实现了更好的收敛和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵感知优化器（如Muon）的核心操作Newton-Schulz迭代严重依赖于输入的条件数，但原始动量矩阵存在严重的坐标级尺度异质性，导致正交化误差较大。作者通过卡方均匀性检验验证了这种尺度不平衡在Transformer层中普遍存在，并发现坐标白化可以有效纠正这一问题。

Method: 提出Zeta优化器，采用严格有序的双白化流水线：首先应用坐标白化建立谱白化所需的统计各向同性，然后应用谱白化。这种顺序不是可调选择，而是源于数学依赖关系。作者从理论上证明该双白化流水线通过改善输入的条件数，严格降低了相对于纯谱方法的正交化误差。

Result: 在语言建模（0.6B到8B参数）、混合专家架构和视觉任务上，Zeta匹配或超越了强基线方法。实验表明在正交化之前解决尺度不平衡问题能够带来更快的收敛速度和更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证，证明了在矩阵感知优化中先进行坐标白化再进行谱白化的双白化策略的重要性，为大规模神经网络训练提供了一种更有效的优化方法。

Abstract: Large-scale neural network training increasingly relies on matrix-aware optimizers that exploit the structure of weight parameters beyond element-wise adaptation. However, existing matrix-aware methods such as Muon have an underappreciated vulnerability: their core operation, Newton-Schulz iteration, depends critically on input conditioning, yet the raw momentum matrices exhibit severe coordinate-wise scale heterogeneity. In this paper, we first verify this scale heterogeneity through a chi-square uniformity test, showing that intra-matrix scale imbalance is prevalent across Transformer layers and that coordinate whitening effectively corrects it. Motivated by this finding, we propose Zeta, a dual whitening optimizer that applies coordinate whitening and spectral whitening in a strictly ordered pipeline. The ordering is not a tunable choice but follows from a mathematical dependency: coordinate whitening establishes the statistical isotropy that spectral whitening requires to function reliably. We further prove that this dual pipeline strictly reduces orthogonalization error relative to pure spectral methods by improving the condition number of the input. Empirically, Zeta matches or surpasses strong baselines across language modeling (0.6B to 8B parameters), mixture-of-experts architectures, and vision tasks, demonstrating that resolving scale imbalance before orthogonalization leads to faster convergence and better generalization. Code is available at https://gitcode.com/kevin259/MindSpeed.

</details>


### [115] [DRIVE: Distributional and Retrieval-Augmented Bidding with Value Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14192)
*Miduo Cui,Haochen Wang,Shangqin Mao,Xun Yang,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Xuri Ge,Ying Zhou,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRIVE框架，一种基于Transformer的离线自动竞价方法，通过解耦候选动作生成与决策过程，结合分布建模、检索增强和价值评估，有效解决了现有方法动作坍缩和长尾流量表现不佳的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习和基于Transformer的竞价方法存在两个主要局限：（1）单模态和纯参数化公式会将多种有效的竞价策略坍缩为次优的平均动作；（2）在稀疏或长尾流量下表现不可靠。这些限制阻碍了自动竞价系统在真实广告场景中的性能提升。

Method: 提出DRIVE（Distributional and Retrieval-Augmented Bidding with Value Evaluation）框架，核心创新在于将候选动作生成与决策制定解耦。该方法包含三个关键组件：（1）分布式动作建模，学习动作分布而非单一动作；（2）检索增强的候选生成，从高质量历史决策中检索相关动作；（3）基于价值的评估，在推理时选择最优出价。

Result: 在AuctionNet数据集和额外的离线强化学习基准上进行了广泛实验，结果表明DRIVE能够持续提升竞价性能，并且在多种基于Transformer的方法上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DRIVE通过解耦候选生成与决策、引入分布建模和检索增强机制，有效解决了现有离线竞价方法的局限性，为实时广告系统中的自动竞价提供了一个更加鲁棒和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a core component of real-time advertising systems, where decisions must optimize long-term performance under budget and cost constraints, while online exploration is prohibitively risky. Offline reinforcement learning and, more recently, Transformer-based sequence modeling have shown promise for learning bidding policies from logged data, but their unimodal and purely parametric formulations often collapse multiple effective bidding strategies into suboptimal averaged actions and perform unreliably under sparse or long-tail traffic. To mitigate these limitations, we propose DRIVE (Distributional and Retrieval-Augmented Bidding with Value Evaluation), a unified Transformer-based framework that decouples candidate action generation from decision making for offline auto-bidding. DRIVE combines distributional action modeling, retrieval-augmented candidate generation from high-quality historical decisions, and value-based evaluation to select the most promising bid at inference time. Extensive experiments on AuctionNet and additional offline reinforcement learning benchmarks demonstrate that DRIVE consistently improves bidding performance and generalizes well across multiple Transformer-based methods.

</details>


### [116] [Structured Noise Adaptation for Sequential Bayesian Filtering with Embedded Latent Transfer Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14195)
*Naichang Ke,Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于嵌入潜在转移算子(ELTO)的新型贝叶斯滤波方法，通过结构化噪声自适应机制解决了传统方法无法适应非平稳过程的问题，提升了时变环境下的状态估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于嵌入潜在转移算子(ELTO)的卡尔曼滤波器存在关键局限性：其采用的简化噪声模型无法动态适应非平稳过程，限制了在时变环境中的应用效果。

Method: 提出一种基于ELTO的贝叶斯滤波方法，引入新的结构化参数化方法来建模滤波器的噪声模型。该结构化噪声自适应方法将最优时不变噪声模型的数据驱动学习与动态参数自适应相结合，使其能够响应非平稳过程中动力学的变化。

Result: 实证结果表明，所提出的结构化噪声自适应方法在噪声和时变环境中显著提升了滤波器的动态状态估计性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化噪声自适应机制，成功解决了ELTO卡尔曼滤波器在非平稳过程中的局限性，为复杂时变环境下的序贯状态估计提供了更有效的统计工具。

Abstract: Kalman filters based on the Embedded Latent Transfer Operators (ELTO) emerge as novel statistical tools for sequential state estimation. However, a critical limitation stems from their use of simplified noise models, which fail to dynamically adapt to non-stationary processes. To address this limitation, we introduce an ELTO-based Bayesian filtering approach with a new structured parameterization for the filter's noise model. This parameterization enables structured noise adaptation, which couples the data-driven learning of an optimal time-invariant noise model with dynamic parameter adaptation that responds to changes in dynamics within non-stationary processes. Empirical results show that our structured noise adaptation improves the filter's dynamic state estimation performance in noisy, time-varying environments.

</details>


### [117] [LapidaryEngine: Fully Conversational Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14215)
*Yusei Ito,Yuta Suzuki,Tomoya Murata,Masaki Adachi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LapidaryEngine，首个支持完全对话式晶体生成的模型，通过中间表示实现文本与晶体结构的双向转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到晶体生成模型存在两大局限：需要高度结构化的输入格式，以及只能单向生成而无法反向映射，这阻碍了真正对话式材料设计工作流的实现。

Method: 引入枢轴表示（pivot representation）作为中间形式，在缺乏直接配对数据集的情况下实现文本与晶体结构的双向转换，支持自由形式自然语言请求和迭代式对话优化编辑。

Result: 在绝缘体发现、稳定性优化、组分修改和结构编辑等多样化任务中验证了LapidaryEngine的能力，展示了其在交互式场景中将生成材料与用户意图对齐的性能。

Conclusion: LapidaryEngine成功实现了对话式晶体生成，突破了现有方法的输入格式和单向生成限制，为用户提供了直观的材料设计交互体验。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has inspired the vision of generating bespoke crystal materials directly from natural-language instructions, enabling users to design materials through intuitive, conversational interaction. Existing text-to-crystal generative models represent important early steps toward this goal, but they suffer from two critical limitations: (i) restricted input formats that require highly structured descriptions (e.g., chemical formulas), and (ii) one-directional generation, where models can map text to crystal but cannot perform the inverse. These limitations prevent fully conversational workflows and hinder alignment with users' inherently ambiguous and evolving desiderata. We address these challenges with LapidaryEngine, the first model to support fully conversational crystal generation. LapidaryEngine accepts free-form natural-language requests and performs iterative refinement and editing in a dialogue-like manner. The key innovation is a pivot representation, a third, intermediate form that enables bidirectional translation between text and crystal structures despite the absence of direct paired datasets. Leveraging this pivot allows robust interpretation of user feedback and precise structural control. We demonstrate LapidaryEngine across diverse tasks, including insulator discovery, stability optimization, compositional modification, and structural editing, showcasing its ability to align generated materials with user intent in an interactive manner.

</details>


### [118] [Curvature-Informed Potential Energy Surface for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14217)
*Peng-Fei Sun,Chuan-Xian Ren,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CPES图神经网络方法，通过引入曲率信息势能面表示来建模构象柔性，融合动态与静态特征以提升蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何深度学习的蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测方法主要依赖单一结合构象的静态相互作用几何，忽视了分子柔性和结合诱导的构象变化，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 提出CPES图神经网络：首先从平衡构型处势能面的Hessian矩阵推导曲率谱描述符，其特征值定义势能面的局部主曲率；使用谱交叉注意力比较未结合的配体与蛋白质和结合态复合物，捕捉结合诱导的构象动力学变化；同时通过几何感知消息传递、软聚类和双向交叉注意力从静态结构特征学习层次化蛋白质-配体相互作用表示；最后融合曲率信息的动态表示与静态相互作用表示进行亲和力回归。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，CPES实现了更好的预测性能，并提供了物理可解释性。

Conclusion: CPES通过将物理启发的曲率表示与静态结构特征相结合，有效解决了现有方法忽视分子柔性的问题，为蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测提供了一种具有物理可解释性的改进方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of protein-ligand binding affinity is essential for structure-based drug discovery. Recent geometric deep learning methods have achieved promising performance by representing protein-ligand complexes as three-dimensional graphs. However, most existing approaches mainly rely on static interaction geometry from a single bound conformation, while neglecting molecular flexibility and binding-induced conformational changes. To address this limitation, we propose a curvature-informed potential energy surface (CPES) graph neural network for protein-ligand binding affinity prediction, which incorporates physics-informed curvature representations to model conformational flexibility. CPES first derives curvature spectral descriptors from the Hessian of the potential energy surface evaluated at equilibrium configurations, whose eigenvalues define the local principal curvatures of the potential energy surface. It then uses spectral cross-attention to compare the unbound ligand and protein with the bound complex, thereby capturing binding-induced changes in conformational dynamics. In parallel, hierarchical protein-ligand interaction representations are learned from static structural features through geometry-aware message passing, soft clustering, and bidirectional cross-attention. Finally, CPES fuses the curvature-informed dynamic representations with static interaction representations for affinity regression. Extensive evaluations on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPES achieves improved predictive performance and offers physical interpretability.

</details>


### [119] [Learning the Context of Errors: Black-Box Online Adaptation of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14222)
*Xilin Dai,Yiding Liu,Hongjie Xia,Yifan Hu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时序基础模型在黑盒API服务场景下的在线适应问题，提出ORCA方法，通过学习基于输入输出上下文的预测误差来实现有效的黑盒在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着时序基础模型（TSFMs）的快速发展，未来可能以商业化、闭源API服务的形式提供。然而现有的在线适应方法仍依赖于白盒访问进行参数微调或梯度反向传播，这种范式不匹配促使作者思考：在TSFMs的黑盒在线适应中应该学习什么？

Method: 提出ORCA（Online Residual Contextual Adaptation）方法。核心洞察是：基础模型的预测误差同时以基础模型的输入和输出为条件（即误差上下文）。基于此，ORCA通过学习这种误差上下文来进行黑盒在线适应，而不需要访问模型参数或梯度信息。

Result: 在5个最先进的TSFMs和8个数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了ORCA方法的有效性。通过消融研究定量分析了不同适配器学习假设对黑盒在线适应最终性能的影响。

Conclusion: ORCA成功解决了TSFMs黑盒在线适应中的关键问题，证明了学习基于输入输出上下文的预测误差是一种有效的黑盒适应策略，为未来商业化TSFM服务的在线适应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) has advanced zero-shot forecasting across diverse domains. Inspired by the current form of Large Language Models, future TSFMs may be offered as commercialized, closed-source API services. However, many existing online adaptation methods still rely on white-box access for parameter fine-tuning or gradient backpropagation. This paradigm mismatch raises a question: In black-box online adaptation for TSFMs, what should we learn? We answer this with an insight: the predictive errors of the base model are conditioned on both the input and output of the base model (i.e., the context of errors). To validate this insight, we propose ORCA (Online Residual Contextual Adaptation). We conduct extensive experiments across 5 state-of-the-art TSFMs and 8 datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, through ablation studies, we quantitatively analyze the impact of different adapter learning hypotheses on the final adaptation performance in black-box online adaptation. Code available at https://github.com/Fifthky/ORCA.

</details>


### [120] [Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14235)
*Jian Xu,Shigui Li,Wei Chen,Jiacheng Li,Zhiqi Lin,Delu Zeng,Xinghao Ding,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐式变分拒绝采样方法（IVRS），通过结合隐式分布与拒绝采样来改进后验近似，并引入IR-ELBO作为新的变分下界，实验表明该方法优于传统变分推断技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分推断方法通常依赖平均场分解，难以充分捕捉真实后验分布的复杂性；而近期基于神经网络建模隐式分布的方法虽然提高了灵活性，但受限于网络架构的实际约束，仍然存在近似精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出隐式变分拒绝采样（IVRS）方法：使用神经网络构建隐式提议分布，结合拒绝采样技术；引入判别器网络估计隐式提议分布与真实后验分布之间的密度比率，用于精炼近似；推导出隐式重采样证据下界（IR-ELBO）来刻画重采样分布质量并获得更紧的变分下界。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在后验近似质量上优于传统变分推断技术，验证了IVRS方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合隐式分布与拒绝采样，IVRS方法成功克服了平均场假设和神经网络架构限制带来的近似不足问题，为贝叶斯机器学习中的后验推断提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Variational Inference (VI) is a fundamental inference technique in Bayesian machine learning for approximating complex posterior distributions. Traditional VI often relies on the mean-field factorization, which can inadequately capture true posterior complexity. Recent advancements have leveraged neural networks to model implicit distributions, offering increased flexibility. However, the practical constraints of neural network architectures still produces inaccuracies. In this paper, we propose a method called Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling (IVRS), which integrates implicit distributions with rejection sampling to improve the posterior approximation. Our method uses neural networks to construct implicit proposal distributions, and rejection sampling with a discriminator network that estimates the density ratio between the implicit proposal and the true posterior for refining the approximation. Towards this end, we introduce the Implicit Resampling Evidence Lower Bound (IR-ELBO) as a metric to characterize the resampled distribution's quality and derive a tighter variational lower bound. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms traditional variational inference techniques.

</details>
